###################################################
### ARCHIVO: .github/workflows/build-apk.yml
###################################################

name: Compilar APK Android (Capacitor)

on:
  push:
    branches: [ "**" ]
  pull_request:
    branches: [ "**" ]
  workflow_dispatch:

jobs:
  build-apk:
    name: Generar APK de Android
    runs-on: ubuntu-24.04

    steps:
      - name: Descargar codigo fuente
        uses: actions/checkout@v4

      - name: Configurar Node.js (v22 LTS)
        uses: actions/setup-node@v4
        with:
          node-version: 22

      - name: Configurar Java JDK 21
        uses: actions/setup-java@v5
        with:
          distribution: 'temurin'
          java-version: '21'

      - name: Configurar Gradle
        uses: gradle/actions/setup-gradle@v4

      - name: Aceptar licencias de Android SDK
        run: |
          yes | sdkmanager --licenses || true

      - name: Instalar dependencias
        run: npm install --legacy-peer-deps

      - name: Compilar aplicacion web
        run: npm run build

      - name: Sincronizar proyecto Android
        run: |
          npx cap sync android

      - name: Configurar firma fija para evitar conflicto de paquete
        run: |
          # Si no existe debug.keystore, crearlo con credenciales fijas
          if [ ! -f android/app/debug.keystore ]; then
            keytool -genkey -v -keystore android/app/debug.keystore -storepass android -alias androiddebugkey -keypass android -keyalg RSA -keysize 2048 -validity 10000 -dname "CN=Boveda,O=Boveda,C=ES"
          fi
          # Incrementar versionCode con el run_number de GitHub para que Android siempre reconozca la actualización
          BUILD_NUM=${{ github.run_number }}
          sed -i "s/versionCode [0-9]*/versionCode $BUILD_NUM/g" android/app/build.gradle

      - name: Compilar APK con Gradle
        run: |
          cd android
          chmod +x gradlew
          ./gradlew assembleDebug --no-daemon --stacktrace

      - name: Subir APK listo para descargar
        uses: actions/upload-artifact@v4
        with:
          name: Boveda-Segura-APK
          path: android/app/build/outputs/apk/debug/app-debug.apk
          if-no-files-found: error
          retention-days: 30

### ARCHIVO: android/app/src/main/java/com/calculadora/boveda/privada/MainActivity.java
###################################################

package com.calculadora.boveda.privada;

import android.Manifest;
import android.content.pm.PackageManager;
import android.os.Build;
import android.os.Bundle;
import android.view.WindowManager;
import android.webkit.PermissionRequest;
import androidx.core.app.ActivityCompat;
import androidx.core.content.ContextCompat;
import com.getcapacitor.BridgeActivity;
import com.getcapacitor.BridgeWebChromeClient;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;

public class MainActivity extends BridgeActivity {
    private static final int PERMISSION_REQ_CODE = 1001;

    @Override
    public void onCreate(Bundle savedInstanceState) {
        registerPlugin(VaultBiometricsPlugin.class);
        registerPlugin(VaultSecurityPlugin.class);
        registerPlugin(VaultMediaPlugin.class);
        super.onCreate(savedInstanceState);

        // Bloqueo total a nivel de sistema operativo para capturas y grabaciones de pantalla
        getWindow().setFlags(
            WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_SECURE,
            WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_SECURE
        );

        // Solicitar permisos necesarios desde el inicio (Cámara + Acceso Multimedia para MediaStore)
        checkAndRequestAppPermissions();

        // Concesión automática de permisos de cámara al WebView interno
        if (getBridge() != null && getBridge().getWebView() != null) {
            getBridge().getWebView().setWebChromeClient(new BridgeWebChromeClient(getBridge()) {
                @Override
                public void onPermissionRequest(final PermissionRequest request) {
                    request.grant(request.getResources());
                }
            });
        }
    }

    private void checkAndRequestAppPermissions() {
        List<String> neededPermissions = new ArrayList<>();

        if (ContextCompat.checkSelfPermission(this, Manifest.permission.CAMERA) != PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED) {
            neededPermissions.add(Manifest.permission.CAMERA);
        }

        if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.TIRAMISU) {
            // Android 13+ (API 33+)
            if (ContextCompat.checkSelfPermission(this, Manifest.permission.READ_MEDIA_IMAGES) != PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED) {
                neededPermissions.add(Manifest.permission.READ_MEDIA_IMAGES);
            }
            if (ContextCompat.checkSelfPermission(this, Manifest.permission.READ_MEDIA_VIDEO) != PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED) {
                neededPermissions.add(Manifest.permission.READ_MEDIA_VIDEO);
            }
            if (ContextCompat.checkSelfPermission(this, Manifest.permission.READ_MEDIA_AUDIO) != PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED) {
                neededPermissions.add(Manifest.permission.READ_MEDIA_AUDIO);
            }
        } else {
            // Android 12 y anteriores
            if (ContextCompat.checkSelfPermission(this, Manifest.permission.READ_EXTERNAL_STORAGE) != PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED) {
                neededPermissions.add(Manifest.permission.READ_EXTERNAL_STORAGE);
            }
            if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT <= Build.VERSION_CODES.Q) {
                if (ContextCompat.checkSelfPermission(this, Manifest.permission.WRITE_EXTERNAL_STORAGE) != PackageManager.PERMISSION_GRANTED) {
                    neededPermissions.add(Manifest.permission.WRITE_EXTERNAL_STORAGE);
                }
            }
        }

        if (!neededPermissions.isEmpty()) {
            ActivityCompat.requestPermissions(this, neededPermissions.toArray(new String[0]), PERMISSION_REQ_CODE);
        }
    }
}


###################################################
### ARCHIVO: android/app/src/main/java/com/calculadora/boveda/privada/VaultMediaPlugin.java
###################################################

package com.calculadora.boveda.privada;

import android.app.Activity;
import android.app.PendingIntent;
import android.content.ContentResolver;
import android.content.ContentUris;
import android.content.Intent;
import android.content.IntentSender;
import android.database.Cursor;
import android.net.Uri;
import android.os.Build;
import android.provider.MediaStore;
import androidx.activity.result.ActivityResult;
import androidx.activity.result.IntentSenderRequest;
import com.getcapacitor.JSArray;
import com.getcapacitor.JSObject;
import com.getcapacitor.Plugin;
import com.getcapacitor.PluginCall;
import com.getcapacitor.PluginMethod;
import com.getcapacitor.annotation.ActivityCallback;
import com.getcapacitor.annotation.CapacitorPlugin;
import java.util.ArrayList;
import java.util.List;
import org.json.JSONObject;

@CapacitorPlugin(name = "VaultMedia")
public class VaultMediaPlugin extends Plugin {

    @PluginMethod
    public void openGalleryApp(PluginCall call) {
        try {
            Intent intent = new Intent(Intent.ACTION_VIEW);
            intent.setDataAndType(MediaStore.Images.Media.EXTERNAL_CONTENT_URI, "image/*");
            intent.setFlags(Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK);
            getContext().startActivity(intent);

            JSObject ret = new JSObject();
            ret.put("success", true);
            call.resolve(ret);
        } catch (Exception e) {
            try {
                Intent fallback = Intent.makeMainSelectorActivity(Intent.ACTION_MAIN, Intent.CATEGORY_APP_GALLERY);
                fallback.setFlags(Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK);
                getContext().startActivity(fallback);
                JSObject ret = new JSObject();
                ret.put("success", true);
                call.resolve(ret);
            } catch (Exception ex) {
                JSObject ret = new JSObject();
                ret.put("success", false);
                ret.put("error", ex.getMessage());
                call.resolve(ret);
            }
        }
    }

    /**
     * Borrado por lotes de archivos multimedia en Android MediaStore.
     * Soporta recibir items con { filename, size, lastModified, mimeType, uri }.
     */
    @PluginMethod
    public void deleteBatchFromGallery(PluginCall call) {
        JSArray itemsArray = call.getArray("items");
        if (itemsArray == null || itemsArray.length() == 0) {
            String filename = call.getString("filename");
            if (filename != null && !filename.isEmpty()) {
                itemsArray = new JSArray();
                JSObject single = new JSObject();
                single.put("filename", filename);
                if (call.getData().has("size")) single.put("size", call.getInt("size"));
                if (call.getData().has("lastModified")) single.put("lastModified", call.getLong("lastModified"));
                if (call.getData().has("uri")) single.put("uri", call.getString("uri"));
                itemsArray.put(single);
            } else {
                call.reject("Se requiere array 'items' o parámetro 'filename'");
                return;
            }
        }

        try {
            ContentResolver resolver = getContext().getContentResolver();
            List<Uri> urisToDelete = new ArrayList<>();

            Uri[] baseUris = new Uri[]{
                MediaStore.Images.Media.EXTERNAL_CONTENT_URI,
                MediaStore.Video.Media.EXTERNAL_CONTENT_URI,
                MediaStore.Audio.Media.EXTERNAL_CONTENT_URI,
                MediaStore.Files.getContentUri("external")
            };

            for (int i = 0; i < itemsArray.length(); i++) {
                JSONObject item = itemsArray.getJSONObject(i);
                String uriStr = item.optString("uri", null);
                String filename = item.optString("filename", item.optString("name", null));
                long size = item.optLong("size", -1);
                long lastModified = item.optLong("lastModified", -1);

                if (uriStr != null && !uriStr.isEmpty() && uriStr.startsWith("content://")) {
                    Uri parsed = Uri.parse(uriStr);
                    if (!urisToDelete.contains(parsed)) {
                        urisToDelete.add(parsed);
                    }
                    continue;
                }

                if (filename == null || filename.isEmpty()) continue;

                Uri foundUri = findMediaStoreUri(resolver, baseUris, filename, size, lastModified);
                if (foundUri != null && !urisToDelete.contains(foundUri)) {
                    urisToDelete.add(foundUri);
                }
            }

            if (urisToDelete.isEmpty()) {
                JSObject ret = new JSObject();
                ret.put("success", false);
                ret.put("matchedCount", 0);
                ret.put("requested", false);
                ret.put("message", "No se encontraron los archivos en MediaStore");
                call.resolve(ret);
                return;
            }

            // En Android 11+ (API 30+) MediaStore.createDeleteRequest pide permiso al usuario con el diálogo nativo del sistema
            if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.R) {
                PendingIntent pi = null;
                try {
                    pi = MediaStore.createDeleteRequest(resolver, urisToDelete);
                } catch (Exception exBatch) {
                    List<Uri> imageOnly = new ArrayList<>();
                    for (Uri u : urisToDelete) {
                        if (u.toString().contains("images")) imageOnly.add(u);
                    }
                    if (!imageOnly.isEmpty()) {
                        try {
                            pi = MediaStore.createDeleteRequest(resolver, imageOnly);
                        } catch (Exception ignored) {}
                    }
                }

                if (pi != null) {
                    IntentSender intentSender = pi.getIntentSender();
                    try {
                        getActivity().startIntentSenderForResult(intentSender, 2002, null, 0, 0, 0, null);
                        JSObject ret = new JSObject();
                        ret.put("success", true);
                        ret.put("requested", true);
                        ret.put("urisCount", urisToDelete.size());
                        call.resolve(ret);
                        return;
                    } catch (Exception exSender) {
                        call.reject("No se pudo iniciar el diálogo de eliminación: " + exSender.getMessage());
                        return;
                    }
                } else {
                    JSObject ret = new JSObject();
                    ret.put("success", false);
                    ret.put("requested", false);
                    call.resolve(ret);
                    return;
                }
            } else {
                int deletedCount = 0;
                for (Uri u : urisToDelete) {
                    try {
                        deletedCount += resolver.delete(u, null, null);
                    } catch (Exception ex) {
                        // continuar
                    }
                }
                JSObject ret = new JSObject();
                ret.put("success", deletedCount > 0);
                ret.put("requested", true);
                ret.put("deletedCount", deletedCount);
                call.resolve(ret);
            }
        } catch (Exception e) {
            JSObject ret = new JSObject();
            ret.put("success", false);
            ret.put("requested", false);
            ret.put("error", e.getMessage());
            call.resolve(ret);
        }
    }

    @PluginMethod
    public void deleteFromGallery(PluginCall call) {
        deleteBatchFromGallery(call);
    }

    /**
     * Búsqueda flexible y robusta en MediaStore:
     * 1) Por Nombre y Tamaño exactos
     * 2) Por Nombre exacto (insensible a mayúsculas/minúsculas)
     * 3) Por ID numérico extraído de Photo Picker / DocumentsUI
     * 4) Por prefijo del nombre
     * 5) Por Tamaño exacto (ordenado por más reciente)
     */
    private Uri findMediaStoreUri(ContentResolver resolver, Uri[] baseUris, String filename, long size, long lastModified) {
        for (Uri baseUri : baseUris) {
            String[] projection = new String[]{
                MediaStore.MediaColumns._ID,
                MediaStore.MediaColumns.DISPLAY_NAME,
                MediaStore.MediaColumns.SIZE,
                MediaStore.MediaColumns.DATE_MODIFIED
            };

            // 1. Intento por Nombre y Tamaño exactos
            if (size > 0 && filename != null && !filename.isEmpty()) {
                String selection = MediaStore.MediaColumns.DISPLAY_NAME + " = ? AND " + MediaStore.MediaColumns.SIZE + " = ?";
                String[] selectionArgs = new String[]{filename, String.valueOf(size)};
                try (Cursor cursor = resolver.query(baseUri, projection, selection, selectionArgs, MediaStore.MediaColumns._ID + " DESC")) {
                    if (cursor != null && cursor.moveToFirst()) {
                        long id = cursor.getLong(cursor.getColumnIndexOrThrow(MediaStore.MediaColumns._ID));
                        return ContentUris.withAppendedId(baseUri, id);
                    }
                } catch (Exception ignored) {}
            }

            // 2. Intento por Nombre exacto (sin importar mayúsculas/minúsculas)
            if (filename != null && !filename.isEmpty()) {
                String selectionName = MediaStore.MediaColumns.DISPLAY_NAME + " = ? OR LOWER(" + MediaStore.MediaColumns.DISPLAY_NAME + ") = LOWER(?)";
                String[] selectionArgsName = new String[]{filename, filename};
                try (Cursor cursor = resolver.query(baseUri, projection, selectionName, selectionArgsName, MediaStore.MediaColumns._ID + " DESC")) {
                    if (cursor != null && cursor.moveToFirst()) {
                        long id = cursor.getLong(cursor.getColumnIndexOrThrow(MediaStore.MediaColumns._ID));
                        return ContentUris.withAppendedId(baseUri, id);
                    }
                } catch (Exception ignored) {}
            }

            // 3. Intento por ID numérico si el selector devolvió "image:12345" o similar
            if (filename != null && filename.matches(".*\\d{3,}.*")) {
                try {
                    java.util.regex.Matcher matcher = java.util.regex.Pattern.compile("\\d{3,}").matcher(filename);
                    if (matcher.find()) {
                        long numericId = Long.parseLong(matcher.group());
                        Uri candidate = ContentUris.withAppendedId(baseUri, numericId);
                        try (Cursor cursor = resolver.query(candidate, new String[]{MediaStore.MediaColumns._ID}, null, null, null)) {
                            if (cursor != null && cursor.moveToFirst()) {
                                return candidate;
                            }
                        } catch (Exception ignored) {}
                    }
                } catch (Exception ignored) {}
            }

            // 4. Intento sin extensión si fue cambiado (ej: "IMG_20261009_072505")
            if (filename != null && filename.contains(".")) {
                String baseName = filename.substring(0, filename.lastIndexOf('.'));
                if (baseName.length() >= 4) {
                    String selectionLike = MediaStore.MediaColumns.DISPLAY_NAME + " LIKE ?";
                    String[] selectionArgsLike = new String[]{baseName + "%"};
                    try (Cursor cursor = resolver.query(baseUri, projection, selectionLike, selectionArgsLike, MediaStore.MediaColumns._ID + " DESC")) {
                        if (cursor != null && cursor.moveToFirst()) {
                            long id = cursor.getLong(cursor.getColumnIndexOrThrow(MediaStore.MediaColumns._ID));
                            return ContentUris.withAppendedId(baseUri, id);
                        }
                    } catch (Exception ignored) {}
                }
            }

            // 5. Intento por tamaño exacto (si el tamaño es > 10KB), tomando el archivo más reciente
            if (size > 10240) {
                String selectionSize = MediaStore.MediaColumns.SIZE + " = ?";
                String[] selectionArgsSize = new String[]{String.valueOf(size)};
                try (Cursor cursor = resolver.query(baseUri, projection, selectionSize, selectionArgsSize, MediaStore.MediaColumns._ID + " DESC")) {
                    if (cursor != null && cursor.moveToFirst()) {
                        long id = cursor.getLong(cursor.getColumnIndexOrThrow(MediaStore.MediaColumns._ID));
                        return ContentUris.withAppendedId(baseUri, id);
                    }
                } catch (Exception ignored) {}
            }
        }
        return null;
    }
}


###################################################
### ARCHIVO: src/services/nativeVaultBridge.ts
###################################################

import { Capacitor, registerPlugin } from '@capacitor/core';

export interface NativeMediaItemIdentifier {
  filename: string;
  size?: number;
  lastModified?: number;
  mimeType?: string;
  uri?: string;
}

export interface NativeVaultMediaPlugin {
  /**
   * Elimina un archivo multimedia de la galería pública usando MediaStore en Android
   */
  deleteFromGallery(options: {
    uri?: string;
    filename?: string;
    size?: number;
    lastModified?: number;
  }): Promise<{ success: boolean; message?: string }>;

  /**
   * Elimina por lotes un conjunto de archivos multimedia de la galería pública usando un único
   * diálogo del sistema en Android 11+ (MediaStore.createDeleteRequest).
   */
  deleteBatchFromGallery(options: {
    items: NativeMediaItemIdentifier[];
  }): Promise<{
    success: boolean;
    requested?: boolean;
    confirmedByUser?: boolean;
    matchedCount?: number;
    deletedCount?: number;
    message?: string;
    error?: string;
  }>;

  /**
   * Abre la aplicación nativa de Galería del dispositivo
   */
  openGalleryApp(): Promise<{ success: boolean; error?: string }>;
}

// Registro del plugin nativo de Capacitor (se enlaza en Android con VaultMediaPlugin.java)
export const NativeVaultMedia = registerPlugin<NativeVaultMediaPlugin>('VaultMedia');

/**
 * Retorna true si la app se está ejecutando como un APK nativo en Android o iOS
 */
export const isNativeApp = (): boolean => {
  return Capacitor.isNativePlatform();
};

/**
 * Abre la app nativa de galería de fotos de Android o iOS de manera directa
 */
export const openNativeGallery = async (): Promise<boolean> => {
  if (isNativeApp()) {
    try {
      const res = await NativeVaultMedia.openGalleryApp();
      if (res && res.success) return true;
    } catch (e) {
      console.warn('Could not launch gallery via native plugin:', e);
    }
  }

  try {
    const isAndroid = /Android/i.test(navigator.userAgent);
    if (isAndroid) {
      // Intent standard de Android para ver la Galería multimedia
      window.location.href = 'intent:#Intent;action=android.intent.action.VIEW;type=image/*;end';
    } else {
      window.open('photos-redirect://', '_blank');
    }
  } catch {}
  return true;
};

/**
 * Abre el explorador de archivos / gestor de descargas en Android
 */
export const openNativeFileManager = async (): Promise<boolean> => {
  try {
    const isAndroid = /Android/i.test(navigator.userAgent);
    if (isAndroid) {
      // Abre el gestor de archivos / descargas de Android
      window.location.href = 'intent:#Intent;action=android.intent.action.GET_CONTENT;type=*/*;category=android.intent.category.OPENABLE;end';
    } else {
      window.open('shareddocuments://', '_blank');
    }
  } catch {}
  return true;
};

/**
 * Función unificada para borrar un archivo individual de la galería pública.
 */
export const requestNativeGalleryDeletion = async (
  filename: string,
  uri?: string,
  size?: number,
  lastModified?: number
): Promise<{ handledNatively: boolean; success: boolean }> => {
  if (!isNativeApp()) {
    return { handledNatively: false, success: false };
  }

  try {
    const result = await NativeVaultMedia.deleteFromGallery({ uri, filename, size, lastModified });
    return { handledNatively: true, success: result.success };
  } catch (error) {
    console.warn('Native deletion not handled or rejected by user:', error);
    return { handledNatively: true, success: false };
  }
};

/**
 * Función por lotes para borrar múltiples archivos originales con un solo diálogo nativo.
 */
export const requestNativeBatchGalleryDeletion = async (
  items: NativeMediaItemIdentifier[]
): Promise<{ handledNatively: boolean; success: boolean; requested?: boolean }> => {
  if (!isNativeApp() || !items || items.length === 0) {
    return { handledNatively: false, success: false };
  }

  try {
    const result = await NativeVaultMedia.deleteBatchFromGallery({ items });
    return {
      handledNatively: true,
      success: result.success,
      requested: result.requested,
    };
  } catch (error) {
    console.warn('Native batch deletion error:', error);
    return { handledNatively: true, success: false };
  }
};


###################################################
### ARCHIVO: src/components/ImportVaultView.tsx
###################################################

import React, { useState, useRef, useEffect } from 'react';
import {
  Image as ImageIcon,
  Video,
  Music,
  LayoutGrid,
  Lock,
  Check,
  Plus,
  Loader2,
  FolderPlus,
} from 'lucide-react';
import { encryptBuffer } from '../crypto/vaultCrypto';
import { saveEncryptedItems, getAllEncryptedItems, VaultItemCategory, EncryptedVaultItem } from '../db/vaultDb';
import { requestNativeBatchGalleryDeletion, isNativeApp } from '../services/nativeVaultBridge';
import { registerBackHandler } from '../services/backButtonService';

interface ImportVaultViewProps {
  masterKey: CryptoKey;
  onImportComplete: (importedItems?: { name: string; size: number; category: VaultItemCategory }[]) => void;
  showToast: (msg: string, type?: 'info' | 'success' | 'alert' | 'error') => void;
}

interface MediaCandidate {
  id: string;
  name: string;
  category: VaultItemCategory;
  url: string;
  size: number;
  mimeType: string;
  isCustomFile?: boolean;
  file?: File;
  lastModified?: number;
}

export const ImportVaultView: React.FC<ImportVaultViewProps> = ({
  masterKey,
  onImportComplete,
  showToast,
}) => {
  const [activeCategory, setActiveCategory] = useState<'photo' | 'video' | 'audio' | 'app'>('photo');
  const [candidates, setCandidates] = useState<MediaCandidate[]>([]);
  const [selectedIds, setSelectedIds] = useState<Set<string>>(new Set());
  const [isEncrypting, setIsEncrypting] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [encryptProgress, setEncryptProgress] = useState<number>(0);
  const [encryptingName, setEncryptingName] = useState<string>('');
  const mediaInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement | null>(null);
  const audioInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement | null>(null);
  const docInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement | null>(null);

  const toggleSelect = (id: string) => {
    setSelectedIds((prev) => {
      const next = new Set(prev);
      if (next.has(id)) {
        next.delete(id);
      } else {
        next.add(id);
      }
      return next;
    });
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    if (selectedIds.size > 0) {
      return registerBackHandler('import_selection', () => {
        setSelectedIds(new Set());
        return true;
      }, 70);
    }
  }, [selectedIds.size]);

  const handleFilesAdded = async (files: FileList | null) => {
    if (!files || files.length === 0) return;

    let existingInVault: { name: string; size: number }[] = [];
    try {
      const allVault = await getAllEncryptedItems();
      existingInVault = allVault.map((item) => ({ name: item.name, size: item.size }));
    } catch {}

    const newCandidates: MediaCandidate[] = [];
    const newSelected = new Set(selectedIds);
    let duplicatesCount = 0;

    Array.from(files).forEach((file, index) => {
      // Verificar si ya existe en la bóveda o en los candidatos actuales
      const alreadyInVault = existingInVault.some(
        (v) => v.name === file.name && v.size === file.size
      );
      const alreadyInCandidates = candidates.some(
        (c) => c.name === file.name && c.size === file.size
      );

      if (alreadyInVault || alreadyInCandidates) {
        duplicatesCount++;
        return;
      }

      const id = `cand_${Date.now()}_${index}_${Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 6)}`;
      const url = URL.createObjectURL(file);
      let cat: VaultItemCategory = 'photo';
      if (file.type.startsWith('video/')) cat = 'video';
      else if (file.type.startsWith('audio/')) cat = 'audio';
      else if (
        file.type.includes('pdf') ||
        file.type.includes('apk') ||
        file.type.includes('zip') ||
        file.type.includes('document') ||
        file.name.endsWith('.apk') ||
        file.name.endsWith('.zip')
      ) {
        cat = 'app';
      }

      newCandidates.push({
        id,
        name: file.name,
        category: cat,
        url,
        size: file.size,
        mimeType: file.type || 'application/octet-stream',
        isCustomFile: true,
        file,
        lastModified: file.lastModified || undefined,
      });

      newSelected.add(id);
    });

    if (duplicatesCount > 0) {
      showToast(`${duplicatesCount} archivo(s) ya existen en la bóveda y fueron omitidos`, 'alert');
    }

    if (newCandidates.length > 0) {
      setCandidates((prev) => [...prev, ...newCandidates]);
      setSelectedIds(newSelected);
      showToast(`${newCandidates.length} archivo(s) nuevos añadidos`, 'info');
    }
  };

  const handleCustomFileSelect = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    handleFilesAdded(e.target.files);
    e.target.value = '';
  };

  const handleSelectAll = () => {
    if (selectedIds.size === filteredCandidates.length) {
      setSelectedIds(new Set());
    } else {
      setSelectedIds(new Set(filteredCandidates.map((c) => c.id)));
    }
  };

  const handleEncryptAndImport = async () => {
    if (selectedIds.size === 0) {
      mediaInputRef.current?.click();
      return;
    }

    setIsEncrypting(true);
    setEncryptProgress(5);

    const itemsToProcess = candidates.filter((c) => selectedIds.has(c.id));
    const total = itemsToProcess.length;
    let completed = 0;
    const newRecords: EncryptedVaultItem[] = [];

    try {
      for (const item of itemsToProcess) {
        setEncryptingName(item.name);
        let buffer: ArrayBuffer;
        if (item.file) {
          buffer = await item.file.arrayBuffer();
        } else {
          try {
            const resp = await fetch(item.url);
            buffer = await resp.arrayBuffer();
          } catch {
            const encoder = new TextEncoder();
            buffer = encoder.encode(`Secure encrypted asset data for ${item.name}`).buffer;
          }
        }

        const encrypted = await encryptBuffer(buffer, masterKey);
        const recordId = `item_${Date.now()}_${Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 8)}`;

        newRecords.push({
          id: recordId,
          timestamp: Date.now() + completed,
          name: item.name,
          mimeType: item.mimeType,
          size: item.size,
          iv: encrypted.iv,
          data: encrypted.data,
          album: 'General',
          isFavorite: false,
          category: item.category,
          lastModified: item.lastModified,
        });

        completed++;
        setEncryptProgress(Math.round((completed / total) * 100));
      }

      // Guardar todos los registros en IndexedDB de forma atómica y segura
      if (newRecords.length > 0) {
        await saveEncryptedItems(newRecords);
      }

      // Prompt gallery deletion for photos/videos natively via unified batch call
      const mediaItemsToDelete = itemsToProcess
        .filter((it) => it.category === 'photo' || it.category === 'video' || it.category === 'audio')
        .map((it) => ({
          filename: it.name,
          size: it.size,
          lastModified: it.lastModified,
          mimeType: it.mimeType,
        }));

      if (isNativeApp() && mediaItemsToDelete.length > 0) {
        await requestNativeBatchGalleryDeletion(mediaItemsToDelete);
      }

      showToast(`¡${total} archivo(s) cifrados con éxito en tu Bóveda!`, 'success');
      setSelectedIds(new Set());
      setCandidates((prev) => prev.filter((c) => !itemsToProcess.some((p) => p.id === c.id)));

      setIsEncrypting(false);
      onImportComplete(itemsToProcess.map((it) => ({ name: it.name, size: it.size, category: it.category })));
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Error importing items:', err);
      showToast('Error al cifrar archivos', 'error');
      setIsEncrypting(false);
    }
  };

  const selectedCount = selectedIds.size;
  const filteredCandidates = candidates.filter((c) => {
    if (activeCategory === 'photo') return c.category === 'photo';
    if (activeCategory === 'video') return c.category === 'video';
    if (activeCategory === 'audio') return c.category === 'audio';
    return c.category === 'app';
  });

  return (
    <div className="w-full max-w-md mx-auto px-4 pb-28 animate-in fade-in duration-200 box-border">
      {/* Fixed Pinned Header */}
      <header className="fixed top-0 left-0 right-0 z-30 bg-[#090d16]/95 backdrop-blur-xl border-b border-slate-800/80 safe-top-sticky pb-3 px-4 shadow-md shadow-black/40 transition-all">
        <div className="max-w-md mx-auto w-full">
          <h1 className="text-2xl sm:text-3xl font-extrabold tracking-tight text-white mb-0.5">
            Impor<span className="text-[#00e5ff]">tar</span>
          </h1>
          <p className="text-[10px] font-semibold text-slate-400 tracking-[0.2em] uppercase">
            SELECCIONA ARCHIVOS PARA CIFRAR
          </p>
        </div>
      </header>

      {/* Spacer so content starts right below fixed header */}
      <div className="safe-top-header-spacer shrink-0 w-full mb-3" />

      {/* Sub-header and selection actions */}
      <div className="flex items-center justify-between mb-3 px-1">
        <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-400">
          {candidates.length > 0
            ? `${selectedCount} de ${candidates.length} seleccionados`
            : 'Elige archivos de tu dispositivo'}
        </span>
        {candidates.length > 0 && (
          <button
            type="button"
            onClick={handleSelectAll}
            className="text-xs font-bold text-[#00e5ff] hover:underline cursor-pointer"
          >
            {selectedIds.size === filteredCandidates.length ? 'Deseleccionar' : 'Todos'}
          </button>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* 3x3 Media Selection Grid or Empty Dropzone */}
      {candidates.length > 0 ? (
        <div className="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2 sm:gap-2.5 mb-4">
          {filteredCandidates.map((item) => {
            const isSelected = selectedIds.has(item.id);

            return (
              <div
                key={item.id}
                onClick={() => toggleSelect(item.id)}
                className="group relative aspect-square rounded-2xl overflow-hidden bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800/80 hover:border-[#00e5ff]/50 cursor-pointer select-none transition-all active:scale-95 shadow-md"
              >
                {item.category === 'photo' || item.category === 'video' ? (
                  <img
                    src={item.url}
                    alt={item.name}
                    className="w-full h-full object-contain p-0.5 group-hover:scale-105 transition-transform duration-300"
                    loading="lazy"
                  />
                ) : (
                  <div className="w-full h-full flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-2 bg-gradient-to-br from-[#101728] to-slate-950 text-slate-400">
                    {item.category === 'audio' ? (
                      <Music className="w-8 h-8 text-amber-400 mb-1" />
                    ) : (
                      <LayoutGrid className="w-8 h-8 text-[#00e5ff] mb-1" />
                    )}
                    <span className="text-[10px] text-center font-medium line-clamp-2 px-1 text-slate-300">
                      {item.name}
                    </span>
                  </div>
                )}

                {/* Circular Checkbox (Top Right) */}
                <div className="absolute top-2 right-2 z-10">
                  {isSelected ? (
                    <div className="w-6 h-6 rounded-full bg-[#00e5ff] text-black flex items-center justify-center shadow-lg shadow-cyan-500/40">
                      <Check className="w-3.5 h-3.5 stroke-[3.5]" />
                    </div>
                  ) : (
                    <div className="w-6 h-6 rounded-full border-2 border-white/80 bg-black/40 backdrop-blur-sm" />
                  )}
                </div>

                {/* Bottom shade with name */}
                <div className="absolute inset-x-0 bottom-0 bg-gradient-to-t from-black/90 via-black/40 to-transparent p-1.5 pt-3 text-[10px] text-white/90 truncate pointer-events-none">
                  {item.name}
                </div>
              </div>
            );
          })}

          {/* Plus tile to pick local files */}
          <div
            onClick={() => {
              if (activeCategory === 'audio') audioInputRef.current?.click();
              else if (activeCategory === 'app') docInputRef.current?.click();
              else mediaInputRef.current?.click();
            }}
            className="aspect-square rounded-2xl border-2 border-dashed border-slate-800 hover:border-[#00e5ff]/50 bg-[#0a121d]/60 hover:bg-[#0a121d] flex flex-col items-center justify-center p-2 text-center cursor-pointer transition-all active:scale-95 group"
          >
            <div className="w-8 h-8 rounded-full bg-[#00e5ff]/10 text-[#00e5ff] flex items-center justify-center mb-1 group-hover:scale-110 transition-transform">
              <Plus className="w-4 h-4" />
            </div>
            <span className="text-[10px] font-semibold text-slate-300 group-hover:text-[#00e5ff] transition-colors">
              + Añadir más
            </span>
          </div>
        </div>
      ) : (
        <div className="mb-5 bg-[#0a121d] border border-slate-800/90 rounded-3xl p-5 text-center shadow-xl">
          <div className="w-14 h-14 rounded-2xl bg-[#00e5ff]/10 border border-[#00e5ff]/30 text-[#00e5ff] flex items-center justify-center mx-auto mb-3 shadow-md shadow-cyan-500/10">
            <Plus className="w-7 h-7 stroke-[2.5]" />
          </div>
          <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-white mb-1">
            Selecciona archivos para tu Bóveda
          </h3>
          <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 mb-5 max-w-xs mx-auto">
            Elige fotos, videos, grabaciones o aplicaciones desde el almacenamiento de tu celular.
          </p>

          <div className="space-y-2.5">
            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => mediaInputRef.current?.click()}
              className="w-full py-3 px-4 rounded-2xl bg-slate-900/90 hover:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-800 hover:border-[#00e5ff]/40 text-left flex items-center justify-between transition-all active:scale-98 cursor-pointer group"
            >
              <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-xl bg-blue-500/15 text-blue-400 flex items-center justify-center">
                  <ImageIcon className="w-5 h-5" />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <h4 className="text-xs font-bold text-white group-hover:text-blue-300">
                    Fotos y Videos
                  </h4>
                  <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-500">Galería, cámara y descargas</p>
                </div>
              </div>
              <Plus className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-500 group-hover:text-[#00e5ff]" />
            </button>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => audioInputRef.current?.click()}
              className="w-full py-3 px-4 rounded-2xl bg-slate-900/90 hover:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-800 hover:border-amber-500/40 text-left flex items-center justify-between transition-all active:scale-98 cursor-pointer group"
            >
              <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-xl bg-amber-500/15 text-amber-400 flex items-center justify-center">
                  <Music className="w-5 h-5" />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <h4 className="text-xs font-bold text-white group-hover:text-amber-300">
                    Audio y Grabaciones
                  </h4>
                  <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-500">Música, audios de voz y grabadora</p>
                </div>
              </div>
              <Plus className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-500 group-hover:text-amber-400" />
            </button>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => docInputRef.current?.click()}
              className="w-full py-3 px-4 rounded-2xl bg-slate-900/90 hover:bg-slate-800 border border-slate-800 hover:border-emerald-500/40 text-left flex items-center justify-between transition-all active:scale-98 cursor-pointer group"
            >
              <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-xl bg-emerald-500/15 text-emerald-400 flex items-center justify-center">
                  <LayoutGrid className="w-5 h-5" />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <h4 className="text-xs font-bold text-white group-hover:text-emerald-300">
                    Archivos y Aplicaciones (.apk)
                  </h4>
                  <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-500">Documentos privados, paquetes y zips</p>
                </div>
              </div>
              <Plus className="w-4 h-4 text-slate-500 group-hover:text-emerald-400" />
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Encryption Status Card */}
      <div className="bg-[#0a121d] border border-slate-800/90 rounded-2xl p-4 mb-4 shadow-xl">
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3.5 mb-3">
          <div className="w-11 h-11 rounded-xl bg-[#00e5ff]/15 border border-[#00e5ff]/30 text-[#00e5ff] flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 shadow-md shadow-cyan-500/10">
            <Lock className="w-5 h-5" />
          </div>
          <div className="flex-1 min-w-0">
            <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-white truncate">
              {isEncrypting
                ? `Cifrando: ${encryptingName || 'archivo'}`
                : selectedCount > 0
                ? `${selectedCount} archivo(s) listos para cifrar`
                : 'Cifrado local AES-256 en reposo'}
            </h3>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-400">
              {isEncrypting
                ? 'Generando IV único y cifrado criptográfico local'
                : 'Cifrado blindado en Bóveda • Conservas control para eliminar originales'}
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>

        {/* Cyan Progress Bar */}
        <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
          <div className="flex-1 h-2 bg-slate-800/90 rounded-full overflow-hidden">
            <div
              className="h-full bg-[#00e5ff] transition-all duration-300 rounded-full shadow-sm shadow-cyan-500/50"
              style={{ width: `${selectedCount > 0 ? encryptProgress : 0}%` }}
            />
          </div>
          <span className="text-xs font-mono font-bold text-slate-300 shrink-0">
            {selectedCount > 0 ? `${encryptProgress}%` : '0%'}
          </span>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Sleek Action Button */}
      <button
        type="button"
        onClick={handleEncryptAndImport}
        disabled={isEncrypting}
        className="w-full py-2.5 sm:py-3 px-5 rounded-2xl bg-gradient-to-r from-cyan-400 via-[#00e5ff] to-cyan-400 hover:brightness-105 active:scale-[0.98] disabled:opacity-40 text-slate-950 font-bold text-xs sm:text-sm flex items-center justify-center gap-2 shadow-md shadow-cyan-500/20 transition-all cursor-pointer"
      >
        {isEncrypting ? (
          <>
            <Loader2 className="w-4 h-4 animate-spin text-slate-950 shrink-0" />
            <span>Cifrando con AES-256...</span>
          </>
        ) : (
          <>
            <Lock className="w-4 h-4 stroke-[2.2] text-slate-950 shrink-0" />
            <span>
              {selectedCount > 0
                ? `Cifrar e importar (${selectedCount})`
                : 'Seleccionar archivos para importar'}
            </span>
          </>
        )}
      </button>

      {/* Hidden file inputs for media, audio and documents */}
      <input
        ref={mediaInputRef}
        type="file"
        accept="image/*,video/*"
        multiple
        className="hidden"
        onChange={handleCustomFileSelect}
      />
      <input
        ref={audioInputRef}
        type="file"
        accept="audio/*"
        multiple
        className="hidden"
        onChange={handleCustomFileSelect}
      />
      <input
        ref={docInputRef}
        type="file"
        accept=".apk,.zip,.pdf,.doc,.docx,.txt,.rar,application/*,*/*"
        multiple
        className="hidden"
        onChange={handleCustomFileSelect}
      />
    </div>
  );
};


###################################################
### ARCHIVO: src/components/MainVaultView.tsx
###################################################

import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef, useMemo } from 'react';
import {
  Shield,
  ShieldCheck,
  Lock,
  Settings,
  ImagePlus,
  Camera,
  Search,
  Heart,
  Trash2,
  Download,
  CheckSquare,
  Cpu,
  UploadCloud,
  LayoutGrid,
  Grid3X3,
  Film,
  Smartphone,
  Image as ImageIcon,
  FolderArchive,
  Layers,
  Play,
  Package,
  X,
  Folder,
  FolderPlus,
  ChevronRight,
  Plus,
  ArrowLeft,
  Edit2,
  Edit3,
  Music,
  FileText,
  Check,
  Clock,
  Home,
  Video,
  AlertTriangle,
  ShieldAlert,
  Maximize2,
} from 'lucide-react';
import {
  EncryptedVaultItem,
  getAllEncryptedItems,
  saveEncryptedItem,
  saveEncryptedItems,
  deleteEncryptedItem,
  deleteMultipleEncryptedItems,
  updateItemMetadata,
  batchUpdateAlbum,
  batchRenameAlbum,
  detectCategory,
  VaultItemCategory,
} from '../db/vaultDb';
import { encryptBuffer, decryptBuffer } from '../crypto/vaultCrypto';
import { PhotoViewerModal, DecryptedVaultItem } from './PhotoViewerModal';
import { CameraCaptureModal } from './CameraCaptureModal';
import { DeleteGalleryReminderModal } from './DeleteGalleryReminderModal';
import { CreateFolderModal, MoveToFolderModal, RenameFolderModal } from './FolderModals';
import { isNativeApp, requestNativeBatchGalleryDeletion } from '../services/nativeVaultBridge';
import { ImportVaultView } from './ImportVaultView';
import { SecurityVaultView } from './SecurityVaultView';
import { SettingsVaultView } from './SettingsVaultView';
import { registerBackHandler, triggerBackAction } from '../services/backButtonService';

interface MainVaultViewProps {
  masterKey: CryptoKey;
  sessionPin?: string | null;
  onLock: () => void;
  onOpenSettings: () => void;
  showToast: (msg: string, type?: 'info' | 'success' | 'alert' | 'error') => void;
  refreshTrigger?: number;
  autoLockMinutes?: number;
  onAutoLockChange?: (mins: number) => void;
  lockOnTabSwitch?: boolean;
  onLockOnTabSwitchChange?: (enabled: boolean) => void;
  onMasterKeyUpdated?: (newKey: CryptoKey) => void;
  onResetVault?: () => void;
}

export const MainVaultView: React.FC<MainVaultViewProps> = ({
  masterKey,
  sessionPin,
  onLock,
  onOpenSettings,
  showToast,
  refreshTrigger = 0,
  autoLockMinutes = 5,
  onAutoLockChange,
  lockOnTabSwitch = true,
  onLockOnTabSwitchChange,
  onMasterKeyUpdated,
  onResetVault,
}) => {
  const [items, setItems] = useState<DecryptedVaultItem[]>([]);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState<boolean>(true);
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState<string>('');
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<'all' | 'photo' | 'video' | 'audio' | 'app' | 'favorites'>('all');
  const [activeNavTab, setActiveNavTab] = useState<'vault' | 'import' | 'security' | 'settings'>('vault');
  const [activeCategory, setActiveCategory] = useState<'photo' | 'video' | 'audio' | 'app' | null>(null);
  const [lastAccessTime] = useState<string>(() => {
    return localStorage.getItem('nullvault_last_access') || '21:14';
  });
  const [gridColumns, setGridColumns] = useState<2 | 3 | 4>(3);
  const [aspectMode, setAspectMode] = useState<'real' | 'square'>(() => {
    return (localStorage.getItem('vault_aspect_mode') as 'real' | 'square') || 'real';
  });

  const toggleAspectMode = () => {
    const next = aspectMode === 'real' ? 'square' : 'real';
    setAspectMode(next);
    localStorage.setItem('vault_aspect_mode', next);
    showToast(next === 'real' ? 'Visualización: Tamaño y proporción real (sin recorte)' : 'Visualización: Cuadrícula cuadrada 1:1', 'info');
  };

  // Recordatorio de copia de seguridad (> 7 días)
  const [backupReminder, setBackupReminder] = useState<{
    show: boolean;
    days: number;
    hasNeverBackedUp: boolean;
  }>(() => {
    const lastBackupTime = Number(
      localStorage.getItem('nullvault_last_backup_timestamp') ||
      localStorage.getItem('vault_last_backup_timestamp') ||
      '0'
    );
    const dismissedUntil = Number(
      localStorage.getItem('nullvault_backup_reminder_dismissed_until') ||
      localStorage.getItem('vault_backup_reminder_dismissed_until') ||
      '0'
    );
    if (Date.now() < dismissedUntil) {
      return { show: false, days: 0, hasNeverBackedUp: false };
    }
    const ONE_WEEK_MS = 7 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
    if (lastBackupTime > 0) {
      const diffMs = Date.now() - lastBackupTime;
      if (diffMs > ONE_WEEK_MS) {
        const days = Math.floor(diffMs / (24 * 60 * 60 * 1000));
        return { show: true, days, hasNeverBackedUp: false };
      }
    } else {
      return { show: true, days: 7, hasNeverBackedUp: true };
    }
    return { show: false, days: 0, hasNeverBackedUp: false };
  });

  useEffect(() => {
    const handleBackupUpdated = () => {
      setBackupReminder({ show: false, days: 0, hasNeverBackedUp: false });
    };
    window.addEventListener('vault_backup_updated', handleBackupUpdated);
    window.addEventListener('nullvault_backup_updated', handleBackupUpdated);
    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener('vault_backup_updated', handleBackupUpdated);
      window.removeEventListener('nullvault_backup_updated', handleBackupUpdated);
    };
  }, []);

  const dismissBackupReminder = () => {
    const nextDismiss = (Date.now() + 3 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000).toString();
    localStorage.setItem('vault_backup_reminder_dismissed_until', nextDismiss);
    localStorage.setItem('nullvault_backup_reminder_dismissed_until', nextDismiss);
    setBackupReminder({ show: false, days: 0, hasNeverBackedUp: false });
  };

  // Selection mode
  const [isSelectMode, setIsSelectMode] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [selectedIds, setSelectedIds] = useState<Set<string>>(new Set());

  // Subfolders / Custom independent folders per category
  const [customFolders, setCustomFolders] = useState<{
    photo: string[];
    video: string[];
    audio: string[];
    app: string[];
  }>(() => {
    const load = (cat: 'photo' | 'video' | 'audio' | 'app') => {
      try {
        const saved = localStorage.getItem(`vault_subfolders_${cat}`);
        if (saved) {
          const parsed = JSON.parse(saved);
          if (Array.isArray(parsed) && parsed.length > 0) return parsed;
        }
      } catch (e) {
        console.error(e);
      }
      return ['General'];
    };
    return {
      photo: load('photo'),
      video: load('video'),
      audio: load('audio'),
      app: load('app'),
    };
  });

  const [activeSubfolder, setActiveSubfolder] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [folderToRename, setFolderToRename] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [isCreateFolderOpen, setIsCreateFolderOpen] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [isMoveModalOpen, setIsMoveModalOpen] = useState<boolean>(false);

  // Modals
  const [activeViewerItem, setActiveViewerItem] = useState<DecryptedVaultItem | null>(null);
  const [isCameraOpen, setIsCameraOpen] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [isDraggingOver, setIsDraggingOver] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [isAddSheetOpen, setIsAddSheetOpen] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [importedCountForReminder, setImportedCountForReminder] = useState<number>(0);
  const [recentImportedItems, setRecentImportedItems] = useState<{ name: string; size: number }[]>([]);
  const [showBatchDeleteModal, setShowBatchDeleteModal] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [isDeletingBatch, setIsDeletingBatch] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const longPressTimeoutRef = useRef<NodeJS.Timeout | null>(null);
  const isLongPressTriggered = useRef<boolean>(false);
  const [encryptingProgress, setEncryptingProgress] = useState<{
    current: number;
    total: number;
    name: string;
  } | null>(null);

  // Hidden file inputs
  const mediaInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement | null>(null);
  const audioInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement | null>(null);
  const appInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement | null>(null);
  const blobUrlsRef = useRef<string[]>([]);

  // Load and decrypt items into memory
  const loadVaultItems = async () => {
    setIsLoading(true);
    try {
      const records = await getAllEncryptedItems();
      records.sort((a, b) => b.timestamp - a.timestamp);

      // Clean up previous blob URLs to prevent memory leaks
      blobUrlsRef.current.forEach((url) => URL.revokeObjectURL(url));
      blobUrlsRef.current = [];

      const decryptedItems: DecryptedVaultItem[] = [];

      for (let i = 0; i < records.length; i++) {
        const rec = records[i];
        try {
          const decryptedData = await decryptBuffer(rec.data, rec.iv, masterKey);
          const blob = new Blob([decryptedData], { type: rec.mimeType || 'application/octet-stream' });
          const blobUrl = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
          blobUrlsRef.current.push(blobUrl);

          decryptedItems.push({
            id: rec.id,
            blobUrl,
            record: rec,
          });
        } catch (decryptErr) {
          console.error(`Error decrypting item ${rec.id}:`, decryptErr);
        }

        if (i > 0 && i % 10 === 0) {
          await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 0));
        }
      }

      setItems(decryptedItems);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Error loading vault items:', err);
      showToast('Error al leer datos de la bóveda', 'error');
    } finally {
      setIsLoading(false);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    loadVaultItems();

    // Reload folders in case a backup brought new custom folders
    const loadFolder = (cat: 'photo' | 'video' | 'audio' | 'app') => {
      try {
        const saved = localStorage.getItem(`vault_subfolders_${cat}`);
        if (saved) {
          const parsed = JSON.parse(saved);
          if (Array.isArray(parsed) && parsed.length > 0) return parsed;
        }
      } catch (e) {
        console.error(e);
      }
      return ['General'];
    };
    setCustomFolders({
      photo: loadFolder('photo'),
      video: loadFolder('video'),
      audio: loadFolder('audio'),
      app: loadFolder('app'),
    });

    return () => {
      // Memory cleanup when component unmounts / locks
      blobUrlsRef.current.forEach((url) => URL.revokeObjectURL(url));
      blobUrlsRef.current = [];
    };
  }, [masterKey, refreshTrigger]);

  // Android Back Button / Gesture Navigation Support
  const activeViewerItemRef = useRef(activeViewerItem);
  activeViewerItemRef.current = activeViewerItem;
  const wasViewerOpenRef = useRef(false);

  const isCameraOpenRef = useRef(isCameraOpen);
  isCameraOpenRef.current = isCameraOpen;
  const wasCameraOpenRef = useRef(false);

  const showBatchDeleteModalRef = useRef(showBatchDeleteModal);
  showBatchDeleteModalRef.current = showBatchDeleteModal;
  const wasDeleteModalOpenRef = useRef(false);

  const importedCountRef = useRef(importedCountForReminder);
  importedCountRef.current = importedCountForReminder;
  const wasReminderOpenRef = useRef(false);

  const isSelectModeRef = useRef(isSelectMode);
  isSelectModeRef.current = isSelectMode;
  const selectedIdsRef = useRef(selectedIds);
  selectedIdsRef.current = selectedIds;
  const wasSelectingRef = useRef(false);

  const isCreateFolderOpenRef = useRef(isCreateFolderOpen);
  isCreateFolderOpenRef.current = isCreateFolderOpen;
  const wasCreateFolderOpenRef = useRef(false);

  const isMoveModalOpenRef = useRef(isMoveModalOpen);
  isMoveModalOpenRef.current = isMoveModalOpen;
  const wasMoveModalOpenRef = useRef(false);

  // Sync modal refs
  useEffect(() => {
    wasCreateFolderOpenRef.current = isCreateFolderOpen;
  }, [isCreateFolderOpen]);

  useEffect(() => {
    wasMoveModalOpenRef.current = isMoveModalOpen;
  }, [isMoveModalOpen]);

  // Reset activeSubfolder when changing activeTab
  useEffect(() => {
    setActiveSubfolder(null);
  }, [activeTab]);

  const activeSubfolderRef = useRef(activeSubfolder);
  activeSubfolderRef.current = activeSubfolder;
  const wasSubfolderOpenRef = useRef(false);

  const folderToRenameRef = useRef(folderToRename);
  folderToRenameRef.current = folderToRename;

  // Sync open subfolder ref
  useEffect(() => {
    wasSubfolderOpenRef.current = activeSubfolder !== null;
  }, [activeSubfolder]);

  // Dynamic list of all folders for active category
  const activeCategoryFolders = useMemo(() => {
    const targetCat =
      activeTab === 'favorites' || activeTab === 'all'
        ? activeCategory || 'photo'
        : activeTab;
    const list = customFolders[targetCat] || ['General'];
    const set = new Set<string>(list);
    items.forEach((it) => {
      if (
        (activeTab === 'favorites' ? it.record.isFavorite : it.record.category === targetCat) &&
        it.record.album
      ) {
        set.add(it.record.album);
      }
    });
    return Array.from(set);
  }, [activeTab, activeCategory, customFolders, items]);

  // Robust Native Android Back Button & Gesture Navigation Stack
  useEffect(() => {
    if (activeViewerItem) {
      return registerBackHandler('main_viewer_item', () => {
        setActiveViewerItem(null);
        return true;
      }, 95);
    }
  }, [activeViewerItem]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (isCameraOpen) {
      return registerBackHandler('main_camera_open', () => {
        setIsCameraOpen(false);
        return true;
      }, 90);
    }
  }, [isCameraOpen]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (isAddSheetOpen) {
      return registerBackHandler('main_add_sheet', () => {
        setIsAddSheetOpen(false);
        return true;
      }, 85);
    }
  }, [isAddSheetOpen]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (isCreateFolderOpen) {
      return registerBackHandler('main_create_folder', () => {
        setIsCreateFolderOpen(false);
        return true;
      }, 80);
    }
  }, [isCreateFolderOpen]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (isMoveModalOpen) {
      return registerBackHandler('main_move_modal', () => {
        setIsMoveModalOpen(false);
        return true;
      }, 80);
    }
  }, [isMoveModalOpen]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (folderToRename) {
      return registerBackHandler('main_rename_folder', () => {
        setFolderToRename(null);
        return true;
      }, 80);
    }
  }, [folderToRename]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (showBatchDeleteModal) {
      return registerBackHandler('main_batch_delete', () => {
        setShowBatchDeleteModal(false);
        return true;
      }, 80);
    }
  }, [showBatchDeleteModal]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (importedCountForReminder > 0) {
      return registerBackHandler('main_import_reminder', () => {
        setImportedCountForReminder(0);
        return true;
      }, 80);
    }
  }, [importedCountForReminder]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (isSelectMode) {
      return registerBackHandler('main_select_mode', () => {
        setIsSelectMode(false);
        setSelectedIds(new Set());
        return true;
      }, 50);
    }
  }, [isSelectMode]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (activeSubfolder !== null) {
      return registerBackHandler('main_subfolder_nav', () => {
        setActiveSubfolder(null);
        return true;
      }, 40);
    }
  }, [activeSubfolder]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (activeCategory !== null) {
      return registerBackHandler('main_category_nav', () => {
        setActiveCategory(null);
        setActiveSubfolder(null);
        setIsSelectMode(false);
        setSelectedIds(new Set());
        return true;
      }, 30);
    }
  }, [activeCategory]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (activeNavTab !== 'vault') {
      return registerBackHandler('main_tab_nav', () => {
        setActiveNavTab('vault');
        setActiveCategory(null);
        return true;
      }, 20);
    }
  }, [activeNavTab]);

  const handleCreateFolder = (name: string) => {
    if (activeTab === 'all' || activeTab === 'favorites') return;
    const cat = activeTab;
    const currentList = customFolders[cat] || ['General'];
    if (!currentList.includes(name)) {
      const updated = [...currentList, name];
      setCustomFolders((prev) => ({ ...prev, [cat]: updated }));
      localStorage.setItem(`vault_subfolders_${cat}`, JSON.stringify(updated));
      showToast(`Carpeta "${name}" creada con éxito`, 'success');
      setActiveSubfolder(name);
    }
  };

  const handleRenameFolder = async (oldName: string, newName: string) => {
    if (activeTab === 'all' || activeTab === 'favorites') return;
    if (oldName === 'General') return;
    const cat = activeTab;

    await batchRenameAlbum(cat, oldName, newName);

    // Update in memory items
    setItems((prev) =>
      prev.map((it) => {
        const itemCat = it.record.category;
        const itemAlbum = it.record.album || 'General';
        if (itemCat === cat && itemAlbum === oldName) {
          return { ...it, record: { ...it.record, album: newName } };
        }
        return it;
      })
    );

    // Update custom folders
    const currentList = customFolders[cat] || ['General'];
    const updated = currentList.map((f) => (f === oldName ? newName : f));
    setCustomFolders((prev) => ({ ...prev, [cat]: updated }));
    localStorage.setItem(`vault_subfolders_${cat}`, JSON.stringify(updated));

    if (activeSubfolder === oldName) {
      setActiveSubfolder(newName);
    }

    setFolderToRename(null);
    showToast(`Carpeta renombrada a "${newName}"`, 'success');
  };

  const handleDeleteFolder = async (folderNameToDelete: string) => {
    if (folderNameToDelete === 'General' || activeTab === 'all' || activeTab === 'favorites') return;
    const cat = activeTab;

    // Move any items in this folder back to 'General' in DB and memory
    const itemsInFolder = items.filter(
      (it) => it.record.category === cat && it.record.album === folderNameToDelete
    );

    if (itemsInFolder.length > 0) {
      const ids = itemsInFolder.map((it) => it.id);
      await batchUpdateAlbum(ids, 'General');
      setItems((prev) =>
        prev.map((it) =>
          ids.includes(it.id)
            ? { ...it, record: { ...it.record, album: 'General' } }
            : it
        )
      );
    }

    const currentList = customFolders[cat] || ['General'];
    const updated = currentList.filter((f) => f !== folderNameToDelete);
    setCustomFolders((prev) => ({ ...prev, [cat]: updated.length > 0 ? updated : ['General'] }));
    localStorage.setItem(`vault_subfolders_${cat}`, JSON.stringify(updated.length > 0 ? updated : ['General']));

    if (activeSubfolder === folderNameToDelete) {
      setActiveSubfolder(null);
    }
    showToast(`Carpeta "${folderNameToDelete}" eliminada (sus archivos se pasaron a General)`, 'info');
  };

  const handleMoveSelected = async (targetFolder: string) => {
    if (selectedIds.size === 0) return;
    const ids = Array.from(selectedIds);
    await batchUpdateAlbum(ids, targetFolder);

    // Update in memory state
    setItems((prev) =>
      prev.map((it) =>
        ids.includes(it.id)
          ? { ...it, record: { ...it.record, album: targetFolder } }
          : it
      )
    );

    showToast(`${ids.length} elemento(s) movidos a "${targetFolder}"`, 'success');
    setSelectedIds(new Set());
    setIsSelectMode(false);
    setIsMoveModalOpen(false);
  };

  // Navigation tab and category state references
  const activeNavTabRef = useRef(activeNavTab);
  activeNavTabRef.current = activeNavTab;
  const activeCategoryRef = useRef(activeCategory);
  activeCategoryRef.current = activeCategory;

  const navigateToTab = (tab: 'vault' | 'import' | 'security' | 'settings') => {
    if (tab !== activeNavTab) {
      window.history.pushState({ vaultNav: tab }, '');
      setActiveNavTab(tab);
    }
  };

  const openCategory = (cat: 'photo' | 'video' | 'audio' | 'app') => {
    window.history.pushState({ vaultCat: cat }, '');
    setActiveCategory(cat);
    setActiveTab(cat);
  };

  // Swipe Gesture Handling - retroceso en pantalla únicamente dentro de vistas hijas
  const touchStartX = useRef<number | null>(null);
  const touchStartY = useRef<number | null>(null);

  const handleTouchStart = (e: React.TouchEvent) => {
    touchStartX.current = e.touches[0].clientX;
    touchStartY.current = e.touches[0].clientY;
  };

  const handleTouchEnd = (e: React.TouchEvent) => {
    if (touchStartX.current === null || touchStartY.current === null) return;
    const deltaX = e.changedTouches[0].clientX - touchStartX.current;
    const deltaY = e.changedTouches[0].clientY - touchStartY.current;
    touchStartX.current = null;
    touchStartY.current = null;

    // Deslizar de izquierda a derecha (swipe horizontal para atrás: deltaX > 65 y dominante)
    if (deltaX > 65 && Math.abs(deltaX) > Math.abs(deltaY) * 1.4) {
      const target = e.target as HTMLElement | null;
      if (target && (target.tagName === 'INPUT' || target.tagName === 'SELECT' || target.tagName === 'TEXTAREA')) {
        return;
      }
      triggerBackAction();
    }
  };

  // Total storage calculated from real user files
  const totalStorageBytes = useMemo(() => {
    return items.reduce((acc, it) => acc + (it.record.size || 0), 0);
  }, [items]);

  const storagePercent = useMemo(() => {
    if (items.length === 0) return 0;
    // Percentage calculated on a standard 500MB secure quota partition
    const baseQuota = 500 * 1024 * 1024;
    return Math.min(100, Math.max(1, Math.round((totalStorageBytes / baseQuota) * 100)));
  }, [items.length, totalStorageBytes]);

  // Recent files list matching real user saved files - exactly last 5 records
  const recentList = useMemo(() => {
    return items.slice(0, 5).map((it) => {
      const d = new Date(it.record.timestamp);
      const timeStr = `${String(d.getHours()).padStart(2, '0')}:${String(d.getMinutes()).padStart(2, '0')}`;
      const nameWithoutExt = it.record.name.replace(/\.[^/.]+$/, '');
      const encName = it.record.name.endsWith('.enc') ? it.record.name : `${nameWithoutExt}.enc`;
      const sizeMb = (it.record.size / (1024 * 1024)).toFixed(1).replace('.', ',') + ' MB';
      return {
        id: it.id,
        name: encName,
        size: sizeMb,
        time: `Hoy ${timeStr}`,
        previewUrl: it.record.category === 'photo' || it.record.category === 'video' ? it.blobUrl : null,
        category: it.record.category,
        realItem: it,
      };
    });
  }, [items]);

  // Track last back press timestamp for Double-Back-To-Exit on root
  const lastBackPressTimeRef = useRef<number>(0);

  // Sync refs for state
  useEffect(() => {
    wasSelectingRef.current = isSelectMode || selectedIds.size > 0;
  }, [isSelectMode, selectedIds.size]);

  useEffect(() => {
    wasViewerOpenRef.current = !!activeViewerItem;
  }, [activeViewerItem]);

  useEffect(() => {
    wasCameraOpenRef.current = isCameraOpen;
  }, [isCameraOpen]);

  useEffect(() => {
    wasDeleteModalOpenRef.current = showBatchDeleteModal;
  }, [showBatchDeleteModal]);

  useEffect(() => {
    wasReminderOpenRef.current = importedCountForReminder > 0;
  }, [importedCountForReminder]);

  // History Pushing Effect 1: Subfolder
  const prevSubfolderRef = useRef<string | null>(null);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (activeSubfolder !== null && prevSubfolderRef.current === null) {
      try {
        window.history.pushState({ vaultSubfolder: activeSubfolder }, '');
      } catch {}
    }
    prevSubfolderRef.current = activeSubfolder;
  }, [activeSubfolder]);

  // History Pushing Effect 2: Category
  const prevCategoryRef = useRef<string | null>(null);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (activeCategory !== null && prevCategoryRef.current === null) {
      try {
        window.history.pushState({ vaultCat: activeCategory }, '');
      } catch {}
    }
    prevCategoryRef.current = activeCategory;
  }, [activeCategory]);

  // History Pushing Effect 3: NavTab
  const prevNavTabRef = useRef<string>('vault');
  useEffect(() => {
    if (activeNavTab !== 'vault' && prevNavTabRef.current === 'vault') {
      try {
        window.history.pushState({ vaultNav: activeNavTab }, '');
      } catch {}
    }
    prevNavTabRef.current = activeNavTab;
  }, [activeNavTab]);

  // History Pushing Effect 4: Viewer
  const prevViewerOpenRef = useRef<boolean>(false);
  useEffect(() => {
    if (activeViewerItem !== null && !prevViewerOpenRef.current) {
      try {
        window.history.pushState({ vaultViewer: true }, '');
      } catch {}
    }
    prevViewerOpenRef.current = !!activeViewerItem;
  }, [activeViewerItem]);

  // History Pushing Effect 5: Selection Mode
  const prevSelectingRef = useRef<boolean>(false);
  const isSelectingNow = isSelectMode || selectedIds.size > 0;
  useEffect(() => {
    if (isSelectingNow && !prevSelectingRef.current) {
      try {
        window.history.pushState({ vaultSelecting: true }, '');
      } catch {}
    }
    prevSelectingRef.current = isSelectingNow;
  }, [isSelectingNow]);

  // Initial Root History State
  useEffect(() => {
    try {
      window.history.pushState({ vaultRoot: true }, '');
    } catch {}
  }, []);

  // Process and encrypt incoming files (Photos, Videos, Apps)
  const processFilesForVault = async (files: File[]) => {
    if (!files || files.length === 0) return;

    // Filtrar archivos que ya existan en la bóveda por nombre y tamaño
    const uniqueFiles = files.filter((f) => {
      const alreadyExists = items.some(
        (it) => it.record.name === f.name && it.record.size === f.size
      );
      return !alreadyExists;
    });

    const duplicatesSkipped = files.length - uniqueFiles.length;
    if (duplicatesSkipped > 0) {
      showToast(`${duplicatesSkipped} archivo(s) ya existían en la bóveda y fueron omitidos`, 'alert');
    }

    if (uniqueFiles.length === 0) return;

    const total = uniqueFiles.length;
    showToast(`Iniciando cifrado de ${total} elemento(s)...`, 'info');

    const newRecords: EncryptedVaultItem[] = [];
    const baseTime = Date.now();

    for (let i = 0; i < total; i++) {
      const file = uniqueFiles[i];
      setEncryptingProgress({
        current: i + 1,
        total,
        name: file.name,
      });

      try {
        const buffer = await file.arrayBuffer();
        const encrypted = await encryptBuffer(buffer, masterKey);
        const category = detectCategory(file.name, file.type);

        newRecords.push({
          id: `item_${baseTime}_${i}_${Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 8)}`,
          timestamp: baseTime + i, // preserve selection order
          name: file.name,
          mimeType: file.type || 'application/octet-stream',
          size: file.size,
          iv: encrypted.iv,
          data: encrypted.data,
          album: activeSubfolder !== null ? activeSubfolder : 'General',
          isFavorite: false,
          category,
          lastModified: file.lastModified || undefined,
        });
      } catch (err) {
        console.error(`Error encrypting file ${file.name}:`, err);
      }
    }

    setEncryptingProgress(null);

    if (newRecords.length > 0) {
      // Save all encrypted items in a single, fast IndexedDB transaction
      await saveEncryptedItems(newRecords);
      await loadVaultItems();

      let photoCount = 0;
      let videoCount = 0;
      let appCount = 0;
      newRecords.forEach((r) => {
        if (r.category === 'photo') photoCount++;
        else if (r.category === 'video') videoCount++;
        else appCount++;
      });

      const destList: string[] = [];
      if (photoCount > 0) destList.push(`${photoCount} a Fotos`);
      if (videoCount > 0) destList.push(`${videoCount} a Videos`);
      if (appCount > 0) destList.push(`${appCount} a Aplicaciones`);

      showToast(`Guardados en sus carpetas: ${destList.join(', ')}`, 'success');

      // Navigate to destination folder so user immediately sees their files
      if (photoCount > 0 && videoCount === 0 && appCount === 0) {
        setActiveTab('photo');
      } else if (videoCount > 0 && photoCount === 0 && appCount === 0) {
        setActiveTab('video');
      } else if (appCount > 0 && photoCount === 0 && videoCount === 0) {
        setActiveTab('app');
      }

      const mediaRecords = newRecords.filter((r) => r.category === 'photo' || r.category === 'video' || r.category === 'audio');
      if (mediaRecords.length > 0) {
        setRecentImportedItems(mediaRecords.map((r) => ({ name: r.name, size: r.size })));

        if (isNativeApp()) {
          const itemsToDelete = mediaRecords.map((r) => ({
            filename: r.name,
            size: r.size,
            lastModified: r.lastModified,
            mimeType: r.mimeType,
          }));
          await requestNativeBatchGalleryDeletion(itemsToDelete);
        }
      }
    } else {
      showToast('No se pudieron procesar los archivos seleccionados', 'alert');
    }
  };

  const handleFileInputChange = (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    if (e.target.files && e.target.files.length > 0) {
      // CRITICAL: Convert FileList to a concrete JavaScript array BEFORE clearing input.value!
      // In Chromium / Android Chrome, setting input.value = '' empties e.target.files in place.
      const filesArray = Array.from(e.target.files);
      e.target.value = '';
      processFilesForVault(filesArray);
    }
  };

  // Drag and Drop handlers
  const handleDragOver = (e: React.DragEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setIsDraggingOver(true);
  };

  const handleDragLeave = (e: React.DragEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setIsDraggingOver(false);
  };

  const handleDrop = (e: React.DragEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setIsDraggingOver(false);
    if (e.dataTransfer.files && e.dataTransfer.files.length > 0) {
      const filesArray = Array.from(e.dataTransfer.files);
      processFilesForVault(filesArray);
    }
  };

  // Delete & Favorite
  const handleDeleteItem = async (id: string) => {
    await deleteEncryptedItem(id);
    setItems((prev) => prev.filter((p) => p.id !== id));
    setActiveViewerItem(null);
  };

  const handleToggleFavorite = async (id: string, isFav: boolean) => {
    await updateItemMetadata(id, { isFavorite: isFav });
    setItems((prev) =>
      prev.map((p) =>
        p.id === id ? { ...p, record: { ...p.record, isFavorite: isFav } } : p
      )
    );
  };

  // Batch actions
  const toggleSelectItem = (id: string, e?: React.MouseEvent | React.TouchEvent) => {
    if (e && typeof e.stopPropagation === 'function') {
      e.stopPropagation();
    }
    setSelectedIds((prev) => {
      const next = new Set(prev);
      if (next.has(id)) {
        next.delete(id);
      } else {
        next.add(id);
      }
      return next;
    });
  };

  const handleSelectAll = () => {
    if (selectedIds.size === filteredItems.length) {
      setSelectedIds(new Set());
    } else {
      setSelectedIds(new Set(filteredItems.map((p) => p.id)));
    }
  };

  const handleBatchDelete = () => {
    if (selectedIds.size === 0) return;
    setShowBatchDeleteModal(true);
  };

  const confirmBatchDelete = async () => {
    if (selectedIds.size === 0) return;
    setIsDeletingBatch(true);
    try {
      const idsToDelete = Array.from(selectedIds);
      await deleteMultipleEncryptedItems(idsToDelete);
      showToast(`${idsToDelete.length} elemento(s) eliminado(s) de la bóveda`, 'success');
      setItems((prev) => prev.filter((p) => !selectedIds.has(p.id)));
      setSelectedIds(new Set());
      setIsSelectMode(false);
      setShowBatchDeleteModal(false);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Error deleting items:', err);
      showToast('Error al eliminar elementos', 'error');
    } finally {
      setIsDeletingBatch(false);
    }
  };

  const handleCardPointerDown = (id: string) => {
    isLongPressTriggered.current = false;
    longPressTimeoutRef.current = setTimeout(() => {
      isLongPressTriggered.current = true;
      if (typeof navigator !== 'undefined' && 'vibrate' in navigator) {
        try {
          navigator.vibrate(40);
        } catch {}
      }
      setIsSelectMode(true);
      setSelectedIds((prev) => new Set(prev).add(id));
    }, 400);
  };

  const handleCardPointerUp = () => {
    if (longPressTimeoutRef.current) {
      clearTimeout(longPressTimeoutRef.current);
      longPressTimeoutRef.current = null;
    }
  };

  const handleBatchExport = () => {
    if (selectedIds.size === 0) return;
    const selected = items.filter((p) => selectedIds.has(p.id));

    selected.forEach((p, idx) => {
      setTimeout(() => {
        const link = document.createElement('a');
        link.href = p.blobUrl;
        link.download = p.record.name;
        document.body.appendChild(link);
        link.click();
        document.body.removeChild(link);
      }, idx * 250);
    });

    showToast(`Exportando ${selected.length} elemento(s)...`, 'success');
    setIsSelectMode(false);
    setSelectedIds(new Set());
  };

  // Counts by category
  const counts = useMemo(() => {
    let photos = 0;
    let videos = 0;
    let audios = 0;
    let apps = 0;
    let favs = 0;
    let totalBytes = 0;

    items.forEach((item) => {
      totalBytes += item.record.size || 0;
      if (item.record.category === 'photo') photos++;
      else if (item.record.category === 'video') videos++;
      else if (item.record.category === 'audio') audios++;
      else apps++;

      if (item.record.isFavorite) favs++;
    });

    return {
      photos,
      videos,
      audios,
      apps,
      favs,
      totalBytes,
      totalMB: (totalBytes / (1024 * 1024)).toFixed(1),
    };
  }, [items]);

  // Filtered items
  const filteredItems = useMemo(() => {
    return items.filter((item) => {
      if (activeTab === 'favorites') {
        if (!item.record.isFavorite) return false;
      } else if (activeTab !== 'all') {
        if (item.record.category !== activeTab) return false;

        // Subfolder filter
        if (activeSubfolder !== null) {
          const itemAlbum = item.record.album || 'General';
          if (itemAlbum !== activeSubfolder) return false;
        }
      }

      if (searchQuery.trim()) {
        const q = searchQuery.toLowerCase();
        const matchesName = (item.record.name || '').toLowerCase().includes(q);
        const matchesDate = new Date(item.record.timestamp).toLocaleDateString().includes(q);
        const matchesAlbum = (item.record.album || '').toLowerCase().includes(q);
        return matchesName || matchesDate || matchesAlbum;
      }

      return true;
    });
  }, [items, activeTab, activeSubfolder, searchQuery]);

  const formatSize = (bytes: number) => {
    if (!bytes) return '0 KB';
    if (bytes < 1024 * 1024) return (bytes / 1024).toFixed(1) + ' KB';
    return (bytes / (1024 * 1024)).toFixed(1) + ' MB';
  };

  return (
    <div
      onTouchStart={handleTouchStart}
      onTouchEnd={handleTouchEnd}
      onDragOver={handleDragOver}
      onDragLeave={handleDragLeave}
      onDrop={handleDrop}
      className="min-h-screen bg-[#090d16] text-slate-100 flex flex-col relative select-none pb-28 w-full max-w-full"
    >
      {/* Drag & Drop Visual Overlay */}
      {isDraggingOver && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 bg-[#0a121d]/90 backdrop-blur-md border-4 border-dashed border-[#00e5ff] flex flex-col items-center justify-center pointer-events-none transition-all">
          <UploadCloud className="w-16 h-16 text-[#00e5ff] animate-bounce mb-3" />
          <h3 className="text-xl font-bold text-white">Suelta fotos, videos o aplicaciones aquí</h3>
          <p className="text-sm text-cyan-200">Se cifrarán con AES-GCM de 256 bits inmediatamente</p>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Screen 2: Importar Tab */}
      {activeNavTab === 'import' ? (
        <ImportVaultView
          masterKey={masterKey}
          onImportComplete={async (imported) => {
            await loadVaultItems();
            setActiveNavTab('vault');
            if (imported && imported.length > 0) {
              const photoCount = imported.filter((i) => i.category === 'photo').length;
              const videoCount = imported.filter((i) => i.category === 'video').length;
              const appCount = imported.filter((i) => i.category === 'app').length;
              const audioCount = imported.filter((i) => i.category === 'audio').length;
