ARCHIVO: src/db/vaultDb.ts
================================================================================
/**
 * IndexedDB storage engine for Bóveda Segura.
 * Stores AES-GCM encrypted binary photos, videos, and application files locally.
 */

import { hexToBuf } from '../crypto/vaultCrypto';

export type VaultItemCategory = 'photo' | 'video' | 'audio' | 'app';

export function detectCategory(filename: string, mimeType: string): VaultItemCategory {
  const lowerName = filename.toLowerCase();
  const lowerMime = (mimeType || '').toLowerCase();

  if (
    lowerMime.startsWith('video/') ||
    /\.(mp4|webm|mkv|mov|avi|flv|m4v|3gp|wmv|ts)$/i.test(lowerName)
  ) {
    return 'video';
  }

  if (
    lowerMime.startsWith('audio/') ||
    /\.(mp3|wav|ogg|m4a|aac|flac|opus|wma|amr|mid|midi)$/i.test(lowerName)
  ) {
    return 'audio';
  }

  if (
    lowerMime.includes('android.package-archive') ||
    lowerMime.includes('x-msdownload') ||
    lowerMime.includes('zip') ||
    lowerMime.includes('x-zip') ||
    lowerMime.includes('octet-stream') ||
    lowerMime.includes('pdf') ||
    /\.(apk|exe|msi|dmg|pkg|deb|rpm|ipa|zip|rar|tar|gz|7z|bin|iso|appimage|jar|pdf|docx?|xlsx?|pptx?|txt)$/i.test(lowerName)
  ) {
    return 'app';
  }

  if (
    lowerMime.startsWith('image/') ||
    /\.(jpg|jpeg|png|gif|webp|bmp|svg|avif|heic|ico)$/i.test(lowerName)
  ) {
    return 'photo';
  }

  // Fallback for document or application packages
  return 'app';
}

export interface EncryptedVaultItem {
  id: string;
  timestamp: number;
  name: string;
  mimeType: string;
  size: number;
  iv: number[];
  data: ArrayBuffer;
  album?: string;
  isFavorite?: boolean;
  category: VaultItemCategory;
  lastModified?: number;
  originalUri?: string;
  deletedAt?: number;
}

// Backwards compatibility alias
export type EncryptedPhotoRecord = EncryptedVaultItem;

const DB_NAME = 'SecurePhotoVaultDB';
const STORE_NAME = 'encrypted_photos';
const DB_VERSION = 2;

let dbInstance: IDBDatabase | null = null;

export function openVaultDatabase(): Promise<IDBDatabase> {
  if (dbInstance) return Promise.resolve(dbInstance);

  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const request = indexedDB.open(DB_NAME, DB_VERSION);

    request.onerror = () => {
      reject(request.error);
    };

    request.onsuccess = () => {
      dbInstance = request.result;
      resolve(dbInstance);
    };

    request.onupgradeneeded = (event) => {
      const db = (event.target as IDBOpenDBRequest).result;
      if (!db.objectStoreNames.contains(STORE_NAME)) {
        const store = db.createObjectStore(STORE_NAME, { keyPath: 'id' });
        store.createIndex('timestamp', 'timestamp', { unique: false });
      }
    };
  });
}

export async function saveEncryptedItem(record: EncryptedVaultItem): Promise<void> {
  return saveEncryptedItems([record]);
}

export async function saveEncryptedItems(records: EncryptedVaultItem[]): Promise<void> {
  if (!records || records.length === 0) return;
  const db = await openVaultDatabase();
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const transaction = db.transaction([STORE_NAME], 'readwrite');
    const store = transaction.objectStore(STORE_NAME);

    transaction.oncomplete = () => resolve();
    transaction.onerror = () => reject(transaction.error);
    transaction.onabort = () => reject(transaction.error);

    for (const record of records) {
      store.put(record);
    }
  });
}

// Alias for photo saving
export const saveEncryptedPhoto = saveEncryptedItem;

export async function getAllEncryptedItems(includeTrash: boolean = false): Promise<EncryptedVaultItem[]> {
  const db = await openVaultDatabase();
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const transaction = db.transaction([STORE_NAME], 'readonly');
    const store = transaction.objectStore(STORE_NAME);
    const request = store.getAll();

    request.onsuccess = () => {
      const all: EncryptedVaultItem[] = (request.result || []).map((raw: any) => {
        const name = raw.name || `item_${raw.id}`;
        const mimeType = raw.mimeType || (raw.category === 'video' ? 'video/mp4' : 'image/jpeg');
        const category = raw.category || detectCategory(name, mimeType);

        return {
          id: raw.id,
          timestamp: raw.timestamp || Date.now(),
          name,
          mimeType,
          size: raw.size || (raw.data ? raw.data.byteLength : 0),
          iv: raw.iv,
          data: raw.data,
          album: raw.album || 'General',
          isFavorite: !!raw.isFavorite,
          category,
          lastModified: raw.lastModified,
          originalUri: raw.originalUri,
          deletedAt: raw.deletedAt,
        };
      });

      // Filter out items in trash unless explicitly requested
      const results = includeTrash ? all : all.filter((item) => !item.deletedAt);
      resolve(results);
    };
    request.onerror = () => reject(request.error);
  });
}

// Alias for photos
export const getAllEncryptedPhotos = getAllEncryptedItems;

/**
 * Get all items currently in the Encrypted Trash / Papelera de Reciclaje
 */
export async function getTrashItems(): Promise<EncryptedVaultItem[]> {
  const all = await getAllEncryptedItems(true);
  return all
    .filter((item) => typeof item.deletedAt === 'number' && item.deletedAt > 0)
    .sort((a, b) => (b.deletedAt || 0) - (a.deletedAt || 0));
}

/**
 * Move a single item to the trash (soft-delete with 30-day countdown)
 */
export async function moveToTrash(id: string): Promise<void> {
  return batchMoveToTrash([id]);
}

/**
 * Move multiple items to the trash
 */
export async function batchMoveToTrash(ids: string[]): Promise<void> {
  if (!ids || ids.length === 0) return;
  const db = await openVaultDatabase();
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const transaction = db.transaction([STORE_NAME], 'readwrite');
    const store = transaction.objectStore(STORE_NAME);
    const now = Date.now();

    ids.forEach((id) => {
      const getReq = store.get(id);
      getReq.onsuccess = () => {
        if (getReq.result) {
          store.put({ ...getReq.result, deletedAt: now });
        }
      };
    });

    transaction.oncomplete = () => {
      window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('vault_trash_updated'));
      resolve();
    };
    transaction.onerror = () => reject(transaction.error);
  });
}

/**
 * Restore an item from the trash back to the active vault
 */
export async function restoreFromTrash(id: string): Promise<void> {
  return batchRestoreFromTrash([id]);
}

/**
 * Restore multiple items from trash
 */
export async function batchRestoreFromTrash(ids: string[]): Promise<void> {
  if (!ids || ids.length === 0) return;
  const db = await openVaultDatabase();
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const transaction = db.transaction([STORE_NAME], 'readwrite');
    const store = transaction.objectStore(STORE_NAME);

    ids.forEach((id) => {
      const getReq = store.get(id);
      getReq.onsuccess = () => {
        if (getReq.result) {
          const item = { ...getReq.result };
          delete item.deletedAt;
          store.put(item);
        }
      };
    });

    transaction.oncomplete = () => {
      window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('vault_trash_updated'));
      resolve();
    };
    transaction.onerror = () => reject(transaction.error);
  });
}

/**
 * Empty the entire trash permanently (hard-delete)
 */
export async function emptyTrash(): Promise<number> {
  const trashItems = await getTrashItems();
  if (trashItems.length === 0) return 0;

  const ids = trashItems.map((item) => item.id);
  await deleteMultipleEncryptedItems(ids);
  window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('vault_trash_updated'));
  return ids.length;
}

/**
 * Automatically purge items that have been in the trash for more than maxDays (default 30 days)
 */
export async function autoPurgeTrash(maxDays: number = 30): Promise<number> {
  const trashItems = await getTrashItems();
  const cutoff = Date.now() - maxDays * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
  const expiredIds = trashItems
    .filter((item) => item.deletedAt && item.deletedAt < cutoff)
    .map((item) => item.id);

  if (expiredIds.length > 0) {
    await deleteMultipleEncryptedItems(expiredIds);
    window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('vault_trash_updated'));
  }
  return expiredIds.length;
}

/**
 * Get statistics of the encrypted trash
 */
export async function getTrashStats(): Promise<{ count: number; totalBytes: number }> {
  const trash = await getTrashItems();
  let totalBytes = 0;
  trash.forEach((item) => {
    totalBytes += item.size || item.data?.byteLength || 0;
  });
  return {
    count: trash.length,
    totalBytes,
  };
}

export async function getEncryptedItemById(id: string): Promise<EncryptedVaultItem | null> {
  const db = await openVaultDatabase();
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const transaction = db.transaction([STORE_NAME], 'readonly');
    const store = transaction.objectStore(STORE_NAME);
    const request = store.get(id);

    request.onsuccess = () => {
      const raw = request.result;
      if (!raw) {
        resolve(null);
        return;
      }
      const name = raw.name || `item_${raw.id}`;
      const mimeType = raw.mimeType || 'image/jpeg';
      const category = raw.category || detectCategory(name, mimeType);

      resolve({
        id: raw.id,
        timestamp: raw.timestamp,
        name,
        mimeType,
        size: raw.size || (raw.data ? raw.data.byteLength : 0),
        iv: raw.iv,
        data: raw.data,
        album: raw.album || 'General',
        isFavorite: !!raw.isFavorite,
        category,
        deletedAt: raw.deletedAt,
      });
    };
    request.onerror = () => reject(request.error);
  });
}

export async function deleteEncryptedItem(id: string): Promise<void> {
  const db = await openVaultDatabase();
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const transaction = db.transaction([STORE_NAME], 'readwrite');
    const store = transaction.objectStore(STORE_NAME);
    const request = store.delete(id);

    request.onsuccess = () => resolve();
    request.onerror = () => reject(request.error);
  });
}

export const deleteEncryptedPhoto = deleteEncryptedItem;

export async function deleteMultipleEncryptedItems(ids: string[]): Promise<void> {
  const db = await openVaultDatabase();
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const transaction = db.transaction([STORE_NAME], 'readwrite');
    const store = transaction.objectStore(STORE_NAME);

    ids.forEach((id) => {
      store.delete(id);
    });

    transaction.oncomplete = () => resolve();
    transaction.onerror = () => reject(transaction.error);
  });
}

export const deleteMultipleEncryptedPhotos = deleteMultipleEncryptedItems;

export async function updateItemMetadata(
  id: string,
  updates: Partial<Omit<EncryptedVaultItem, 'id' | 'data' | 'iv'>>
): Promise<void> {
  const db = await openVaultDatabase();
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const transaction = db.transaction([STORE_NAME], 'readwrite');
    const store = transaction.objectStore(STORE_NAME);
    const request = store.get(id);

    request.onsuccess = () => {
      const existing = request.result;
      if (!existing) {
        reject(new Error('Record not found'));
        return;
      }
      const updated = { ...existing, ...updates };
      const putRequest = store.put(updated);
      putRequest.onsuccess = () => resolve();
      putRequest.onerror = () => reject(putRequest.error);
    };
    request.onerror = () => reject(request.error);
  });
}

export const updatePhotoMetadata = updateItemMetadata;

export async function batchUpdateAlbum(ids: string[], album: string): Promise<void> {
  const db = await openVaultDatabase();
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const transaction = db.transaction([STORE_NAME], 'readwrite');
    const store = transaction.objectStore(STORE_NAME);

    ids.forEach((id) => {
      const getReq = store.get(id);
      getReq.onsuccess = () => {
        if (getReq.result) {
          store.put({ ...getReq.result, album });
        }
      };
    });

    transaction.oncomplete = () => resolve();
    transaction.onerror = () => reject(transaction.error);
  });
}

export async function batchRenameAlbum(
  category: VaultItemCategory,
  oldAlbum: string,
  newAlbum: string
): Promise<void> {
  const db = await openVaultDatabase();
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const transaction = db.transaction([STORE_NAME], 'readwrite');
    const store = transaction.objectStore(STORE_NAME);
    const request = store.openCursor();

    request.onsuccess = (event) => {
      const cursor = (event.target as IDBRequest<IDBCursorWithValue>).result;
      if (cursor) {
        const item: EncryptedVaultItem = cursor.value;
        const currentAlbum = item.album || 'General';
        if (item.category === category && currentAlbum === oldAlbum) {
          cursor.update({ ...item, album: newAlbum });
        }
        cursor.continue();
      }
    };

    transaction.oncomplete = () => resolve();
    transaction.onerror = () => reject(transaction.error);
  });
}

export async function clearVaultDatabase(): Promise<void> {
  const db = await openVaultDatabase();
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const transaction = db.transaction([STORE_NAME], 'readwrite');
    const store = transaction.objectStore(STORE_NAME);
    const request = store.clear();

    request.onsuccess = () => resolve();
    request.onerror = () => reject(request.error);
  });
}

export async function getVaultStats(): Promise<{
  count: number;
  totalBytes: number;
  photoCount: number;
  videoCount: number;
  appCount: number;
}> {
  const items = await getAllEncryptedItems();
  let totalBytes = 0;
  let photoCount = 0;
  let videoCount = 0;
  let appCount = 0;

  items.forEach((item) => {
    totalBytes += item.size || item.data.byteLength || 0;
    if (item.category === 'photo') photoCount++;
    else if (item.category === 'video') videoCount++;
    else appCount++;
  });

  return {
    count: items.length,
    totalBytes,
    photoCount,
    videoCount,
    appCount,
  };
}

/**
 * Fast synchronous binary buffer to Base64 conversion with 32KB chunks to prevent stack limits
 */
export function bufferToBase64(buffer: ArrayBuffer | Uint8Array): string {
  try {
    const bytes = buffer instanceof Uint8Array ? buffer : new Uint8Array(buffer);
    let binary = '';
    const len = bytes.byteLength;
    const chunkSize = 0x8000; // 32KB chunks
    for (let i = 0; i < len; i += chunkSize) {
      binary += String.fromCharCode.apply(null, bytes.subarray(i, Math.min(i + chunkSize, len)) as any);
    }
    return btoa(binary);
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn('bufferToBase64 fallback failed:', err);
    return '';
  }
}

/**
 * Creates exportable JSON containing encrypted blobs as Base64 strings.
 * Highly optimized native FileReader-based ArrayBuffer to Base64 conversion with fallback.
 */
async function arrayBufferToBase64Async(buffer: ArrayBuffer | Uint8Array | string | undefined | null): Promise<string> {
  if (!buffer) return '';
  if (typeof buffer === 'string') return buffer;

  const uint8 = buffer instanceof Uint8Array ? buffer : new Uint8Array(buffer);

  return new Promise((resolve) => {
    try {
      const blob = new Blob([uint8 as unknown as BlobPart]);
      const reader = new FileReader();

      // Guard timeout in case FileReader hangs on mobile browser
      const timeoutId = setTimeout(() => {
        try {
          resolve(bufferToBase64(uint8));
        } catch {
          resolve('');
        }
      }, 2500);

      reader.onloadend = () => {
        clearTimeout(timeoutId);
        try {
          const dataUrl = reader.result as string;
          if (!dataUrl) {
            resolve(bufferToBase64(uint8));
            return;
          }
          const commaIndex = dataUrl.indexOf(',');
          const base64 = commaIndex > -1 ? dataUrl.substring(commaIndex + 1) : '';
          resolve(base64);
        } catch (e) {
          resolve(bufferToBase64(uint8));
        }
      };

      reader.onerror = () => {
        clearTimeout(timeoutId);
        resolve(bufferToBase64(uint8));
      };

      reader.readAsDataURL(blob);
    } catch (err) {
      try {
        resolve(bufferToBase64(uint8));
      } catch {
        resolve('');
      }
    }
  });
}

/**
 * Fast native conversion from Base64 to ArrayBuffer without fetch data URL memory limits.
 * Uses chunked decoding for large files to avoid V8 mobile heap exhaustion and atob string limits.
 */
function base64ToArrayBuffer(rawBase64: string): ArrayBuffer {
  if (!rawBase64) return new ArrayBuffer(0);
  try {
    let clean = rawBase64;
    const commaIdx = clean.indexOf(',');
    if (commaIdx !== -1) {
      clean = clean.substring(commaIdx + 1);
    }
    clean = clean.replace(/[\r\n\s]/g, '');

    const padding = clean.endsWith('==') ? 2 : clean.endsWith('=') ? 1 : 0;
    const totalLen = Math.floor((clean.length * 3) / 4) - padding;
    if (totalLen <= 0) return new ArrayBuffer(0);

    const result = new Uint8Array(totalLen);
    const CHUNK_SIZE = 65536; // 64KB multiple of 4
    let destOffset = 0;

    for (let i = 0; i < clean.length; i += CHUNK_SIZE) {
      const chunkStr = clean.substring(i, Math.min(i + CHUNK_SIZE, clean.length));
      const binChunk = atob(chunkStr);
      for (let j = 0; j < binChunk.length && destOffset < totalLen; j++) {
        result[destOffset++] = binChunk.charCodeAt(j);
      }
    }
    return result.buffer;
  } catch (err) {
    console.error('Error decoding base64:', err);
    return new ArrayBuffer(0);
  }
}

async function base64ToArrayBufferAsync(base64: string): Promise<ArrayBuffer> {
  return base64ToArrayBuffer(base64);
}

function getSafeFolders(cat: string): string[] {
  try {
    const raw = localStorage.getItem(`vault_subfolders_${cat}`);
    if (raw) {
      const parsed = JSON.parse(raw);
      if (Array.isArray(parsed) && parsed.length > 0) return parsed;
    }
  } catch (e) {
    console.warn(`Error reading subfolders for ${cat}:`, e);
  }
  return ['General'];
}

/**
 * Exports all encrypted items and cryptographic salt to a Blob in streaming chunks
 */
export async function exportEncryptedBackupBlob(
  onProgress?: (current: number, total: number) => void
): Promise<Blob> {
  const db = await openVaultDatabase();
  const blobParts: BlobPart[] = [];

  const saltHex = localStorage.getItem('vault_salt') || '';
  const testIvHex = localStorage.getItem('vault_test_iv') || '';
  const testCipherHex = localStorage.getItem('vault_test_cipher') || '';
  const customFolders = {
    photo: getSafeFolders('photo'),
    video: getSafeFolders('video'),
    audio: getSafeFolders('audio'),
    app: getSafeFolders('app'),
  };

  blobParts.push('{\n');
  blobParts.push('  "version": 3,\n');
  blobParts.push('  "appName": "Bóveda Segura - Fotos, Videos y Apps",\n');
  blobParts.push(`  "exportedAt": ${JSON.stringify(new Date().toISOString())},\n`);
  blobParts.push(`  "saltHex": ${JSON.stringify(saltHex)},\n`);
  blobParts.push(`  "testIvHex": ${JSON.stringify(testIvHex)},\n`);
  blobParts.push(`  "testCipherHex": ${JSON.stringify(testCipherHex)},\n`);
  blobParts.push(`  "customFolders": ${JSON.stringify(customFolders)},\n`);
  blobParts.push('  "items": [\n');

  // Pre-flight key list query (uses very little memory)
  const keys = await new Promise<IDBValidKey[]>((resolve) => {
    const transaction = db.transaction([STORE_NAME], 'readonly');
    const store = transaction.objectStore(STORE_NAME);
    const req = store.getAllKeys();
    req.onsuccess = () => resolve(req.result || []);
    req.onerror = () => resolve([]);
  });

  let firstItem = true;
  const totalCount = keys.length;

  // Process and stringify each item sequentially, opening fresh small transactions
  for (let i = 0; i < totalCount; i++) {
    const id = keys[i];

    if (onProgress) {
      onProgress(i + 1, totalCount);
    }

    const raw = await new Promise<any>((resolve) => {
      const transaction = db.transaction([STORE_NAME], 'readonly');
      const store = transaction.objectStore(STORE_NAME);
      const req = store.get(id);
      req.onsuccess = () => resolve(req.result);
      req.onerror = () => resolve(null);
    });

    if (!raw) continue;

    try {
      const name = raw.name || `item_${raw.id}`;
      const mimeType = raw.mimeType || (raw.category === 'video' ? 'video/mp4' : 'image/jpeg');
      const category = raw.category || detectCategory(name, mimeType);

      // Memory-efficient native base64 conversion
      const base64Data = raw.data ? await arrayBufferToBase64Async(raw.data) : '';
      
      // IMMEDIATELY dereference raw.data to free binary memory from V8 heap
      raw.data = null;

      const itemObj = {
        id: raw.id,
        timestamp: raw.timestamp || Date.now(),
        name,
        mimeType,
        size: raw.size || 0,
        iv: Array.isArray(raw.iv) ? raw.iv : (raw.iv ? Array.from(raw.iv) : []),
        base64Data,
        album: raw.album || 'General',
        isFavorite: !!raw.isFavorite,
        category,
      };

      if (!firstItem) {
        blobParts.push(',\n');
      }
      blobParts.push('    ' + JSON.stringify(itemObj));
      firstItem = false;
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Error exporting item details:', err);
    }

    // Force full yield to event loop and Garbage Collection between files
    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 30));
  }

  blobParts.push('\n  ]\n}');

  return new Blob(blobParts, { type: 'application/json' });
}

export async function exportEncryptedBackup(): Promise<string> {
  const blob = await exportEncryptedBackupBlob();
  return await blob.text();
}

/**
 * Attempts to repair truncated JSON files if missing closing brackets
 */
export function parseWithAutoRepair(jsonStr: string): any {
  let trimmed = jsonStr.trim();
  if (!trimmed) {
    throw new Error('El archivo de copia de seguridad está completamente vacío');
  }

  // Strip Byte Order Mark (BOM) if present
  if (trimmed.charCodeAt(0) === 0xFEFF) {
    trimmed = trimmed.slice(1);
  }
  // Strip trailing null characters and spaces
  trimmed = trimmed.replace(/\0+$/, '').trim();

  // Try direct parse first in case it was just padded
  try {
    return JSON.parse(trimmed);
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn('Initial parse in auto-repair failed, attempting structural repair:', err);
  }

  // Bracket balancing scan ignoring string literals and escapes
  let inString = false;
  let escape = false;
  const stack: ('{' | '[')[] = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < trimmed.length; i++) {
    const char = trimmed[i];
    if (escape) {
      escape = false;
      continue;
    }
    if (char === '\\') {
      escape = true;
      continue;
    }
    if (char === '"') {
      inString = !inString;
      continue;
    }
    if (!inString) {
      if (char === '{') {
        stack.push('{');
      } else if (char === '[') {
        stack.push('[');
      } else if (char === '}') {
        if (stack[stack.length - 1] === '{') {
          stack.pop();
        }
      } else if (char === ']') {
        if (stack[stack.length - 1] === '[') {
          stack.pop();
        }
      }
    }
  }

  // Find the last complete item ending with '}'
  const lastItemEnd = trimmed.lastIndexOf('}');
  if (lastItemEnd > 0) {
    let repaired = trimmed.substring(0, lastItemEnd + 1).trim();
    if (repaired.endsWith(',')) {
      repaired = repaired.slice(0, -1).trim();
    }

    // Now balance the stack for this repaired prefix
    const prefixStack: ('{' | '[')[] = [];
    inString = false;
    escape = false;
    for (let i = 0; i < repaired.length; i++) {
      const char = repaired[i];
      if (escape) { escape = false; continue; }
      if (char === '\\') { escape = true; continue; }
      if (char === '"') { inString = !inString; continue; }
      if (!inString) {
        if (char === '{') prefixStack.push('{');
        else if (char === '[') prefixStack.push('[');
        else if (char === '}') { if (prefixStack[prefixStack.length - 1] === '{') prefixStack.pop(); }
        else if (char === ']') { if (prefixStack[prefixStack.length - 1] === '[') prefixStack.pop(); }
      }
    }

    // Close open structures in reverse order
    while (prefixStack.length > 0) {
      const openToken = prefixStack.pop();
      if (openToken === '{') {
        repaired += '\n}';
      } else if (openToken === '[') {
        repaired += '\n  ]';
      }
    }

    try {
      return JSON.parse(repaired);
    } catch (err2: any) {
      console.error('Failed auto-repaired JSON parse:', err2);
      throw new Error(`El archivo de copia de seguridad está dañado: ${err2?.message || 'Unexpected end of JSON input'}`);
    }
  }

  throw new Error('El archivo de copia de seguridad está dañado o incompleto (Unexpected end of JSON input)');
}

export interface BackupHeaderInfo {
  saltHex?: string;
  testIvHex?: string;
  testCipherHex?: string;
  customFolders?: any;
  hasItems: boolean;
}

/**
 * Rapidly reads only the first ~512KB of the backup file to inspect header and cryptographic tokens,
 * without loading the rest of a multi-hundred-megabyte file into memory.
 */
export async function readBackupHeaderFromBlob(blob: Blob): Promise<BackupHeaderInfo> {
  const headerSlice = blob.slice(0, Math.min(blob.size, 512 * 1024));
  const headerText = await headerSlice.text();

  const saltMatch = headerText.match(/"saltHex"\s*:\s*"([a-fA-F0-9]+)"/);
  const ivMatch = headerText.match(/"testIvHex"\s*:\s*"([a-fA-F0-9]+)"/);
  const cipherMatch = headerText.match(/"testCipherHex"\s*:\s*"([a-fA-F0-9]+)"/);

  let customFolders: any = null;
  const foldersMatch = headerText.match(/"customFolders"\s*:\s*(\{.+?\})/s);
  if (foldersMatch) {
    try {
      customFolders = JSON.parse(foldersMatch[1]);
    } catch {}
  }

  const hasItems = /"items"\s*:\s*\[|"photos"\s*:\s*\[|"data"\s*:\s*\[|"records"\s*:\s*\[|^\s*\[/m.test(headerText);

  return {
    saltHex: saltMatch?.[1],
    testIvHex: ivMatch?.[1],
    testCipherHex: cipherMatch?.[1],
    customFolders,
    hasItems,
  };
}

async function saveItemsBatch(db: IDBDatabase, records: EncryptedVaultItem[]): Promise<void> {
  if (records.length === 0) return;
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    try {
      const tx = db.transaction([STORE_NAME], 'readwrite');
      const store = tx.objectStore(STORE_NAME);
      for (const rec of records) {
        store.put(rec);
      }
      tx.oncomplete = () => resolve();
      tx.onerror = () => reject(tx.error || new Error('Error al escribir registros en IndexedDB'));
      tx.onabort = () => reject(new Error('Transacción abortada'));
    } catch (err) {
      reject(err);
    }
  });
}

/**
 * Streams and restores encrypted items directly from a Blob or File of ANY size (e.g. 548MB+)
 * using a memory-constant chunked JSON object parser. It processes items one-by-one, saves
 * each to IndexedDB, and releases memory immediately, avoiding V8 string length limits and mobile crashes.
 */
export async function importEncryptedBackupBlobStream(
  blob: Blob,
  onProgress?: (loadedBytes: number, totalBytes: number, itemCount: number) => void,
  overwriteCryptographicTokens: boolean = true
): Promise<{ importedCount: number; saltHex?: string; testIvHex?: string; testCipherHex?: string }> {
  // First, verify header tokens if present
  const header = await readBackupHeaderFromBlob(blob);

  if (header.saltHex && header.testIvHex && header.testCipherHex) {
    if (overwriteCryptographicTokens || !localStorage.getItem('vault_salt')) {
      localStorage.setItem('vault_salt', header.saltHex);
      localStorage.setItem('vault_test_iv', header.testIvHex);
      localStorage.setItem('vault_test_cipher', header.testCipherHex);
    }
  }

  if (header.customFolders) {
    try {
      if (Array.isArray(header.customFolders.photo)) localStorage.setItem('vault_subfolders_photo', JSON.stringify(header.customFolders.photo));
      if (Array.isArray(header.customFolders.video)) localStorage.setItem('vault_subfolders_video', JSON.stringify(header.customFolders.video));
      if (Array.isArray(header.customFolders.audio)) localStorage.setItem('vault_subfolders_audio', JSON.stringify(header.customFolders.audio));
      if (Array.isArray(header.customFolders.app)) localStorage.setItem('vault_subfolders_app', JSON.stringify(header.customFolders.app));
    } catch {}
  }

  const db = await openVaultDatabase();
  let count = 0;
  const totalBytes = blob.size;
  const CHUNK_SIZE = 1024 * 1024 * 4; // 4MB stream slices for high throughput
  let offset = 0;

  let buffer = '';
  let inArray = false;
  let depth = 0;
  let inString = false;
  let escape = false;
  let itemStart = -1;
  let cursor = 0;

  let pendingBatch: EncryptedVaultItem[] = [];
  const decoder = new TextDecoder('utf-8', { fatal: false });

  const parseAndQueueItem = (item: any) => {
    let rawBase64 =
      item.base64Data ||
      item.base64 ||
      item.content ||
      item.dataUrl ||
      item.data_url ||
      item.base64_data ||
      item.fileData ||
      item.file_data ||
      '';
    if (!rawBase64 && typeof item.data === 'string') {
      rawBase64 = item.data;
    }

    let dataBuffer: ArrayBuffer;
    if (item.data instanceof ArrayBuffer) {
      dataBuffer = item.data;
    } else if (rawBase64) {
      dataBuffer = base64ToArrayBuffer(rawBase64);
    } else if (Array.isArray(item.data)) {
      dataBuffer = new Uint8Array(item.data).buffer;
    } else if (item.data && typeof item.data === 'object') {
      if (item.data.type === 'Buffer' && Array.isArray(item.data.data)) {
        dataBuffer = new Uint8Array(item.data.data).buffer;
      } else {
        const values = Object.values(item.data).map(Number);
        dataBuffer = new Uint8Array(values).buffer;
      }
    } else {
      dataBuffer = new ArrayBuffer(0);
    }

    // Auto-generate safe fallback ID and IV
    const id = item.id || `item_${Math.random().toString(36).substring(2, 11)}_${Date.now()}`;
    let iv: number[] = [];
    if (Array.isArray(item.iv)) {
      iv = item.iv;
    } else if (item.iv && typeof item.iv === 'object') {
      iv = Object.values(item.iv).map(Number);
    } else if (typeof item.iv === 'string') {
      if (/^[0-9a-fA-F]+$/.test(item.iv)) {
        iv = Array.from(hexToBuf(item.iv));
      } else {
        try {
          const bin = atob(item.iv);
          iv = Array.from(bin).map((c) => c.charCodeAt(0));
        } catch {}
      }
    }

    const name = item.name || item.fileName || item.filename || item.title || 'archivo_restaurado';
    const mimeType = item.mimeType || item.type || (item.category === 'video' ? 'video/mp4' : 'image/jpeg');
    const category: VaultItemCategory = item.category || detectCategory(name, mimeType);

    const record: EncryptedVaultItem = {
      id,
      timestamp: item.timestamp || Date.now(),
      name,
      mimeType,
      size: item.size || dataBuffer.byteLength,
      iv,
      data: dataBuffer,
      album: item.album || item.folder || item.subfolder || 'General',
      isFavorite: !!item.isFavorite,
      category,
    };

    pendingBatch.push(record);
    count++;
  };

  while (offset < totalBytes || cursor < buffer.length) {
    // Read next slice into buffer if needed
    if (offset < totalBytes && buffer.length - cursor < CHUNK_SIZE) {
      const nextOffset = Math.min(offset + CHUNK_SIZE, totalBytes);
      const slice = blob.slice(offset, nextOffset);
      const chunkText = decoder.decode(await slice.arrayBuffer(), { stream: nextOffset < totalBytes });
      offset = nextOffset;
      buffer += chunkText;
    }

    if (!inArray) {
      const arrayKeyMatches = [
        buffer.indexOf('"items":'),
        buffer.indexOf('"photos":'),
        buffer.indexOf('"files":'),
        buffer.indexOf('"archivos":'),
        buffer.indexOf('"data":'),
        buffer.indexOf('"records":'),
        buffer.indexOf('"media":'),
        buffer.indexOf('"elements":'),
        buffer.indexOf('"elementos":'),
      ].filter((idx) => idx !== -1);

      if (arrayKeyMatches.length > 0) {
        const earliestKeyIdx = Math.min(...arrayKeyMatches);
        const arrIdx = buffer.indexOf('[', earliestKeyIdx);
        if (arrIdx !== -1) {
          buffer = buffer.substring(arrIdx + 1);
          cursor = 0;
          inArray = true;
        } else {
          if (offset >= totalBytes) break;
          cursor = buffer.length;
        }
      } else {
        const arrIdx = buffer.indexOf('[');
        if (arrIdx !== -1 && !buffer.slice(0, arrIdx).includes('{')) {
          buffer = buffer.substring(arrIdx + 1);
          cursor = 0;
          inArray = true;
        } else {
          if (offset >= totalBytes) break;
          cursor = buffer.length;
        }
      }
    }

    if (inArray) {
      while (cursor < buffer.length) {
        const c = buffer[cursor];
        if (escape) {
          escape = false;
          cursor++;
          continue;
        }
        if (c === '\\') {
          escape = true;
          cursor++;
          continue;
        }
        if (c === '"') {
          inString = !inString;
          cursor++;
          continue;
        }
        if (!inString) {
          if (c === '{') {
            if (depth === 0) itemStart = cursor;
            depth++;
          } else if (c === '}') {
            depth--;
            if (depth === 0 && itemStart !== -1) {
              const itemJson = buffer.substring(itemStart, cursor + 1);
              try {
                const item = JSON.parse(itemJson);
                parseAndQueueItem(item);

                if (pendingBatch.length >= 10) {
                  await saveItemsBatch(db, pendingBatch);
                  pendingBatch = [];
                }
              } catch (parseErr) {
                console.warn('Skipping malformed item in stream:', parseErr);
              }

              // Discard parsed object from buffer immediately to free memory
              buffer = buffer.substring(cursor + 1);
              cursor = 0;
              itemStart = -1;
              continue;
            }
          } else if (c === ']' && depth === 0) {
            // End of items array reached
            buffer = '';
            offset = totalBytes;
            break;
          }
        }
        cursor++;
      }
    }

    if (onProgress) {
      onProgress(offset, totalBytes, count);
    }

    // If offset has reached totalBytes and no item is currently being read, we can finish
    if (offset >= totalBytes && itemStart === -1 && (!inArray || cursor >= buffer.length)) {
      break;
    }

    // Cooperative yield to keep UI responsive
    await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 0));
  }

  // Save any remaining items in pendingBatch
  if (pendingBatch.length > 0) {
    await saveItemsBatch(db, pendingBatch);
    pendingBatch = [];
  }

  // Fallback for smaller files or unconventional JSON layout if stream found 0 items
  if (count === 0 && blob.size < 30 * 1024 * 1024) {
    try {
      const fullText = await blob.text();
      const parsed = parseWithAutoRepair(fullText);
      const itemsList =
        (Array.isArray(parsed) && parsed) ||
        (Array.isArray(parsed.items) && parsed.items) ||
        (Array.isArray(parsed.photos) && parsed.photos) ||
        (Array.isArray(parsed.files) && parsed.files) ||
        (Array.isArray(parsed.archivos) && parsed.archivos) ||
        (Array.isArray(parsed.data) && parsed.data) ||
        (Array.isArray(parsed.records) && parsed.records) ||
        (Array.isArray(parsed.media) && parsed.media) ||
        [];

      if (itemsList.length > 0) {
        for (const it of itemsList) {
          parseAndQueueItem(it);
          if (pendingBatch.length >= 10) {
            await saveItemsBatch(db, pendingBatch);
            pendingBatch = [];
          }
        }
        if (pendingBatch.length > 0) {
          await saveItemsBatch(db, pendingBatch);
          pendingBatch = [];
        }
      }
    } catch (fbErr) {
      console.warn('Fallback full parse attempt error:', fbErr);
    }
  }

  if (onProgress) {
    onProgress(totalBytes, totalBytes, count);
  }

  return {
    importedCount: count,
    saltHex: header.saltHex,
    testIvHex: header.testIvHex,
    testCipherHex: header.testCipherHex,
  };
}

/**
 * Restores items and optional cryptographic tokens from a backup file (compatibility wrapper)
 */
export async function importEncryptedBackup(
  jsonContent: string,
  onProgress?: (current: number, total: number) => void,
  overwriteCryptographicTokens: boolean = false
): Promise<{ importedCount: number }> {
  const blob = new Blob([jsonContent], { type: 'application/json' });
  return importEncryptedBackupBlobStream(
    blob,
    (loaded, total, count) => {
      if (onProgress) onProgress(count, count);
    },
    overwriteCryptographicTokens
  );
}

const formatFileSize = (bytes: number): string => {
  if (!bytes || bytes <= 0) return '0 B';
  const k = 1024;
  const sizes = ['B', 'KB', 'MB', 'GB'];
  const i = Math.floor(Math.log(bytes) / Math.log(k));
  return `${parseFloat((bytes / Math.pow(k, i)).toFixed(1))} ${sizes[i]}`;
};

interface SettingsVaultViewProps {
  masterKey: CryptoKey | null;
  sessionPin?: string | null;
  onMasterKeyUpdated: (newKey: CryptoKey) => void;
  autoLockMinutes: number;
  onAutoLockChange: (mins: number) => void;

  onLockOnTabSwitchChange: (enabled: boolean) => void;
  onPhotosUpdated: () => void;
  onResetVault: () => void;
  showToast: (msg: string, type?: 'info' | 'success' | 'alert' | 'error') => void;
}

export const SettingsVaultView: React.FC<SettingsVaultViewProps> = ({
  masterKey,
  sessionPin,

      sessionStorage.removeItem('open_updates_tab_on_settings');
      return 'updates';

    if (sessionStorage.getItem('open_backup_tab_on_settings') === 'true') {
      sessionStorage.removeItem('open_backup_tab_on_settings');
      return 'backup';
    }
    if (sessionStorage.getItem('open_trash_tab_on_settings') === 'true') {
      sessionStorage.removeItem('open_trash_tab_on_settings');
      return 'trash';
    }
    return null;
  });

  // Theme state
  const [currentTheme, setCurrentTheme] = useState<VaultTheme>(() => themeService.getTheme());
  useEffect(() => {
    return themeService.subscribe((t) => {
      setCurrentTheme(t);
    });
  }, []);

  const handleSelectTheme = (theme: VaultTheme) => {
    themeService.setTheme(theme);
    showToast(`Tema "${VAULT_THEMES[theme].name}" activado`, 'success');
  };

  const handleToggleFlipToLock = (enabled: boolean) => {
    setFlipToLockEnabled(enabled);
    flipToLockService.setEnabled(enabled);
    showToast(
      enabled
        ? 'Bloqueo al voltear boca abajo activado 🔄'
        : 'Bloqueo al voltear desactivado',

    );
  };

  };

  // Trash / Papelera de reciclaje state

  const [trashStats, setTrashStats] = useState<{ count: number; totalBytes: number }>({ count: 0, totalBytes: 0 });
  const [isLoadingTrash, setIsLoadingTrash] = useState(false);
  const [selectedTrashIds, setSelectedTrashIds] = useState<Set<string>>(new Set());
  const [showEmptyTrashModal, setShowEmptyTrashModal] = useState(false);
  const [isEmptyingTrash, setIsEmptyingTrash] = useState(false);
  const [itemToDeleteDefinitively, setItemToDeleteDefinitively] = useState<EncryptedVaultItem | null>(null);

  const loadTrash = async () => {
    setIsLoadingTrash(true);
    try {
      const items = await getTrashItems();
      setTrashItems(items);
      const stats = await getTrashStats();
      setTrashStats(stats);
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Error loading trash items:', err);
    } finally {
      setIsLoadingTrash(false);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    loadTrash();
    const handleTrashUpdated = () => {
      loadTrash();
    };
    window.addEventListener('vault_trash_updated', handleTrashUpdated);
    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener('vault_trash_updated', handleTrashUpdated);
    };
  }, []);

  const handleRestoreItem = async (id: string) => {
    try {
      await restoreFromTrash(id);
      showToast('Elemento restaurado a la bóveda', 'success');

      await onPhotosUpdated();
    } catch {
      showToast('Error al restaurar elemento', 'error');

      showToast('Error al restaurar elementos', 'error');

      setShowEmptyTrashModal(false);

      showToast('Error al vaciar la papelera', 'error');
    } finally {
      setIsEmptyingTrash(false);
    }
  };

      await deleteEncryptedItem(itemToDeleteDefinitively.id);

      await loadTrash();

      showToast('Error al eliminar elemento', 'error');
    }

    try {
      const ids = Array.from(selectedTrashIds);
      await deleteMultipleEncryptedItems(ids);
      showToast(`${ids.length} elementos eliminados definitivamente`, 'success');

      await loadTrash();
      await onPhotosUpdated();

      showToast('Error al eliminar elementos', 'error');

  // Gesto táctil de deslizamiento (swipe right) para retroceder
  const touchStartXRef = useRef<number | null>(null);
  const touchStartYRef = useRef<number | null>(null);

  const handleTouchStart = (e: React.TouchEvent) => {
    touchStartXRef.current = e.touches[0].clientX;
    touchStartYRef.current = e.touches[0].clientY;
  };

  const handleTouchEnd = (e: React.TouchEvent) => {
    if (touchStartXRef.current === null || touchStartYRef.current === null) return;
    const deltaX = e.changedTouches[0].clientX - touchStartXRef.current;
    const deltaY = e.changedTouches[0].clientY - touchStartYRef.current;
    touchStartXRef.current = null;
    touchStartYRef.current = null;

    // Deslizar de izquierda a derecha (swipe horizontal > 60px con baja desviación vertical)
    if (deltaX > 60 && Math.abs(deltaY) < 70) {
      if (activeSubMenu !== null) {
        setActiveSubMenu(null);
      } else {
        triggerBackAction();
      }
    }
  };

  // Track backup timestamp

    const handleSwitchSubmenu = (e: any) => {

    window.addEventListener('nullvault_switch_settings_backup', handleCheckBackupTab);
    window.addEventListener('vault_open_updates_menu', handleCheckUpdatesTab);

    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener('nullvault_switch_settings_backup', handleCheckBackupTab);

      setUpdateCheckResult(result);

        showToast('Tu aplicación ya está actualizada con la versión más reciente.', 'success');
      }

      showToast('No se pudo verificar la actualización. Revisa tu conexión.', 'alert');
    } finally {
      setIsCheckingUpdate(false);
    }
  };

  const handleToggleAutoCheck = (enabled: boolean) => {
    setAutoCheckUpdates(enabled);
    updateService.setAutoCheckEnabled(enabled);
    showToast(enabled ? 'Avisos automáticos de actualización activados' : 'Avisos automáticos desactivados', 'info');
  };

  // Change PIN state
  const [currentPin, setCurrentPin] = useState('');
  const [newPin, setNewPin] = useState('');
  const [confirmNewPin, setConfirmNewPin] = useState('');
  const [showCurrentPin, setShowCurrentPin] = useState(false);
  const [showNewPin, setShowNewPin] = useState(false);
  const [showConfirmNewPin, setShowConfirmNewPin] = useState(false);
  const [isChangingPin, setIsChangingPin] = useState(false);
  const [pinChangeError, setPinChangeError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [pinChangeSuccess, setPinChangeSuccess] = useState(false);

  // Backup / restore state
  const [isExporting, setIsExporting] = useState(false);
  const [exportProgress, setExportProgress] = useState<{ current: number; total: number } | null>(null);
  const [isImporting, setIsImporting] = useState(false);
  const [importProgress, setImportProgress] = useState<{ current: number; total: number } | null>(null);
  const [selectedBackupInfo, setSelectedBackupInfo] = useState<{
    name: string;
    size: number;
    status: 'reading' | 'analyzing' | 'restoring' | 'success' | 'error';
    statusText: string;
    percent: number;
  } | null>(null);
  const [pendingBackupBlob, setPendingBackupBlob] = useState<Blob | null>(null);
  const [pendingBackupHeader, setPendingBackupHeader] = useState<BackupHeaderInfo | null>(null);
  const [showBackupPinPrompt, setShowBackupPinPrompt] = useState(false);
  const [backupPinInput, setBackupPinInput] = useState('');
  const [backupPinError, setBackupPinError] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [isVerifyingBackupPin, setIsVerifyingBackupPin] = useState(false);
  const [showPasteInput, setShowPasteInput] = useState(false);
  const [pastedBackupText, setPastedBackupText] = useState('');
  const [restartPromptInfo, setRestartPromptInfo] = useState<{
    show: boolean;
    importedCount: number;
  }>({ show: false, importedCount: 0 });

  // Back button registrations para retroceder con teclas de navegación o botón físico
  useEffect(() => {
    if (showWipePinModal) {
      return registerBackHandler('settings_wipe_pin', () => {
        setShowWipePinModal(false);
        setWipePinInput('');
        setWipePinError(null);
        return true;
      }, 80);
    }
  }, [showWipePinModal]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (showWipeConfirmModal) {
      return registerBackHandler('settings_wipe_confirm', () => {
        setShowWipeConfirmModal(false);
        return true;
      }, 75);
    }
  }, [showWipeConfirmModal]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (showBackupPinPrompt) {
      return registerBackHandler('settings_backup_pin', () => {
        setShowBackupPinPrompt(false);
        setPendingBackupBlob(null);
        setPendingBackupHeader(null);
        return true;
      }, 75);
    }
  }, [showBackupPinPrompt]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (showPasteInput) {
      return registerBackHandler('settings_paste_input', () => {
        setShowPasteInput(false);
        return true;
      }, 75);
    }
  }, [showPasteInput]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (showEmptyTrashModal) {
      return registerBackHandler('settings_empty_trash', () => {
        setShowEmptyTrashModal(false);
        return true;
      }, 75);
    }
  }, [showEmptyTrashModal]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (itemToDeleteDefinitively) {
      return registerBackHandler('settings_delete_trash_item', () => {
        setItemToDeleteDefinitively(null);
        return true;
      }, 75);
    }
  }, [itemToDeleteDefinitively]);

  // Si está en un submenú, al presionar atrás regresa a la lista vertical de configuración
  useEffect(() => {
    if (activeSubMenu !== null) {
      try {
        window.history.pushState({ settingsSubMenu: activeSubMenu }, '');
      } catch {}
      return registerBackHandler('settings_submenu', () => {
        setActiveSubMenu(null);
        return true;
      }, 70);
    }
  }, [activeSubMenu]);

  const handleChangePin = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    setPinChangeError(null);
    setPinChangeSuccess(false);

    if (newPin.length < 4) {
      setPinChangeError('El nuevo PIN debe tener al menos 4 dígitos');
      return;
    }

    if (newPin !== confirmNewPin) {
      setPinChangeError('Los nuevos PINs no coinciden');
      return;
    }

    setIsChangingPin(true);

    try {
      const saltHex = localStorage.getItem('vault_salt');
      const testIvHex = localStorage.getItem('vault_test_iv');
      const testCipherHex = localStorage.getItem('vault_test_cipher');

      if (!saltHex || !testIvHex || !testCipherHex) {
        throw new Error('Datos de verificación de bóveda no encontrados');
      }

      const salt = hexToBuf(saltHex);
      const testKey = await deriveKeyFromPin(currentPin, salt);
      const isValid = await verifyKey(testKey, testIvHex, testCipherHex);

      if (!isValid) {
        setPinChangeError('El PIN actual es incorrecto');
        setIsChangingPin(false);
        return;
      }

      const newSalt = generateSalt();
      const newDerivedKey = await deriveKeyFromPin(newPin, newSalt);
      const newVerification = await createVerificationPayload(newDerivedKey);

      // Re-encrypt all items in IndexedDB from testKey to newDerivedKey
      try {
        const allItems = await getAllEncryptedItems();
        const updatedItems: EncryptedVaultItem[] = [];
        for (const item of allItems) {
          try {
            const isUnencrypted = !item.iv || item.iv.length === 0 || checkIsUnencryptedMedia(item.data);
            const decrypted = isUnencrypted ? item.data : await decryptBuffer(item.data, item.iv, testKey);
            const reEncrypted = await encryptBuffer(decrypted, newDerivedKey);
            updatedItems.push({
              ...item,
              iv: reEncrypted.iv,
              data: reEncrypted.data,
            });
          } catch (itemErr) {
            console.warn(`Could not re-encrypt item ${item.id} during PIN change:`, itemErr);
            updatedItems.push(item);
          }
        }
        if (updatedItems.length > 0) {
          await saveEncryptedItems(updatedItems);
        }
      } catch (reErr) {
        console.warn('Error during batch item re-encryption on PIN change:', reErr);
      }

      localStorage.setItem('vault_salt', bufToHex(newSalt));
      localStorage.setItem('vault_test_iv', newVerification.ivHex);
      localStorage.setItem('vault_test_cipher', newVerification.cipherHex);
      localStorage.setItem('vault_pin_length', newPin.length.toString());

      try {
        sessionStorage.setItem('vault_session_pin', newPin);
      } catch {}

      onMasterKeyUpdated(newDerivedKey);

      setPinChangeSuccess(true);
      setCurrentPin('');
      setNewPin('');
      setConfirmNewPin('');
      showToast('PIN maestro actualizado con éxito', 'success');
    } catch (err: any) {
      console.error('Error changing PIN:', err);
      setPinChangeError(err?.message || 'Error al cambiar PIN');
    } finally {
      setIsChangingPin(false);
    }
  };

  const handleExportBackup = async () => {
    if (!masterKey) return;
    setIsExporting(true);
    setExportProgress({ current: 0, total: 1 });
    try {
      const backupBlob = await exportEncryptedBackupBlob((current, total) => {
        setExportProgress({ current, total });
      });

      const dateStr = new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0];
      const fileName = `Boveda_Respaldo_${dateStr}.json`;

      let sharedSuccessfully = false;
      try {
        const backupFile = new File([backupBlob], fileName, { type: 'application/json' });
        if (navigator.canShare && navigator.canShare({ files: [backupFile] })) {
          await navigator.share({
            files: [backupFile],
            title: fileName,
            text: 'Copia de seguridad cifrada de Mi Bóveda',
          });
          sharedSuccessfully = true;
        }
      } catch (shareErr) {
        console.log('Mobile share dismissed or unavailable:', shareErr);
      }

      if (!sharedSuccessfully) {
        const url = URL.createObjectURL(backupBlob);
        const a = document.createElement('a');
        a.href = url;
        a.download = fileName;
        document.body.appendChild(a);
        a.click();
        document.body.removeChild(a);

        // Retener URL activa durante 3 minutos para que Android Download Manager no falle
        setTimeout(() => {
          try {
            URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
          } catch {}
        }, 180000);
      }

      const now = Date.now();
      localStorage.setItem('nullvault_last_backup_timestamp', now.toString());
      localStorage.setItem('vault_last_backup_timestamp', now.toString());
      localStorage.setItem('nullvault_last_backup_date', new Date().toISOString());
      setLastBackupTimestamp(now);
      window.dispatchEvent(new Event('nullvault_backup_updated'));
      window.dispatchEvent(new Event('vault_backup_updated'));

      showToast('Copia de seguridad descargada con éxito', 'success');
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Export error:', err);
      showToast('Error al exportar copia de seguridad', 'error');
    } finally {
      setIsExporting(false);
      setExportProgress(null);
    }
  };

  const handleFileRestore = async (e: React.ChangeEvent<HTMLInputElement>) => {
    const file = e.target.files?.[0];
    if (!file) return;

    try {
      setSelectedBackupInfo({
        name: file.name,
        size: file.size,
        status: 'reading',
        statusText: 'Analizando archivo de respaldo...',
        percent: 15,
      });

      const header = await readBackupHeaderFromBlob(file);

      if (header?.saltHex && header?.testIvHex && header?.testCipherHex) {
        const salt = hexToBuf(header.saltHex);
        const testKey = await deriveKeyFromPin(sessionPin || '', salt);
        const isValid = await verifyKey(testKey, header.testIvHex, header.testCipherHex);

        if (isValid) {
          setIsImporting(true);
          setSelectedBackupInfo((prev) =>
            prev ? { ...prev, status: 'restoring', statusText: 'Restaurando elementos...', percent: 45 } : null
          );
          const result = await importEncryptedBackupBlobStream(
            file,
            (curr, tot) => {
              const p = tot > 0 ? Math.round((curr / tot) * 50) + 45 : 50;
              setSelectedBackupInfo((prev) => (prev ? { ...prev, percent: p } : null));
            },
            true
          );

          onMasterKeyUpdated(testKey);
          await onPhotosUpdated();
          setSelectedBackupInfo((prev) =>
            prev ? { ...prev, status: 'success', statusText: `¡Se restauraron ${result.importedCount} archivos!`, percent: 100 } : null
          );
          showToast(`Se restauraron ${result.importedCount} elementos a la bóveda`, 'success');
          setIsImporting(false);

          setRestartPromptInfo({
            show: true,
            importedCount: result.importedCount,
          });
          return;
        }

        setPendingBackupBlob(file);
        setPendingBackupHeader(header);
        setShowBackupPinPrompt(true);
        setBackupPinInput('');
        setBackupPinError(null);
        return;
      }

      // Legacy direct import
      setIsImporting(true);
      const result = await importEncryptedBackupBlobStream(file, undefined, true);
      await onPhotosUpdated();
      setSelectedBackupInfo((prev) =>
        prev ? { ...prev, status: 'success', statusText: `¡Se restauraron ${result.importedCount} archivos!`, percent: 100 } : null
      );
      showToast(`Se restauraron ${result.importedCount} elementos a la bóveda`, 'success');
      setRestartPromptInfo({
        show: true,
        importedCount: result.importedCount,
      });
    } catch (err: any) {
      setSelectedBackupInfo((prev) =>
        prev ? { ...prev, status: 'error', statusText: err?.message || 'Archivo dañado', percent: 100 } : null
      );
      showToast('Error al restaurar respaldo', 'error');
    } finally {
      setIsImporting(false);
      e.target.value = '';
    }
  };

  const handleConfirmBackupPin = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (
      !pendingBackupBlob ||
      !pendingBackupHeader?.saltHex ||
      !pendingBackupHeader?.testIvHex ||
      !pendingBackupHeader?.testCipherHex
    )
      return;
    setIsVerifyingBackupPin(true);
    setBackupPinError(null);

    try {
      const salt = hexToBuf(pendingBackupHeader.saltHex);
      const testKey = await deriveKeyFromPin(backupPinInput, salt);
      const isValid = await verifyKey(testKey, pendingBackupHeader.testIvHex, pendingBackupHeader.testCipherHex);

      if (!isValid) {
        setBackupPinError('PIN de la copia de seguridad incorrecto');
        setIsVerifyingBackupPin(false);
        return;
      }

      setShowBackupPinPrompt(false);
      setIsImporting(true);

      const result = await importEncryptedBackupBlobStream(pendingBackupBlob, undefined, true);
      onMasterKeyUpdated(testKey);
      try {
        sessionStorage.setItem('vault_session_pin', backupPinInput);
        localStorage.setItem('vault_pin_length', backupPinInput.length.toString());
      } catch {}

      await onPhotosUpdated();

      showToast(`Se restauraron ${result.importedCount} elementos a la bóveda`, 'success');
      setPendingBackupBlob(null);
      setPendingBackupHeader(null);
      setSelectedBackupInfo((prev) =>
        prev ? { ...prev, status: 'success', statusText: `¡Se restauraron ${result.importedCount} archivos!`, percent: 100 } : null
      );

      setRestartPromptInfo({
        show: true,
        importedCount: result.importedCount,
      });
    } catch (err: any) {
      setBackupPinError(err?.message || 'Error al restaurar');
    } finally {
      setIsVerifyingBackupPin(false);
      setIsImporting(false);
    }
  };

  const handlePasteRestore = async () => {
    if (!pastedBackupText.trim()) return;
    try {
      const parsed = parseWithAutoRepair(pastedBackupText);
      const blob = new Blob([JSON.stringify(parsed)], { type: 'application/json' });
      const header = await readBackupHeaderFromBlob(blob);

      if (header?.saltHex && header?.testIvHex && header?.testCipherHex) {
        const salt = hexToBuf(header.saltHex);
        const testKey = await deriveKeyFromPin(sessionPin || '', salt);
        const isValid = await verifyKey(testKey, header.testIvHex, header.testCipherHex);

        if (isValid) {
          setIsImporting(true);
          const result = await importEncryptedBackupBlobStream(blob, undefined, true);
          onMasterKeyUpdated(testKey);
          await onPhotosUpdated();
          showToast(`Se restauraron ${result.importedCount} elementos`, 'success');
          setPastedBackupText('');
          setShowPasteInput(false);
          setIsImporting(false);
          setRestartPromptInfo({
            show: true,
            importedCount: result.importedCount,
          });
          return;
        }

        setPendingBackupBlob(blob);
        setPendingBackupHeader(header);
        setShowBackupPinPrompt(true);
        setBackupPinInput('');
        setBackupPinError(null);
      } else {
        setIsImporting(true);
        const result = await importEncryptedBackupBlobStream(blob, undefined, true);
        await onPhotosUpdated();
        showToast(`Se restauraron ${result.importedCount} elementos`, 'success');
        setPastedBackupText('');
        setShowPasteInput(false);
        setIsImporting(false);
        setRestartPromptInfo({
          show: true,
          importedCount: result.importedCount,
        });
      }
    } catch (err: any) {
      showToast(`Error: ${err?.message || 'Texto inválido'}`, 'error');
    }
  };

  const startWipeExecutionWithProgress = () => {
    setShowWipeConfirmModal(false);
    setShowWipePinModal(false);
    setIsWipingData(true);
    setWipeProgress(5);
    setWipeStepText('Iniciando protocolo de borrado seguro...');

    const steps = [
      { progress: 20, text: 'Destruyendo claves maestras y derivaciones AES-256...' },
      { progress: 45, text: 'Eliminando fotos, videos y notas cifradas...' },
      { progress: 70, text: 'Vaciando base de datos IndexedDB y miniaturas...' },
      { progress: 90, text: 'Limpiando credenciales biométricas, registros y logs...' },
      { progress: 100, text: '¡100% de borrado completado con éxito!' },
    ];

    let currentStep = 0;
    const interval = setInterval(async () => {
      if (currentStep < steps.length) {
        setWipeProgress(steps[currentStep].progress);
        setWipeStepText(steps[currentStep].text);
        currentStep++;
      } else {
        clearInterval(interval);
        try {
          await clearVaultDatabase();
          try {
            sessionStorage.removeItem('vault_session_pin');
            sessionStorage.clear();
          } catch {}
          localStorage.removeItem('vault_salt');
          localStorage.removeItem('vault_test_iv');
          localStorage.removeItem('vault_test_cipher');
          localStorage.removeItem('vault_initialized');
          localStorage.removeItem('vault_pin_length');
          localStorage.removeItem('nullvault_access_logs');
          localStorage.removeItem('nullvault_intruder_photo');
          localStorage.removeItem('nullvault_failed_streak');
          localStorage.removeItem('nullvault_alert_streak_count');
          localStorage.removeItem('nullvault_last_failed_time');
          localStorage.removeItem('nullvault_has_unviewed_intruder');
          localStorage.removeItem('nullvault_last_backup_timestamp');
          localStorage.removeItem('nullvault_last_backup_date');
          localStorage.removeItem('vault_bio_credential_id');
          localStorage.removeItem('vault_bio_session_token');
        } catch (err) {
          console.error('Error during wipe:', err);
        }
        setTimeout(() => {
          setIsWipingData(false);
          onResetVault();
        }, 650);
      }
    }, 450);
  };

  const handleConfirmWipeRequest = async () => {
    setShowWipeConfirmModal(false);

    // Inmediatamente debe pedir autenticación:
    // Lanzar inmediatamente sensor de huellas si está configurado
    if (isBiometricsEnrolled()) {
      showToast('Toca el sensor de huella para autorizar el borrado', 'info');
      try {
        const bioSuccess = await authenticateWithBiometrics();
        if (bioSuccess) {
          startWipeExecutionWithProgress();
          return;
        }
      } catch (err) {
        console.warn('Biometrics prompt cancelled or failed, falling back to PIN:', err);
      }
    }

    // Si el celular no tiene sensor de huella o falló/canceló la huella: pedir PIN
    setWipePinInput('');
    setWipePinError(null);
    setShowWipePinModal(true);
  };

  const handleWipePinSubmit = async (e: React.FormEvent) => {
    e.preventDefault();
    if (wipePinInput.length < 4) {
      setWipePinError('Introduce tu PIN completo');
      return;
    }

    setIsVerifyingWipePin(true);
    setWipePinError(null);

    try {
      const saltHex = localStorage.getItem('vault_salt');
      const testIvHex = localStorage.getItem('vault_test_iv');
      const testCipherHex = localStorage.getItem('vault_test_cipher');

      if (saltHex && testIvHex && testCipherHex) {
        const salt = hexToBuf(saltHex);
        const derivedKey = await deriveKeyFromPin(wipePinInput, salt);
        const isValid = await verifyKey(derivedKey, testIvHex, testCipherHex);
        if (!isValid) {
          setWipePinError('PIN incorrecto. Inténtalo de nuevo.');
          setIsVerifyingWipePin(false);
          return;
        }
      } else if (sessionPin && wipePinInput !== sessionPin) {
        setWipePinError('PIN incorrecto. Inténtalo de nuevo.');
        setIsVerifyingWipePin(false);
        return;
      }

      setIsVerifyingWipePin(false);
      setShowWipePinModal(false);
      startWipeExecutionWithProgress();
    } catch (err) {
      console.error('Error verifying PIN for wipe:', err);
      setWipePinError('Error al validar el PIN');
      setIsVerifyingWipePin(false);
    }
  };

  return (
    <div className="w-full max-w-md mx-auto px-4 pb-28 animate-in fade-in duration-200 select-none box-border">
      {/* Fixed Pinned Header */}
      <header className="fixed top-0 left-0 right-0 z-30 bg-[#090d16]/95 backdrop-blur-xl border-b border-slate-800/80 safe-top-sticky pb-3 px-4 shadow-md shadow-black/40 transition-all">
        <div className="max-w-md mx-auto w-full">
          {activeSubMenu === null ? (
            <div>
              <h1 className="text-2xl sm:text-3xl font-extrabold tracking-tight text-white mb-0.5">
                Configu<span className="text-[#00e5ff]">ración</span>
              </h1>
              <p className="text-[10px] font-semibold text-slate-400 tracking-[0.2em] uppercase">
                AJUSTES Y PREFERENCIAS DE BÓVEDA
              </p>
            </div>
          ) : (
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setActiveSubMenu(null)}
                className="p-2 -ml-2 rounded-2xl bg-slate-800/60 hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-300 hover:text-white transition-colors cursor-pointer flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 active:scale-95"
                title="Volver a Configuración"
                aria-label="Volver"
              >
                <ArrowLeft className="w-5 h-5 text-[#00e5ff]" />
              </button>
              <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                <h1 className="text-xl font-bold tracking-tight text-white truncate mb-0.5">
                  {activeSubMenu === 'security'
                    ? 'Seguridad y PIN'
                    : activeSubMenu === 'backup'
                    ? 'Copia de Seguridad'
                    : activeSubMenu === 'updates'
                    ? 'Actualizaciones del Sistema'
                    : activeSubMenu === 'trash'
                    ? 'Papelera de Reciclaje'
                    : activeSubMenu === 'theme'
                    ? 'Temas y Apariencia'
                    : 'Restablecer Bóveda'}
                </h1>
                <p className="text-[10px] font-semibold text-slate-400 tracking-wider uppercase truncate">
                  {activeSubMenu === 'security'
                    ? 'Políticas de acceso y credenciales'
                    : activeSubMenu === 'backup'
                    ? 'Respaldo y restauración cifrada'
                    : activeSubMenu === 'updates'
                    ? 'Búsqueda de novedades y versión actual'
                    : activeSubMenu === 'trash'
                    ? 'Auto-vaciado a 30 días y borrado definitivo'
                    : activeSubMenu === 'theme'
                    ? 'Paletas Neón y Negro Puro AMOLED'
                    : 'Zona de peligro'}
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>
          )}
        </div>
      </header>

      {/* Spacer so content starts right below fixed header */}
      <div className="safe-top-header-spacer shrink-0 w-full mb-3" />

      {/* Menú Principal Vertical (Cuando activeSubMenu es null) */}
      {activeSubMenu === null && (
        <div className="space-y-2.5 animate-in fade-in duration-200">
          {/* Tarjeta 1: Seguridad y PIN */}
          <div
            onClick={() => setActiveSubMenu('security')}
            className="bg-[#0a121d] border border-slate-800/90 hover:border-cyan-500/40 rounded-2xl p-3 sm:p-3.5 shadow-lg flex items-center justify-between gap-3 cursor-pointer group active:scale-[0.99] transition-all"
          >
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3 min-w-0">
              <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-xl bg-[#00e5ff]/10 border border-[#00e5ff]/20 flex items-center justify-center text-[#00e5ff] shrink-0 group-hover:scale-105 transition-transform">
                <KeyRound className="w-4.5 h-4.5" />
              </div>
              <div className="min-w-0">
                <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-white group-hover:text-[#00e5ff] transition-colors truncate">
                  Seguridad y PIN
                </h3>
                <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 truncate mt-0.5">
                  Cambiar PIN, bloqueo al voltear 🔄 y timeout
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div className="shrink-0 flex items-center justify-center w-6 h-6 rounded-full bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 text-slate-500 group-hover:text-[#00e5ff] group-hover:border-[#00e5ff]/40 transition-colors">
              <ChevronRight className="w-3.5 h-3.5 group-hover:translate-x-0.5 transition-transform" />
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Tarjeta 2: Copia de Seguridad */}
          <div
            onClick={() => setActiveSubMenu('backup')}
            className="bg-[#0a121d] border border-slate-800/90 hover:border-emerald-500/40 rounded-2xl p-3 sm:p-3.5 shadow-lg flex items-center justify-between gap-3 cursor-pointer group active:scale-[0.99] transition-all"
          >
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3 min-w-0">
              <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-xl bg-emerald-500/10 border border-emerald-500/20 flex items-center justify-center text-emerald-400 shrink-0 group-hover:scale-105 transition-transform">
                <Download className="w-4.5 h-4.5" />
              </div>
              <div className="min-w-0">
                <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-white group-hover:text-emerald-400 transition-colors truncate">
                  Copia de Seguridad
                </h3>
                <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 truncate mt-0.5">
                  Exportar respaldo cifrado y restaurar datos
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div className="shrink-0 flex items-center justify-center w-6 h-6 rounded-full bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 text-slate-500 group-hover:text-emerald-400 group-hover:border-emerald-500/40 transition-colors">
              <ChevronRight className="w-3.5 h-3.5 group-hover:translate-x-0.5 transition-transform" />
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Tarjeta 3: Papelera de Reciclaje Cifrada (30 días) */}
          <div
            onClick={() => setActiveSubMenu('trash')}
            className="bg-[#0a121d] border border-slate-800/90 hover:border-amber-500/40 rounded-2xl p-3 sm:p-3.5 shadow-lg flex items-center justify-between gap-3 cursor-pointer group active:scale-[0.99] transition-all"
          >
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3 min-w-0">
              <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-xl bg-amber-500/10 border border-amber-500/20 flex items-center justify-center text-amber-400 shrink-0 group-hover:scale-105 transition-transform">
                <Trash2 className="w-4.5 h-4.5" />
              </div>
              <div className="min-w-0">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-white group-hover:text-amber-400 transition-colors truncate">
                    Papelera de Reciclaje Cifrada
                  </h3>
                  {trashStats.count > 0 && (
                    <span className="text-[9px] font-bold px-1.5 py-0.2 rounded-full bg-amber-500/20 text-amber-300 border border-amber-500/30">
                      {trashStats.count} {trashStats.count === 1 ? 'arch.' : 'archs.'}
                    </span>
                  )}
                </div>
                <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 truncate mt-0.5">
                  Auto-vaciado a 30 días o eliminación definitiva
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div className="shrink-0 flex items-center justify-center w-6 h-6 rounded-full bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 text-slate-500 group-hover:text-amber-400 group-hover:border-amber-500/40 transition-colors">
              <ChevronRight className="w-3.5 h-3.5 group-hover:translate-x-0.5 transition-transform" />
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Tarjeta 4: Temas y Apariencia Visual */}
          <div
            onClick={() => setActiveSubMenu('theme')}
            className="bg-[#0a121d] border border-slate-800/90 hover:border-purple-500/40 rounded-2xl p-3 sm:p-3.5 shadow-lg flex items-center justify-between gap-3 cursor-pointer group active:scale-[0.99] transition-all"
          >
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3 min-w-0">
              <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-xl bg-purple-500/10 border border-purple-500/20 flex items-center justify-center text-purple-400 shrink-0 group-hover:scale-105 transition-transform">
                <Palette className="w-4.5 h-4.5" />
              </div>
              <div className="min-w-0">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-white group-hover:text-purple-400 transition-colors truncate">
                    Temas y Apariencia Visual
                  </h3>
                  <span className="text-[9px] font-semibold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-purple-500/15 text-purple-300 border border-purple-500/30">
                    {VAULT_THEMES[currentTheme]?.name || 'Cian Neón'}
                  </span>
                </div>
                <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 truncate mt-0.5">
                  Cian Neón, AMOLED puro, Esmeralda, Violeta y Claro
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div className="shrink-0 flex items-center justify-center w-6 h-6 rounded-full bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 text-slate-500 group-hover:text-purple-400 group-hover:border-purple-500/40 transition-colors">
              <ChevronRight className="w-3.5 h-3.5 group-hover:translate-x-0.5 transition-transform" />
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Tarjeta 5: Actualizaciones del Sistema */}
          <div
            onClick={() => setActiveSubMenu('updates')}
            className="bg-[#0a121d] border border-slate-800/90 hover:border-cyan-500/40 rounded-2xl p-3 sm:p-3.5 shadow-lg flex items-center justify-between gap-3 cursor-pointer group active:scale-[0.99] transition-all"
          >
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3 min-w-0">
              <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-xl bg-cyan-500/10 border border-cyan-500/20 flex items-center justify-center text-[#00e5ff] shrink-0 group-hover:scale-105 transition-transform">
                <RefreshCw className="w-4.5 h-4.5" />
              </div>
              <div className="min-w-0">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-white group-hover:text-[#00e5ff] transition-colors truncate">
                    Actualizaciones del Sistema
                  </h3>
                  {updateCheckResult?.hasUpdate && (
                    <span className="text-[9px] font-bold uppercase tracking-wider px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full bg-cyan-500/20 text-[#00e5ff] border border-cyan-500/40 animate-pulse">
                      Nueva versión
                    </span>
                  )}
                </div>
                <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 truncate mt-0.5">
                  Buscar actualizaciones, versión v{CURRENT_APP_VERSION} y novedades
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div className="shrink-0 flex items-center justify-center w-6 h-6 rounded-full bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 text-slate-500 group-hover:text-[#00e5ff] group-hover:border-[#00e5ff]/40 transition-colors">
              <ChevronRight className="w-3.5 h-3.5 group-hover:translate-x-0.5 transition-transform" />
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Tarjeta 6: Restablecer Bóveda (Zona de Peligro) */}
          <div
            onClick={() => setActiveSubMenu('danger')}
            className="bg-[#0a121d] border border-rose-950/60 hover:border-rose-500/50 rounded-2xl p-3 sm:p-3.5 shadow-lg flex items-center justify-between gap-3 cursor-pointer group active:scale-[0.99] transition-all"
          >
            <div className="flex items-center gap-3 min-w-0">
              <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-xl bg-rose-500/10 border border-rose-500/30 flex items-center justify-center text-rose-400 shrink-0 group-hover:scale-105 transition-transform">
                <ShieldAlert className="w-4.5 h-4.5" />
              </div>
              <div className="min-w-0">
                <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-white group-hover:text-rose-400 transition-colors truncate">
                  Restablecer Bóveda
                </h3>
                <p className="text-[11px] text-rose-300/80 truncate mt-0.5">
                  Zona de peligro: borrado seguro de datos
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>
            <div className="shrink-0 flex items-center justify-center w-6 h-6 rounded-full bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 text-slate-500 group-hover:text-rose-400 group-hover:border-rose-500/40 transition-colors">
              <ChevronRight className="w-3.5 h-3.5 group-hover:translate-x-0.5 transition-transform" />
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Contenido de Sub-menús con soporte táctil de deslizar para volver */}
      {activeSubMenu !== null && (
        <div
          onTouchStart={handleTouchStart}
          onTouchEnd={handleTouchEnd}
          className="space-y-4 text-slate-300 text-sm animate-in fade-in slide-in-from-right-3 duration-200"
        >
          {activeSubMenu === 'security' && (
          <div className="space-y-6">
            {/* Auto-lock Settings */}
            <div>
              <h4 className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-wider mb-3">
                Políticas de Bloqueo
              </h4>
              <div className="space-y-3 bg-[#0a121d] p-4 rounded-3xl border border-slate-800/90 shadow-xl">
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
                    <Clock className="w-4 h-4 text-[#00e5ff]" />
                    <div>
                      <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-200">Auto-bloqueo por inactividad</p>
                      <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400">Bloquea la bóveda si no hay interacción</p>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <select
                    value={autoLockMinutes}
                    onChange={(e) => onAutoLockChange(Number(e.target.value))}
                    className="bg-slate-800 border border-slate-700 text-slate-200 text-xs rounded-xl px-3 py-1.5 focus:outline-none focus:border-[#00e5ff]"
                  >
                    <option value={0.5}>30 segundos</option>
                    <option value={1}>1 minuto</option>
                    <option value={2}>2 minutos</option>
                    <option value={5}>5 minutos</option>
                    <option value={10}>10 minutos</option>
                    <option value={0}>Desactivado</option>
                  </select>
                </div>

                <div className="border-t border-slate-800/80 pt-3 flex items-center justify-between">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
                    <EyeOff className="w-4 h-4 text-[#00e5ff]" />
                    <div>
                      <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-200">Bloquear al cambiar de pestaña</p>
                      <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400">Protege tu pantalla si sales del navegador o minimizas</p>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <NeonSwitch
                    checked={lockOnTabSwitch}
                    onChange={onLockOnTabSwitchChange}
                    ariaLabel="Bloquear al cambiar de pestaña"
                  />
                </div>

                {/* Bloqueo al Voltear Boca Abajo (Flip to Lock / Face-Down) 🔄 */}
                <div className="border-t border-slate-800/80 pt-3 flex items-center justify-between">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5">
                    <RotateCcw className="w-4 h-4 text-[#00e5ff]" />
                    <div>
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                        <p className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-200">Bloqueo al Voltear Boca Abajo</p>
                        <span className="text-[9px] font-bold px-1.5 py-0.2 rounded-full bg-cyan-500/20 text-[#00e5ff] border border-cyan-500/30">
                          Face-Down 🔄
                        </span>
                      </div>
                      <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400">Bloquea la bóveda al colocar el móvil sobre la mesa o voltearlo</p>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <NeonSwitch
                    checked={flipToLockEnabled}
                    onChange={handleToggleFlipToLock}
