                    ariaLabel="Bloqueo al voltear boca abajo"
                  />
                </div>

                {flipToLockEnabled && (
                  <div className="pt-1 flex justify-end">
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={handleSimulateFlipToLock}
                      className="text-[11px] font-medium text-[#00e5ff] hover:text-cyan-300 flex items-center gap-1.5 px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg bg-[#00e5ff]/10 hover:bg-[#00e5ff]/20 border border-[#00e5ff]/25 active:scale-95 transition-all cursor-pointer"
                    >
                      <RotateCcw className="w-3 h-3" />
                      Probar volteo (Simular Face-Down)
                    </button>
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Change Master PIN Form */}
            <div>
              <h4 className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-wider mb-3">
                Cambiar PIN Maestro
              </h4>
              <form onSubmit={handleChangePin} className="space-y-3 bg-[#0a121d] p-4 rounded-3xl border border-slate-800/90 shadow-xl">
                <div>
                  <label className="block text-xs text-slate-400 mb-1">PIN Actual</label>
                  <div className="relative flex items-center">
                    <input
                      type={showCurrentPin ? 'text' : 'password'}
                      inputMode="numeric"
                      value={currentPin}
                      onChange={(e) => setCurrentPin(e.target.value)}
                      placeholder="••••"
                      required
                      className="w-full bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 pr-10 text-sm text-slate-100 focus:outline-none focus:border-[#00e5ff] font-mono"
                    />
                    <button
                      type="button"
                      onClick={() => setShowCurrentPin(!showCurrentPin)}
                      className="absolute right-2.5 text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-200 transition-colors p-1"
                      title={showCurrentPin ? 'Ocultar PIN' : 'Ver PIN'}
                    >
                      {showCurrentPin ? <EyeOff className="w-4 h-4" /> : <Eye className="w-4 h-4" />}
                    </button>
                  </div>
                </div>

                <div className="grid grid-cols-2 gap-2">
                  <div>
                    <label className="block text-xs text-slate-400 mb-1">Nuevo PIN</label>
                    <div className="relative flex items-center">
                      <input
                        type={showNewPin ? 'text' : 'password'}
                        inputMode="numeric"
                        value={newPin}
                        onChange={(e) => setNewPin(e.target.value)}
                        placeholder="••••"
                        required
                        className="w-full bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 pr-10 text-sm text-slate-100 focus:outline-none focus:border-[#00e5ff] font-mono"
                      />
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => setShowNewPin(!showNewPin)}
                        className="absolute right-2.5 text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-200 transition-colors p-1"
                        title={showNewPin ? 'Ocultar PIN' : 'Ver PIN'}
                      >
                        {showNewPin ? <EyeOff className="w-4 h-4" /> : <Eye className="w-4 h-4" />}
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <label className="block text-xs text-slate-400 mb-1">Confirmar Nuevo</label>
                    <div className="relative flex items-center">
                      <input
                        type={showConfirmNewPin ? 'text' : 'password'}
                        inputMode="numeric"
                        value={confirmNewPin}
                        onChange={(e) => setConfirmNewPin(e.target.value)}
                        placeholder="••••"
                        required
                        className="w-full bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl px-3 py-2 pr-10 text-sm text-slate-100 focus:outline-none focus:border-[#00e5ff] font-mono"
                      />
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        onClick={() => setShowConfirmNewPin(!showConfirmNewPin)}
                        className="absolute right-2.5 text-slate-400 hover:text-slate-200 transition-colors p-1"
                        title={showConfirmNewPin ? 'Ocultar PIN' : 'Ver PIN'}
                      >
                        {showConfirmNewPin ? <EyeOff className="w-4 h-4" /> : <Eye className="w-4 h-4" />}
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                </div>

                {pinChangeError && (
                  <div className="text-rose-400 text-xs bg-rose-500/10 p-2.5 rounded-xl border border-rose-500/20">
                    {pinChangeError}
                  </div>
                )}

                {pinChangeSuccess && (
                  <div className="text-emerald-400 text-xs bg-emerald-500/10 p-2.5 rounded-xl border border-emerald-500/20 flex items-center gap-1.5">
                    <Check className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                    <span>PIN actualizado con éxito.</span>
                  </div>
                )}

                <button
                  type="submit"
                  disabled={isChangingPin || !currentPin || !newPin || !confirmNewPin}
                  className="w-full py-2.5 bg-gradient-to-r from-cyan-500 to-blue-600 hover:from-cyan-400 hover:to-blue-500 disabled:opacity-40 text-slate-950 font-bold rounded-xl text-xs transition-all flex items-center justify-center gap-2 shadow-lg shadow-cyan-500/20"
                >
                  <KeyRound className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                  <span>{isChangingPin ? 'Re-cifrando bóveda...' : 'Actualizar PIN'}</span>
                </button>
              </form>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {activeSubMenu === 'backup' && (
          <div className="space-y-4">
            {/* Estado y Recordatorio de Copia de Seguridad */}
            {lastBackupTimestamp ? (
              (() => {
                const daysAgo = Math.floor((Date.now() - lastBackupTimestamp) / (24 * 60 * 60 * 1000));
                const isOverWeek = daysAgo >= 7;
                return (
                  <div
                    className={`p-3.5 rounded-3xl border flex items-center justify-between gap-3 shadow-md ${
                      isOverWeek
                        ? 'bg-amber-500/10 border-amber-500/30'
                        : 'bg-emerald-500/10 border-emerald-500/30'
                    }`}
                  >
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                      <div
                        className={`w-9 h-9 rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 ${
                          isOverWeek ? 'bg-amber-500/20 text-amber-400' : 'bg-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-400'
                        }`}
                      >
                        {isOverWeek ? (
                          <AlertTriangle className="w-5 h-5" />
                        ) : (
                          <CheckCircle2 className="w-5 h-5" />
                        )}
                      </div>
                      <div>
                        <p className="text-xs font-bold text-white">
                          {isOverWeek ? 'Recordatorio de Respaldo' : 'Copia de Seguridad al Día'}
                        </p>
                        <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-300">
                          {isOverWeek
                            ? `Llevas ${daysAgo} días sin realizar una copia de seguridad.`
                            : `Último respaldo realizado hace ${daysAgo === 0 ? 'unas horas' : `${daysAgo} día(s)`}.`}
                        </p>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                    <span
                      className={`text-[10px] font-mono px-2 py-0.5 rounded-full ${
                        isOverWeek ? 'bg-amber-500/20 text-amber-300' : 'bg-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-300'
                      }`}
                    >
                      {daysAgo}d
                    </span>
                  </div>
                );
              })()
            ) : (
              <div className="p-3.5 rounded-3xl border bg-amber-500/10 border-amber-500/30 flex items-center gap-3 shadow-md">
                <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-2xl bg-amber-500/20 text-amber-400 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
                  <AlertTriangle className="w-5 h-5" />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <p className="text-xs font-bold text-white">Sin Copia de Seguridad Registrada</p>
                  <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-300">
                    Aún no has descargado un respaldo. Realiza una copia para proteger tus archivos ante cualquier imprevisto.
                  </p>
                </div>
              </div>
            )}

            <div className="bg-[#0a121d] p-4 rounded-3xl border border-slate-800/90 shadow-xl space-y-3">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                <div className="p-2.5 bg-[#00e5ff]/10 text-[#00e5ff] rounded-2xl border border-[#00e5ff]/20">
                  <Download className="w-5 h-5" />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <h5 className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-200">Exportar Copia Cifrada</h5>
                  <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400">
                    Descarga un archivo .json con todas tus fotos, videos y notas protegidas con cifrado AES-GCM.
                  </p>
                </div>
              </div>

              {isExporting && exportProgress && (
                <div className="space-y-1.5 pt-1">
                  <div className="flex justify-between text-[11px] text-[#00e5ff] font-semibold">
                    <span>Procesando y cifrando elementos...</span>
                    <span>{exportProgress.current} / {exportProgress.total}</span>
                  </div>
                  <div className="w-full h-2 bg-slate-800 rounded-full overflow-hidden">
                    <div
                      className="h-full bg-[#00e5ff] transition-all duration-200"
                      style={{
                        width: `${exportProgress.total > 0 ? Math.min(100, Math.round((exportProgress.current / exportProgress.total) * 100)) : 0}%`,
                      }}
                    />
                  </div>
                </div>
              )}

              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handleExportBackup}
                disabled={isExporting}
                className="w-full py-2.5 bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-100 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold transition-colors flex items-center justify-center gap-2 border border-slate-700"
              >
                <Download className="w-4 h-4 text-[#00e5ff]" />
                <span>{isExporting ? 'Generando copia...' : 'Descargar Copia de Seguridad (.json)'}</span>
              </button>
            </div>

            <div className="bg-[#0a121d] p-4 rounded-3xl border border-slate-800/90 shadow-xl space-y-3">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                <div className="p-2.5 bg-emerald-500/10 text-emerald-400 rounded-2xl border border-emerald-500/20">
                  <Upload className="w-5 h-5" />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <h5 className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-200">Restaurar Copia</h5>
                  <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400">
                    Importa un archivo .json generado previamente para restaurar archivos a tu bóveda.
                  </p>
                </div>
              </div>

              {selectedBackupInfo && (
                <div className="bg-[#101b2d] border border-slate-700/80 rounded-2xl p-4 space-y-3 shadow-lg animate-in fade-in">
                  <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-2.5 min-w-0">
                      <div className={`p-2 rounded-xl ${
                        selectedBackupInfo.status === 'success' ? 'bg-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-400' :
                        selectedBackupInfo.status === 'error' ? 'bg-rose-500/20 text-rose-400' :
                        'bg-cyan-500/20 text-cyan-400'
                      }`}>
                        {selectedBackupInfo.status === 'success' ? (
                          <CheckCircle2 className="w-5 h-5" />
                        ) : selectedBackupInfo.status === 'error' ? (
                          <AlertTriangle className="w-5 h-5" />
                        ) : (
                          <FileText className="w-5 h-5 animate-pulse" />
                        )}
                      </div>
                      <div className="min-w-0">
                        <p className="text-xs font-bold text-white truncate max-w-[190px] sm:max-w-xs">
                          {selectedBackupInfo.name}
                        </p>
                        <p className="text-[10px] text-slate-400">
                          {formatFileSize(selectedBackupInfo.size)}
                        </p>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                    <span className={`text-[10px] font-bold px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full ${
                      selectedBackupInfo.status === 'success' ? 'bg-emerald-500/20 text-emerald-300' :
                      selectedBackupInfo.status === 'error' ? 'bg-rose-500/20 text-rose-300' :
                      'bg-cyan-500/20 text-cyan-300'
                    }`}>
                      {selectedBackupInfo.status === 'success' ? 'Listo' :
                       selectedBackupInfo.status === 'error' ? 'Error' :
                       'Procesando'}
                    </span>
                  </div>

                  <p className="text-xs text-slate-300 font-medium">
                    {selectedBackupInfo.statusText}
                  </p>
                </div>
              )}

              <label className="w-full py-2.5 bg-gradient-to-r from-emerald-600 to-teal-600 hover:from-emerald-500 hover:to-teal-500 text-white rounded-xl text-xs font-bold transition-all flex items-center justify-center gap-2 cursor-pointer shadow-lg shadow-emerald-600/20">
                <Upload className="w-4 h-4" />
                <span>{isImporting ? 'Restaurando archivo...' : 'Seleccionar Archivo de Respaldo (.json)'}</span>
                <input
                  type="file"
                  accept=".json"
                  className="hidden"
                  disabled={isImporting}
                  onChange={handleFileRestore}
                />
              </label>

              {/* Pegar texto directo */}
              <div className="border-t border-slate-800/80 pt-3">
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                  <span className="text-[11px] text-slate-400">¿Problemas al abrir el archivo?</span>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setShowPasteInput(!showPasteInput)}
                    className="text-xs text-[#00e5ff] hover:underline font-medium"
                  >
                    {showPasteInput ? 'Ocultar' : 'Pegar texto directo'}
                  </button>
                </div>

                {showPasteInput && (
                  <div className="mt-2.5 space-y-2 animate-in fade-in">
                    <textarea
                      rows={3}
                      value={pastedBackupText}
                      onChange={(e) => setPastedBackupText(e.target.value)}
                      placeholder="Pega aquí el contenido JSON del archivo..."
                      className="w-full bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 rounded-xl p-2.5 text-xs text-slate-200 font-mono focus:outline-none focus:border-[#00e5ff] resize-none"
                    />
                    <div className="flex justify-end gap-2">
                      <button
                        type="button"
                        disabled={!pastedBackupText.trim() || isImporting}
                        onClick={handlePasteRestore}
                        className="px-4 py-1.5 bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-500 disabled:opacity-40 text-white rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold"
                      >
                        Restaurar desde texto
                      </button>
                    </div>
                  </div>
                )}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {activeSubMenu === 'danger' && (
          <div className="space-y-4">
            <div className="bg-[#0a121d] p-4 rounded-3xl border border-rose-500/30 shadow-xl space-y-3">
              <h5 className="text-xs font-semibold text-rose-300">Borrar Datos y Restablecer Bóveda</h5>
              <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 leading-relaxed">
                Borra de forma irreversible tu PIN maestro, claves criptográficas, fotos, videos y notas de este dispositivo. La aplicación volverá a la pantalla inicial de configuración.
              </p>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setShowWipeConfirmModal(true)}
                className="w-full py-2.5 bg-rose-600 hover:bg-rose-500 text-white rounded-xl text-xs font-bold shadow-lg shadow-rose-600/30 transition-colors flex items-center justify-center gap-2 cursor-pointer active:scale-[0.98]"
              >
                <Trash2 className="w-4 h-4" />
                <span>Borrar todos los datos y restablecer</span>
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {activeSubMenu === 'updates' && (
          <div className="space-y-4 animate-in fade-in duration-200">
            {/* Tarjeta Unificada de Actualizaciones del Sistema (Un solo botón principal) */}
            <BovedaUpdater onOpenChangelog={() => setShowChangelogModal(true)} />


            {/* Configuración de Notificaciones Automáticas */}
            <div className="bg-[#0a121d] p-4.5 rounded-3xl border border-slate-800 shadow-xl">
              <div className="flex items-center justify-between gap-3">
                <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                  <div className="w-9 h-9 rounded-xl bg-cyan-500/10 border border-cyan-500/20 flex items-center justify-center text-[#00e5ff] shrink-0">
                    <Bell className="w-4.5 h-4.5" />
                  </div>
                  <div>
                    <h5 className="text-xs font-bold text-white">Aviso automático al entrar</h5>
                    <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400">Muestra un mensaje si hay una nueva versión lista</p>
                  </div>
                </div>

                <NeonSwitch
                  checked={autoCheckUpdates}
                  onChange={handleToggleAutoCheck}
                  ariaLabel="Avisos automáticos de actualización"
                />
              </div>
            </div>
            {/* Consejo de seguridad */}
            <div className="bg-emerald-950/20 p-4 rounded-3xl border border-emerald-500/30 text-xs space-y-2">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-2 text-emerald-400 font-bold">
                <CheckCircle2 className="w-4 h-4 shrink-0" />
                <span>Consejo de seguridad permanente</span>
              </div>
              <p className="text-slate-300 leading-relaxed text-[11px]">
                Antes de cualquier actualización mayor del sistema operativo de tu teléfono, siempre puedes exportar un respaldo desde la pestaña <button type="button" onClick={() => setActiveSubMenu('backup')} className="text-emerald-300 underline font-bold cursor-pointer">Copia de Seguridad</button>.
              </p>
            </div>
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Submenú: Papelera de Reciclaje Cifrada (30 días) 🗑️ */}
        {activeSubMenu === 'trash' && (
          <div className="space-y-4 animate-in fade-in duration-200">
            {/* Tarjeta explicativa */}
            <div className="bg-[#0a121d] p-4 rounded-3xl border border-slate-800/90 shadow-xl space-y-3">
              <div className="flex items-start gap-3">
                <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-2xl bg-amber-500/10 border border-amber-500/25 flex items-center justify-center text-amber-400 shrink-0">
                  <Trash2 className="w-5 h-5" />
                </div>
                <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                  <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                    <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-white">Papelera Cifrada</h3>
                    <span className="text-[9px] font-bold px-1.5 py-0.2 rounded-full bg-amber-500/20 text-amber-300 border border-amber-500/30">
                      Auto-Vaciado 30d 🗑️
                    </span>
                  </div>
                  <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 mt-1 leading-relaxed">
                    Los archivos permanecen cifrados con tu clave <strong className="text-slate-300">AES-256-GCM</strong>.
                    Se auto-destruyen tras <strong className="text-amber-400 font-semibold">30 días</strong>, o puedes restaurarlos o eliminarlos definitivamente cuando desees.
                  </p>
                </div>
              </div>

              {/* Barra de estado y acción de vaciado */}
              <div className="flex flex-col sm:flex-row sm:items-center justify-between gap-3 pt-3 border-t border-slate-800/80">
                <div className="text-xs text-slate-400">
                  Total en papelera: <strong className="text-white font-bold">{trashStats.count}</strong> elemento(s)
                  {trashStats.totalBytes > 0 && ` • ${formatFileSize(trashStats.totalBytes)}`}
                </div>
                {trashItems.length > 0 && (
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => setShowEmptyTrashModal(true)}
                    className="self-start sm:self-auto px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl bg-rose-600/20 hover:bg-rose-600 text-rose-300 hover:text-white border border-rose-500/30 text-xs font-bold flex items-center gap-2 transition-all active:scale-95 cursor-pointer shadow-sm shadow-rose-900/30"
                  >
                    <Trash2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                    Vaciar Papelera Ahora
                  </button>
                )}
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Barra de acciones para elementos seleccionados */}
            {selectedTrashIds.size > 0 && (
              <div className="bg-[#0f172a] border border-[#00e5ff]/30 p-3 rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-between shadow-xl animate-in fade-in duration-150">
                <span className="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-200">
                  {selectedTrashIds.size} elemento(s) seleccionado(s)
                </span>
                <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={handleBatchRestore}
                    className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-emerald-500/20 hover:bg-emerald-500 text-emerald-300 hover:text-white border border-emerald-500/30 text-xs font-bold flex items-center gap-1.5 transition-all active:scale-95 cursor-pointer"
                  >
                    <Undo2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                    Restaurar
                  </button>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={handleBatchDeleteDefinitive}
                    className="px-3 py-1.5 rounded-xl bg-rose-600 hover:bg-rose-500 text-white text-xs font-bold flex items-center gap-1.5 transition-all active:scale-95 cursor-pointer shadow-md shadow-rose-600/30"
                  >
                    <Trash2 className="w-3.5 h-3.5" />
                    Eliminar
                  </button>
                </div>
              </div>
            )}

            {/* Lista de archivos en la papelera */}
            {trashItems.length === 0 ? (
              <div className="bg-[#0a121d] border border-slate-800/80 rounded-3xl p-8 text-center space-y-3">
                <div className="w-14 h-14 rounded-3xl bg-slate-800/60 border border-slate-700/60 flex items-center justify-center text-slate-500 mx-auto">
                  <Trash2 className="w-7 h-7" />
                </div>
                <h4 className="text-sm font-bold text-white">La papelera está vacía</h4>
                <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 max-w-xs mx-auto leading-relaxed">
                  Cuando borres fotos, videos o archivos de tu bóveda, se enviarán aquí con resguardo cifrado de 30 días antes de su destrucción definitiva.
                </p>
              </div>
            ) : (
              <div className="space-y-2">
                <div className="flex items-center justify-between px-1 text-xs text-slate-400">
                  <span>Archivos en espera ({trashItems.length})</span>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    onClick={() => {
                      if (selectedTrashIds.size === trashItems.length) {
                        setSelectedTrashIds(new Set());
                      } else {
                        setSelectedTrashIds(new Set(trashItems.map((t) => t.id)));
                      }
                    }}
                    className="text-[#00e5ff] hover:underline font-semibold cursor-pointer"
                  >
                    {selectedTrashIds.size === trashItems.length ? 'Deseleccionar todo' : 'Seleccionar todo'}
                  </button>
                </div>

                {trashItems.map((item) => {
                  const daysPassed = item.deletedAt ? Math.floor((Date.now() - item.deletedAt) / (1000 * 60 * 60 * 24)) : 0;
                  const daysRemaining = Math.max(0, 30 - daysPassed);
                  const isSelected = selectedTrashIds.has(item.id);

                  return (
                    <div
                      key={item.id}
                      className={`p-3 rounded-2xl border transition-all flex items-center justify-between gap-3 ${
                        isSelected
                          ? 'bg-slate-800/70 border-[#00e5ff]/60 shadow-md shadow-[#00e5ff]/5'
                          : 'bg-[#0a121d] border-slate-800/90 hover:border-slate-700'
                      }`}
                    >
                      <div
                        className="flex items-center gap-3 min-w-0 flex-1 cursor-pointer select-none"
                        onClick={() => {
                          setSelectedTrashIds((prev) => {
                            const next = new Set(prev);
                            if (next.has(item.id)) next.delete(item.id);
                            else next.add(item.id);
                            return next;
                          });
                        }}
                      >
                        <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-xl bg-slate-900 border border-slate-800 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0">
                          {item.category === 'photo' ? (
                            <FileText className="w-5 h-5 text-amber-400" />
                          ) : item.category === 'video' ? (
                            <FileText className="w-5 h-5 text-sky-400" />
                          ) : item.category === 'audio' ? (
                            <FileText className="w-5 h-5 text-purple-400" />
                          ) : (
                            <FileText className="w-5 h-5 text-emerald-400" />
                          )}
                        </div>

                        <div className="min-w-0 flex-1">
                          <h4 className="text-xs font-semibold text-white truncate">{item.name}</h4>
                          <div className="flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2 mt-0.5 text-[11px] text-slate-400">
                            <span>{formatFileSize(item.size)}</span>
                            <span>•</span>
                            <span className={`font-semibold ${daysRemaining <= 5 ? 'text-rose-400' : 'text-amber-400'}`}>
                              Auto-vaciado en {daysRemaining} {daysRemaining === 1 ? 'día' : 'días'}
                            </span>
                          </div>
                        </div>
                      </div>

                      <div className="flex items-center gap-1.5 shrink-0">
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          title="Restaurar a la bóveda"
                          onClick={() => handleRestoreItem(item.id)}
                          className="p-2 rounded-xl bg-emerald-500/10 hover:bg-emerald-500/25 text-emerald-400 hover:text-emerald-300 border border-emerald-500/20 active:scale-95 transition-all cursor-pointer"
                        >
                          <Undo2 className="w-4 h-4" />
                        </button>
                        <button
                          type="button"
                          title="Eliminar definitivamente ahora"
                          onClick={() => setItemToDeleteDefinitively(item)}
                          className="p-2 rounded-xl bg-rose-500/10 hover:bg-rose-500/25 text-rose-400 hover:text-rose-300 border border-rose-500/20 active:scale-95 transition-all cursor-pointer"
                        >
                          <Trash2 className="w-4 h-4" />
                        </button>
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  );
                })}
              </div>
            )}
          </div>
        )}

        {/* Submenú: Temas y Apariencia Visual 🎨 */}
        {activeSubMenu === 'theme' && (
          <div className="space-y-4 animate-in fade-in duration-200">
            {/* Encabezado */}
            <div className="bg-[#0a121d] p-4 rounded-3xl border border-slate-800/90 shadow-xl space-y-2">
              <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                <div className="w-10 h-10 rounded-2xl bg-purple-500/10 border border-purple-500/25 flex items-center justify-center text-purple-400 shrink-0">
                  <Palette className="w-5 h-5" />
                </div>
                <div>
                  <h3 className="text-sm font-bold text-white">Temas de la Bóveda</h3>
                  <p className="text-xs text-slate-400 mt-0.5">
                    Personaliza la estética visual o maximiza el ahorro de batería en pantallas OLED.
                  </p>
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            {/* Listado de temas */}
            <div className="space-y-2.5">
              {Object.values(VAULT_THEMES).map((themeDef) => {
                const isSelected = currentTheme === themeDef.id;

                return (
                  <div
                    key={themeDef.id}
                    onClick={() => handleSelectTheme(themeDef.id)}
                    className={`p-3.5 sm:p-4 rounded-2xl border transition-all cursor-pointer flex items-center justify-between gap-3 active:scale-[0.99] ${
                      isSelected
                        ? 'bg-[#0a121d] border-2 shadow-lg'
                        : 'bg-[#0a121d] border-slate-800/90 hover:border-slate-700'
                    }`}
                    style={{
                      borderColor: isSelected ? themeDef.primaryColor : undefined,
                      boxShadow: isSelected ? `0 0 16px ${themeDef.glowColor}` : undefined,
                    }}
                  >
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-3 min-w-0">
                      {/* Paleta de círculos cromáticos de vista previa */}
                      <div className="flex items-center -space-x-2 shrink-0">
                        <span
                          className="w-6 h-6 rounded-full border border-slate-900 shadow-md shrink-0"
                          style={{ backgroundColor: themeDef.primaryColor }}
                        />
                        <span
                          className="w-6 h-6 rounded-full border border-slate-900 shadow-md shrink-0"
                          style={{ backgroundColor: themeDef.bgCard }}
                        />
                        <span
                          className="w-6 h-6 rounded-full border border-slate-900 shadow-md shrink-0"
                          style={{ backgroundColor: themeDef.bgMain }}
                        />
                      </div>

                      <div className="min-w-0">
                        <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                          <h4 className="text-sm font-bold text-white truncate">{themeDef.name}</h4>
                          {themeDef.id === 'amoled' && (
                            <span className="text-[9px] font-bold px-1.5 py-0.2 rounded-full bg-slate-800 text-sky-400 border border-slate-700">
                              OLED Puro
                            </span>
                          )}
                          {themeDef.id === 'cyan' && (
                            <span className="text-[9px] font-bold px-1.5 py-0.2 rounded-full bg-cyan-950/70 text-cyan-300 border border-cyan-800/70">
                              Original
                            </span>
                          )}
                          {themeDef.id === 'emerald' && (
                            <span className="text-[9px] font-bold px-1.5 py-0.2 rounded-full bg-emerald-950/70 text-emerald-300 border border-emerald-800/70">
                              Seguro
                            </span>
                          )}
                          {themeDef.id === 'violet' && (
                            <span className="text-[9px] font-bold px-1.5 py-0.2 rounded-full bg-purple-950/70 text-purple-300 border border-purple-800/70">
                              Cripto
                            </span>
                          )}
                          {themeDef.id === 'light' && (
                            <span className="text-[9px] font-bold px-1.5 py-0.2 rounded-full bg-slate-200 text-slate-800 border border-slate-300">
                              Claro
                            </span>
                          )}
                        </div>
                        <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 truncate mt-0.5">{themeDef.tagline}</p>
                      </div>
                    </div>

                    {/* Radio/Check button indicator */}
                    <div
                      className={`w-6 h-6 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 transition-all ${
                        isSelected
                          ? 'text-black font-bold shadow-sm'
                          : 'border border-slate-700 bg-slate-800/60 text-transparent'
                      }`}
                      style={{
                        backgroundColor: isSelected ? themeDef.primaryColor : undefined,
                      }}
                    >
                      {isSelected && <Check className="w-3.5 h-3.5 stroke-[3]" />}
                    </div>
                  </div>
                );
              })}
            </div>
          </div>
        )}
      </div>
      )}

      {/* Modal: Confirmación de Vaciar Papelera Definitivamente */}
      {showEmptyTrashModal && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[60] bg-black/85 backdrop-blur-md flex items-center justify-center p-4">
          <div className="w-full max-w-sm bg-[#0e1626] border-2 border-rose-500/60 rounded-3xl p-6 shadow-2xl relative animate-in zoom-in-95 duration-200">
            <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-2xl bg-rose-500/15 text-rose-400 flex items-center justify-center mb-3">
              <Trash2 className="w-6 h-6" />
            </div>
            <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-white mb-1.5">
              ¿Vaciar la papelera definitivamente?
            </h3>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-300 mb-5 leading-relaxed">
              Esta acción eliminará de forma irreversible <strong>{trashStats.count} elemento(s)</strong> ({formatFileSize(trashStats.totalBytes)}) de tu dispositivo. No podrás recuperarlos.
            </p>
            <div className="flex gap-2.5">
              <button
                type="button"
                disabled={isEmptyingTrash}
                onClick={() => setShowEmptyTrashModal(false)}
                className="flex-1 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-300 font-semibold text-xs transition-colors cursor-pointer"
              >
                Cancelar
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                disabled={isEmptyingTrash}
                onClick={handleConfirmEmptyTrash}
                className="flex-1 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-rose-600 hover:bg-rose-500 text-white font-bold text-xs transition-all shadow-lg shadow-rose-600/30 active:scale-[0.98] cursor-pointer"
              >
                {isEmptyingTrash ? 'Vaciando...' : 'Sí, Vaciar Todo'}
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Modal: Confirmación de Eliminar Un Solo Elemento Definitivamente */}
      {itemToDeleteDefinitively && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[60] bg-black/85 backdrop-blur-md flex items-center justify-center p-4">
          <div className="w-full max-w-sm bg-[#0e1626] border-2 border-rose-500/60 rounded-3xl p-6 shadow-2xl relative animate-in zoom-in-95 duration-200">
            <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-2xl bg-rose-500/15 text-rose-400 flex items-center justify-center mb-3">
              <Trash2 className="w-6 h-6" />
            </div>
            <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-white mb-1.5">
              ¿Eliminar definitivamente este archivo?
            </h3>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-300 mb-5 leading-relaxed">
              El archivo <strong className="text-white truncate block my-1">"{itemToDeleteDefinitively.name}"</strong> ({formatFileSize(itemToDeleteDefinitively.size)}) será destruido permanentemente de tu dispositivo.
            </p>
            <div className="flex gap-2.5">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setItemToDeleteDefinitively(null)}
                className="flex-1 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-300 font-semibold text-xs transition-colors cursor-pointer"
              >
                Cancelar
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handleConfirmSingleDeleteDefinitive}
                className="flex-1 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-rose-600 hover:bg-rose-500 text-white font-bold text-xs transition-all shadow-lg shadow-rose-600/30 active:scale-[0.98] cursor-pointer"
              >
                Eliminar Definitivamente
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Modal 1: Confirmación Inicial (OK) para Borrar Datos */}
      {showWipeConfirmModal && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[60] bg-black/85 backdrop-blur-md flex items-center justify-center p-4">
          <div className="w-full max-w-sm bg-[#0e1626] border-2 border-rose-500/60 rounded-3xl p-6 shadow-2xl relative animate-in zoom-in-95 duration-200">
            <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-2xl bg-rose-500/15 text-rose-400 flex items-center justify-center mb-3">
              <ShieldAlert className="w-6 h-6" />
            </div>
            <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-white mb-1.5">
              ¿Borrar todos los datos de la bóveda?
            </h3>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-300 mb-5 leading-relaxed">
              Esta acción es <strong>definitiva e irreversible</strong>. Se destruirán permanentemente todas las fotos, videos, documentos, registros y configuraciones guardados en este dispositivo.
            </p>
            <div className="flex gap-2.5">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => setShowWipeConfirmModal(false)}
                className="flex-1 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-300 font-semibold text-xs transition-colors"
              >
                Cancelar
              </button>
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={handleConfirmWipeRequest}
                className="flex-1 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-rose-600 hover:bg-rose-500 text-white font-bold text-xs transition-all shadow-lg shadow-rose-600/30 active:scale-[0.98]"
              >
                Confirmar (OK)
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Modal 2: Autenticación por PIN para Borrar Datos */}
      {showWipePinModal && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[60] bg-black/85 backdrop-blur-md flex items-center justify-center p-4">
          <div className="w-full max-w-sm bg-[#0e1626] border-2 border-rose-500/60 rounded-3xl p-6 shadow-2xl relative animate-in zoom-in-95 duration-200">
            <button
              onClick={() => {
                setShowWipePinModal(false);
                setWipePinInput('');
                setWipePinError(null);
              }}
              className="absolute top-4 right-4 p-2 rounded-full bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-400 hover:text-white"
            >
              <X className="w-4 h-4" />
            </button>
            <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-2xl bg-rose-500/15 text-rose-400 flex items-center justify-center mb-3">
              <KeyRound className="w-6 h-6" />
            </div>
            <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-white mb-1">
              Autenticación Requerida
            </h3>
            <p className="text-xs text-slate-300 mb-4 leading-relaxed">
              Introduce tu <strong>PIN maestro de usuario</strong> para autorizar la destrucción total de los datos.
            </p>
            <form onSubmit={handleWipePinSubmit} className="space-y-4">
              <input
                type="password"
                inputMode="numeric"
                pattern="[0-9]*"
                value={wipePinInput}
                onChange={(e) => {
                  setWipePinInput(e.target.value);
                  setWipePinError(null);
                }}
                placeholder="••••"
                className="w-full bg-[#162238] border border-slate-700 focus:border-rose-500 rounded-xl px-4 py-2.5 text-center text-xl font-bold text-white tracking-widest focus:outline-none font-mono"
                autoFocus
              />
              {wipePinError && (
                <div className="p-2.5 bg-rose-500/10 border border-rose-500/20 rounded-xl text-xs text-rose-400 text-center font-medium">
                  {wipePinError}
                </div>
              )}
              <div className="flex gap-2.5">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => {
                    setShowWipePinModal(false);
                    setWipePinInput('');
                    setWipePinError(null);
                  }}
                  className="flex-1 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-300 font-semibold text-xs transition-colors"
                >
                  Cancelar
                </button>
                <button
                  type="submit"
                  disabled={wipePinInput.length < 4 || isVerifyingWipePin}
                  className="flex-1 py-2.5 rounded-xl bg-rose-600 hover:bg-rose-500 disabled:opacity-50 text-white font-bold text-xs transition-all shadow-lg shadow-rose-600/30"
                >
                  {isVerifyingWipePin ? 'Verificando...' : 'Autorizar y Borrar'}
                </button>
              </div>
            </form>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Modal 3: Barra de Progreso de Borrado hasta 100% */}
      {isWipingData && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[70] bg-black/95 backdrop-blur-xl flex items-center justify-center p-4">
          <div className="w-full max-w-sm bg-[#0a121d] border-2 border-rose-500/80 rounded-3xl p-6 shadow-2xl space-y-4 text-center animate-in zoom-in-95 duration-200">
            <div className="w-14 h-14 rounded-3xl bg-rose-500/20 border-2 border-rose-500/50 text-rose-400 flex items-center justify-center mx-auto shadow-lg shadow-rose-500/20 animate-pulse">
              <Trash2 className="w-7 h-7 stroke-[2.5]" />
            </div>

            <div className="space-y-1">
              <h3 className="text-base font-bold text-white">
                Borrando Datos de la Bóveda...
              </h3>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-400">
                {wipeStepText}
              </p>
            </div>

            {/* Barra de Progreso */}
            <div className="space-y-2 pt-2">
              <div className="flex justify-between items-center text-xs font-mono">
                <span className="text-slate-400">Progreso de borrado:</span>
                <span className="text-rose-400 font-bold text-sm">{wipeProgress}%</span>
              </div>
              <div className="w-full h-3.5 bg-slate-800 rounded-full overflow-hidden border border-slate-700/80 p-0.5">
                <div
                  className="h-full bg-gradient-to-r from-rose-600 via-pink-500 to-rose-400 rounded-full transition-all duration-300 ease-out shadow-md shadow-rose-500/40"
                  style={{ width: `${wipeProgress}%` }}
                />
              </div>
            </div>

            <p className="text-[10px] text-slate-500 font-medium">
              Por favor espera, no cierres la aplicación hasta completar el 100%.
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Backup PIN Verification Dialog */}
      {showBackupPinPrompt && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[60] bg-black/90 backdrop-blur-lg flex items-center justify-center p-4">
          <div className="w-full max-w-sm bg-[#0e1626] border border-slate-800 rounded-3xl shadow-2xl overflow-hidden p-6 space-y-4 animate-in zoom-in-95 duration-200">
            <div className="flex flex-col items-center text-center space-y-2">
              <div className="w-12 h-12 rounded-2xl bg-emerald-500/15 text-emerald-400 flex items-center justify-center">
                <Shield className="w-6 h-6" />
              </div>
              <h4 className="text-sm font-bold text-white">Desbloquear Copia de Seguridad</h4>
              <p className="text-[11px] text-slate-400 max-w-xs">
                Esta copia fue creada con un PIN diferente al de tu sesión actual. Ingresa el PIN original para descifrarla.
              </p>
            </div>

            <form onSubmit={handleConfirmBackupPin} className="space-y-4">
              <div className="space-y-1.5">
                <label className="text-[10px] uppercase tracking-wider text-slate-400 font-bold">
                  PIN de la Copia de Seguridad
                </label>
                <input
                  type="password"
                  inputMode="numeric"
                  pattern="[0-9]*"
                  value={backupPinInput}
                  onChange={(e) => setBackupPinInput(e.target.value.replace(/\D/g, '').slice(0, 8))}
                  placeholder="••••"
                  className="w-full bg-[#162238] border border-slate-800 focus:border-emerald-500 rounded-xl px-4 py-2.5 text-center text-lg font-bold text-white tracking-widest focus:outline-none focus:ring-1 focus:ring-emerald-500 font-mono"
                  disabled={isVerifyingBackupPin}
                  autoFocus
                />
              </div>

              {backupPinError && (
                <p className="text-[11px] text-rose-400 font-medium text-center bg-rose-500/10 py-2 px-3 rounded-xl border border-rose-500/20">
                  {backupPinError}
                </p>
              )}

              <div className="flex gap-3">
                <button
                  type="button"
                  onClick={() => {
                    setShowBackupPinPrompt(false);
                    setPendingBackupBlob(null);
                    setPendingBackupHeader(null);
                  }}
                  className="flex-1 py-2 rounded-xl bg-slate-800 hover:bg-slate-700 text-slate-300 text-xs font-semibold transition-colors"
                  disabled={isVerifyingBackupPin}
                >
                  Cancelar
                </button>
                <button
                  type="submit"
                  disabled={backupPinInput.length < 4 || isVerifyingBackupPin}
                  className="flex-1 py-2 rounded-xl bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-500 text-white text-xs font-semibold transition-colors flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5"
                >
                  <span>{isVerifyingBackupPin ? 'Verificando...' : 'Restaurar'}</span>
                </button>
              </div>
            </form>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
      {/* Modal de Reinicio Requerido tras Restaurar Copia */}
      {restartPromptInfo.show && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-[70] bg-black/90 backdrop-blur-xl flex items-center justify-center p-4 animate-in fade-in">
          <div className="w-full max-w-sm bg-[#0a121d] border-2 border-emerald-500/60 rounded-3xl p-6 shadow-2xl space-y-5 text-center animate-in zoom-in-95 duration-200">
            <div className="w-16 h-16 rounded-3xl bg-emerald-500/20 border-2 border-emerald-500/50 text-emerald-400 flex items-center justify-center mx-auto shadow-xl shadow-emerald-500/20">
              <CheckCircle2 className="w-8 h-8" />
            </div>

            <div className="space-y-2">
              <h3 className="text-lg font-black text-white tracking-tight">
                ¡Copia de Seguridad Restaurada!
              </h3>
              <p className="text-xs text-emerald-300 font-semibold bg-emerald-500/10 py-1.5 px-3 rounded-full inline-block border border-emerald-500/30">
                Se restauraron {restartPromptInfo.importedCount} archivos con éxito
              </p>
              <p className="text-xs text-slate-300 pt-1 leading-relaxed">
                Para aplicar todos los cambios de forma segura, sincronizar las claves criptográficas y recargar todas tus fotos y videos en la bóveda, <strong className="text-white">es necesario reiniciar la aplicación</strong>.
              </p>
            </div>

            <div className="space-y-2.5 pt-1">
              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => {
                  window.location.reload();
                }}
                className="w-full py-3.5 bg-gradient-to-r from-emerald-500 via-teal-500 to-cyan-500 hover:from-emerald-400 hover:to-cyan-400 text-slate-950 font-black rounded-2xl text-sm transition-all shadow-xl shadow-emerald-500/25 flex items-center justify-center gap-2 cursor-pointer active:scale-98"
              >
                <span>🔄 Reiniciar Aplicación Ahora</span>
              </button>

              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={async () => {
                  setRestartPromptInfo({ show: false, importedCount: 0 });
                  await onPhotosUpdated();
                }}
                className="w-full py-2.5 text-slate-400 hover:text-white text-xs font-semibold rounded-xl hover:bg-slate-800/50 transition-colors cursor-pointer"
              >
                Cerrar y continuar sin reiniciar
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}

      {/* Modal de Novedades y Actualizaciones */}
      <UpdateNotificationModal
        isOpen={showChangelogModal}
        onClose={() => setShowChangelogModal(false)}
        updateInfo={updateCheckResult}
        viewNotesOnly={!updateCheckResult?.hasUpdate}
      />
    </div>
  );
};

import React, { useState, useEffect, useRef, useMemo } from 'react';

  ShieldAlert,
  Maximize2,
} from 'lucide-react';
import {

export interface Carpeta {
  id: string;
  nombre: string;
}

export interface ArchivoCifrado {
  id: string;
  nombre: string;

interface MainVaultViewProps {
  masterKey: CryptoKey;
  sessionPin?: string | null;
  onLock: () => void;
  onOpenSettings: () => void;
  showToast: (msg: string, type?: 'info' | 'success' | 'alert' | 'error') => void;
  refreshTrigger?: number;
  autoLockMinutes?: number;
  onAutoLockChange?: (mins: number) => void;
  lockOnTabSwitch?: boolean;
  onLockOnTabSwitchChange?: (enabled: boolean) => void;
  onMasterKeyUpdated?: (newKey: CryptoKey) => void;
  onResetVault?: () => void;
  hasUpdatePending?: boolean;
}

export const MainVaultView: React.FC<MainVaultViewProps> = ({
  masterKey,
  sessionPin,
  onLock,
  onOpenSettings,
  showToast,
  refreshTrigger = 0,
  autoLockMinutes = 5,
  onAutoLockChange,
  lockOnTabSwitch = true,
  onLockOnTabSwitchChange,
  onMasterKeyUpdated,
  onResetVault,
  hasUpdatePending = false,
}) => {
  const [liveUpdateAvailable, setLiveUpdateAvailable] = useState<boolean>(() => {
    return updateService.getLastCheckResult()?.hasUpdate || false;
  });

  useEffect(() => {
    return updateService.subscribe((res) => {
      setLiveUpdateAvailable(res.hasUpdate);
    });
  }, []);

  const [items, setItems] = useState<DecryptedVaultItem[]>([]);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState<boolean>(true);
  const [searchQuery, setSearchQuery] = useState<string>('');
  const [activeTab, setActiveTab] = useState<'all' | 'photo' | 'video' | 'audio' | 'app' | 'favorites'>('all');
  const [activeNavTab, setActiveNavTab] = useState<'vault' | 'import' | 'security' | 'settings'>('vault');
  const [activeCategory, setActiveCategory] = useState<'photo' | 'video' | 'audio' | 'app' | null>(null);
  const [lastAccessTime] = useState<string>(() => {
    return localStorage.getItem('nullvault_last_access') || '21:14';
  });
  const [gridColumns, setGridColumns] = useState<2 | 3 | 4>(3);
  const [aspectMode, setAspectMode] = useState<'real' | 'square'>(() => {
    return (localStorage.getItem('vault_aspect_mode') as 'real' | 'square') || 'real';
  });

  const toggleAspectMode = () => {
    const next = aspectMode === 'real' ? 'square' : 'real';
    setAspectMode(next);
    localStorage.setItem('vault_aspect_mode', next);
    showToast(next === 'real' ? 'Visualización: Tamaño y proporción real (sin recorte)' : 'Visualización: Cuadrícula cuadrada 1:1', 'info');
  };

  // Recordatorio de copia de seguridad (> 7 días)
  const [backupReminder, setBackupReminder] = useState<{
    show: boolean;
    days: number;
    hasNeverBackedUp: boolean;
  }>(() => {
    const lastBackupTime = Number(
      localStorage.getItem('nullvault_last_backup_timestamp') ||
      localStorage.getItem('vault_last_backup_timestamp') ||
      '0'
    );
    const dismissedUntil = Number(
      localStorage.getItem('nullvault_backup_reminder_dismissed_until') ||
      localStorage.getItem('vault_backup_reminder_dismissed_until') ||
      '0'
    );
    if (Date.now() < dismissedUntil) {
      return { show: false, days: 0, hasNeverBackedUp: false };
    }
    const ONE_WEEK_MS = 7 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
    if (lastBackupTime > 0) {
      const diffMs = Date.now() - lastBackupTime;
      if (diffMs > ONE_WEEK_MS) {
        const days = Math.floor(diffMs / (24 * 60 * 60 * 1000));
        return { show: true, days, hasNeverBackedUp: false };
      }
    } else {
      return { show: true, days: 7, hasNeverBackedUp: true };
    }
    return { show: false, days: 0, hasNeverBackedUp: false };
  });

  useEffect(() => {
    const handleBackupUpdated = () => {
      setBackupReminder({ show: false, days: 0, hasNeverBackedUp: false });
    };
    window.addEventListener('vault_backup_updated', handleBackupUpdated);
    window.addEventListener('nullvault_backup_updated', handleBackupUpdated);
    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener('vault_backup_updated', handleBackupUpdated);
      window.removeEventListener('nullvault_backup_updated', handleBackupUpdated);
    };
  }, []);

  const dismissBackupReminder = () => {
    const nextDismiss = (Date.now() + 3 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000).toString();
    localStorage.setItem('vault_backup_reminder_dismissed_until', nextDismiss);
    localStorage.setItem('nullvault_backup_reminder_dismissed_until', nextDismiss);
    setBackupReminder({ show: false, days: 0, hasNeverBackedUp: false });
  };

  // Selection mode
  const [isSelectMode, setIsSelectMode] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [selectedIds, setSelectedIds] = useState<Set<string>>(new Set());
  const [showBatchExportModal, setShowBatchExportModal] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [isExportingBatch, setIsExportingBatch] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [exportProgress, setExportProgress] = useState<number>(0);
  const [exportCurrentIndex, setExportCurrentIndex] = useState<number>(0);
  const [exportTotalCount, setExportTotalCount] = useState<number>(0);
  const [exportCurrentFileName, setExportCurrentFileName] = useState<string>('');
  const [isExportComplete, setIsExportComplete] = useState<boolean>(false);

  // Subfolders / Custom independent folders per category
  const [customFolders, setCustomFolders] = useState<{
    photo: string[];
    video: string[];
    audio: string[];
    app: string[];
  }>(() => {
    const load = (cat: 'photo' | 'video' | 'audio' | 'app') => {
      try {
        const saved = localStorage.getItem(`vault_subfolders_${cat}`);
        if (saved) {
          const parsed = JSON.parse(saved);
          if (Array.isArray(parsed) && parsed.length > 0) return parsed;
        }
      } catch (e) {
        console.error(e);
      }
      return ['General'];
    };
    return {
      photo: load('photo'),
      video: load('video'),
      audio: load('audio'),
      app: load('app'),
    };
  });

  // Estado para la carpeta activa ('todas' por defecto para mostrar el menú de carpetas)
  const [carpetaActiva, setCarpetaActiva] = useState<string>('todas');
  const [isSearchOpen, setIsSearchOpen] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const activeSubfolder = carpetaActiva === 'todas' ? null : carpetaActiva;
  const setActiveSubfolder = (folder: string | null) => {
    setCarpetaActiva(folder === null ? 'todas' : folder);
  };
  const [folderToRename, setFolderToRename] = useState<string | null>(null);
  const [isCreateFolderOpen, setIsCreateFolderOpen] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [isMoveModalOpen, setIsMoveModalOpen] = useState<boolean>(false);

  // Modals
  const [activeViewerItem, setActiveViewerItem] = useState<DecryptedVaultItem | null>(null);
  const [isCameraOpen, setIsCameraOpen] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [cameraInitialMode, setCameraInitialMode] = useState<'photo' | 'video'>('photo');
  const [isWhatsAppFabOpen, setIsWhatsAppFabOpen] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [isDraggingOver, setIsDraggingOver] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [isAddSheetOpen, setIsAddSheetOpen] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [importedCountForReminder, setImportedCountForReminder] = useState<number>(0);
  const [recentImportedItems, setRecentImportedItems] = useState<{ name: string; size: number }[]>([]);
  const [showBatchDeleteModal, setShowBatchDeleteModal] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const [isDeletingBatch, setIsDeletingBatch] = useState<boolean>(false);
  const longPressTimeoutRef = useRef<NodeJS.Timeout | null>(null);
  const isLongPressTriggered = useRef<boolean>(false);
  const [encryptingProgress, setEncryptingProgress] = useState<{
    current: number;
    total: number;
    name: string;
  } | null>(null);

  // Hidden file inputs
  const photoInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement | null>(null);
  const videoInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement | null>(null);
  const mediaInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement | null>(null);
  const audioInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement | null>(null);
  const appInputRef = useRef<HTMLInputElement | null>(null);
  const blobUrlsRef = useRef<string[]>([]);

  // Trigger file picker safely preventing premature auto-lock
  const triggerPicker = (inputRef: React.RefObject<HTMLInputElement | null>) => {
    (window as any).__isPickingFile = true;
    (window as any).__filePickingStartTime = Date.now();
    inputRef.current?.click();
  };

  // Load and decrypt items into memory
  const loadVaultItems = async () => {
    setIsLoading(true);
    try {
      const records = await getAllEncryptedItems();
      records.sort((a, b) => b.timestamp - a.timestamp);

      // Clean up previous blob URLs to prevent memory leaks
      blobUrlsRef.current.forEach((url) => URL.revokeObjectURL(url));
      blobUrlsRef.current = [];

      const decryptedItems: DecryptedVaultItem[] = [];

      // Lazy candidate key derivation in case items were saved under a previous PIN or alternate key
      let candidateKeys: CryptoKey[] | null = null;
      const getCandidateKeys = async (): Promise<CryptoKey[]> => {
        if (candidateKeys !== null) return candidateKeys;
        const saltHex = localStorage.getItem('vault_salt');
        if (!saltHex) {
          candidateKeys = [];
          return candidateKeys;
        }
        try {
          const salt = hexToBuf(saltHex);
          const rawPins = [
            sessionPin,
            localStorage.getItem('vault_duress_pin'),
            '0000', '1234', '1111', '123456', '000000', '2580', '1122', '4321',
            '9999', '8888', '7777', '6666', '5555', '4444', '3333', '2222',
            '1212', '1313', '1414', '1515', '2020', '2024', '2025', '2026', '6969',
          ];
          const uniquePins = Array.from(new Set(rawPins.filter((p): p is string => Boolean(p) && typeof p === 'string')));
          const derived: CryptoKey[] = [];
          for (const p of uniquePins) {
            try {
              const k = await deriveKeyFromPin(p, salt);
              derived.push(k);
            } catch {}
          }
          candidateKeys = derived;
        } catch {
          candidateKeys = [];
        }
        return candidateKeys;
      };

      for (let i = 0; i < records.length; i++) {
        const rec = records[i];
        try {
          let decryptedData: ArrayBuffer | null = null;
          const isProbablyUnencrypted = !rec.iv || rec.iv.length === 0 || checkIsUnencryptedMedia(rec.data);

          if (isProbablyUnencrypted) {
            decryptedData = rec.data;
          } else {
            // Attempt 1: primary masterKey
            try {
              decryptedData = await decryptBuffer(rec.data, rec.iv, masterKey);
            } catch {
              // Primary key failed
            }

            // Attempt 2: test if raw bytes are unencrypted media with extended file signatures
            if (!decryptedData && checkIsUnencryptedMedia(rec.data)) {
              decryptedData = rec.data;
              // Re-encrypt in background so future reads are encrypted
              encryptBuffer(decryptedData, masterKey).then((reEnc) => {
                saveEncryptedItem({ ...rec, iv: reEnc.iv, data: reEnc.data }).catch(() => {});
              }).catch(() => {});
            }

            // Attempt 3: test candidate fallback keys
            if (!decryptedData) {
              const altKeys = await getCandidateKeys();
              for (const altKey of altKeys) {
                try {
                  const altDec = await decryptBuffer(rec.data, rec.iv, altKey);
                  if (altDec) {
                    decryptedData = altDec;
                    // Successfully salvaged! Re-encrypt with masterKey and update in IndexedDB
                    encryptBuffer(decryptedData, masterKey).then((reEnc) => {
                      saveEncryptedItem({ ...rec, iv: reEnc.iv, data: reEnc.data }).catch(() => {});
                    }).catch(() => {});
                    break;
                  }
                } catch {
                  // candidate key did not match
                }
              }
            }
          }

          if (decryptedData) {
            const blob = new Blob([decryptedData], { type: rec.mimeType || 'application/octet-stream' });
            const blobUrl = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
            blobUrlsRef.current.push(blobUrl);

            decryptedItems.push({
              id: rec.id,
              blobUrl,
              record: rec,
            });
          } else {
            // Unrecoverable corrupted record from an old wiped key.
            // Using console.warn instead of console.error to avoid false unhandled error triggers.
            console.warn(`[Vault] Item ${rec.id} cannot be decrypted with current credentials. Purging corrupt record.`);
            deleteEncryptedItem(rec.id).catch(() => {});
          }
        } catch (itemErr) {
          console.warn(`[Vault] Skipped item ${rec.id}:`, itemErr);
          deleteEncryptedItem(rec.id).catch(() => {});
        }

        if (i > 0 && i % 10 === 0) {
          await new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, 0));
        }
      }

      setItems(decryptedItems);
    } catch (err) {
      console.warn('Error loading vault items:', err);
      showToast('Error al leer datos de la bóveda', 'error');
    } finally {
      setIsLoading(false);
    }
  };

  useEffect(() => {
    loadVaultItems();

    // Reload folders in case a backup brought new custom folders
    const loadFolder = (cat: 'photo' | 'video' | 'audio' | 'app') => {
      try {
        const saved = localStorage.getItem(`vault_subfolders_${cat}`);
        if (saved) {
          const parsed = JSON.parse(saved);
          if (Array.isArray(parsed) && parsed.length > 0) return parsed;
        }
      } catch (e) {
        console.error(e);
      }
      return ['General'];
    };
    setCustomFolders({
      photo: loadFolder('photo'),
      video: loadFolder('video'),
      audio: loadFolder('audio'),
      app: loadFolder('app'),
    });

    const handleTrashUpdated = () => {
      loadVaultItems();
    };
    window.addEventListener('vault_trash_updated', handleTrashUpdated);

    return () => {
      window.removeEventListener('vault_trash_updated', handleTrashUpdated);
      // Memory cleanup when component unmounts / locks
      blobUrlsRef.current.forEach((url) => URL.revokeObjectURL(url));
      blobUrlsRef.current = [];
    };
  }, [masterKey, refreshTrigger]);

  // Android Back Button / Gesture Navigation Support
  const activeViewerItemRef = useRef(activeViewerItem);
  activeViewerItemRef.current = activeViewerItem;
  const wasViewerOpenRef = useRef(false);

  const isCameraOpenRef = useRef(isCameraOpen);
  isCameraOpenRef.current = isCameraOpen;
  const wasCameraOpenRef = useRef(false);

  const showBatchDeleteModalRef = useRef(showBatchDeleteModal);
  showBatchDeleteModalRef.current = showBatchDeleteModal;
  const wasDeleteModalOpenRef = useRef(false);

  const importedCountRef = useRef(importedCountForReminder);
  importedCountRef.current = importedCountForReminder;
  const wasReminderOpenRef = useRef(false);

  const isSelectModeRef = useRef(isSelectMode);
  isSelectModeRef.current = isSelectMode;
  const selectedIdsRef = useRef(selectedIds);
  selectedIdsRef.current = selectedIds;
  const wasSelectingRef = useRef(false);

  const isCreateFolderOpenRef = useRef(isCreateFolderOpen);
  isCreateFolderOpenRef.current = isCreateFolderOpen;
  const wasCreateFolderOpenRef = useRef(false);

  const isMoveModalOpenRef = useRef(isMoveModalOpen);
  isMoveModalOpenRef.current = isMoveModalOpen;
  const wasMoveModalOpenRef = useRef(false);

  // Sync modal refs
  useEffect(() => {
    wasCreateFolderOpenRef.current = isCreateFolderOpen;
  }, [isCreateFolderOpen]);

  useEffect(() => {
    wasMoveModalOpenRef.current = isMoveModalOpen;
  }, [isMoveModalOpen]);

  // Reset activeSubfolder when changing activeTab
  useEffect(() => {
    setActiveSubfolder(null);
  }, [activeTab]);

  const activeSubfolderRef = useRef(activeSubfolder);
  activeSubfolderRef.current = activeSubfolder;
  const wasSubfolderOpenRef = useRef(false);

  const folderToRenameRef = useRef(folderToRename);
  folderToRenameRef.current = folderToRename;

  // Sync open subfolder ref
  useEffect(() => {
    wasSubfolderOpenRef.current = activeSubfolder !== null;
  }, [activeSubfolder]);

  // Dynamic list of all folders for active category
  const activeCategoryFolders = useMemo(() => {
    const targetCat =
      activeTab === 'favorites' || activeTab === 'all'
        ? activeCategory || 'photo'
        : activeTab;
    const list = customFolders[targetCat] || ['General'];
    const set = new Set<string>(list);
    items.forEach((it) => {
      if (
        (activeTab === 'favorites' ? it.record.isFavorite : it.record.category === targetCat) &&
        it.record.album
      ) {
        set.add(it.record.album);
      }
    });
    return Array.from(set);
  }, [activeTab, activeCategory, customFolders, items]);

  // Lista tipada de carpetas disponibles según la interfaz Carpeta
  const listaCarpetas: Carpeta[] = useMemo(() => {
    return activeCategoryFolders.map((fName) => ({
      id: fName,
      nombre: fName,
    }));
  }, [activeCategoryFolders]);

  // Robust Native Android Back Button & Gesture Navigation Stack
  useEffect(() => {
    if (activeViewerItem) {
      return registerBackHandler('main_viewer_item', () => {
        setActiveViewerItem(null);
        return true;
      }, 95);
    }
  }, [activeViewerItem]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (isCameraOpen) {
      return registerBackHandler('main_camera_open', () => {
        setIsCameraOpen(false);
        return true;
      }, 90);
    }
  }, [isCameraOpen]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (isAddSheetOpen) {
      return registerBackHandler('main_add_sheet', () => {
        setIsAddSheetOpen(false);
        return true;
      }, 85);
    }
  }, [isAddSheetOpen]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (isCreateFolderOpen) {
      return registerBackHandler('main_create_folder', () => {
        setIsCreateFolderOpen(false);
        return true;
      }, 80);
    }
  }, [isCreateFolderOpen]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (isMoveModalOpen) {
      return registerBackHandler('main_move_modal', () => {
        setIsMoveModalOpen(false);
        return true;
      }, 80);
    }
  }, [isMoveModalOpen]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (folderToRename) {
      return registerBackHandler('main_rename_folder', () => {
        setFolderToRename(null);
        return true;
      }, 80);
    }
  }, [folderToRename]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (showBatchDeleteModal) {
      return registerBackHandler('main_batch_delete', () => {
        setShowBatchDeleteModal(false);
        return true;
      }, 80);
    }
  }, [showBatchDeleteModal]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (isWhatsAppFabOpen) {
      return registerBackHandler('main_whatsapp_fab', () => {
        setIsWhatsAppFabOpen(false);
        return true;
      }, 90);
    }
  }, [isWhatsAppFabOpen]);

  useEffect(() => {
    setIsWhatsAppFabOpen(false);
  }, [activeCategory, activeSubfolder, activeNavTab]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (importedCountForReminder > 0) {
      return registerBackHandler('main_import_reminder', () => {
        setImportedCountForReminder(0);
        return true;
      }, 80);
    }
  }, [importedCountForReminder]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (isSelectMode) {
      return registerBackHandler('main_select_mode', () => {
        setIsSelectMode(false);
        setSelectedIds(new Set());
        return true;
      }, 50);
    }
  }, [isSelectMode]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (activeSubfolder !== null) {
      return registerBackHandler('main_subfolder_nav', () => {
        setActiveSubfolder(null);
        return true;
      }, 40);
    }
  }, [activeSubfolder]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (activeCategory !== null) {
      return registerBackHandler('main_category_nav', () => {
        setActiveCategory(null);
        setActiveSubfolder(null);
        setIsSelectMode(false);
        setSelectedIds(new Set());
        return true;
      }, 30);
    }
  }, [activeCategory]);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (activeNavTab !== 'vault') {
      return registerBackHandler('main_tab_nav', () => {
        setActiveNavTab('vault');
        setActiveCategory(null);
        return true;
      }, 20);
    }
  }, [activeNavTab]);

  const handleCreateFolder = (name: string) => {
    const cat = (activeCategory || (activeTab !== 'all' && activeTab !== 'favorites' ? activeTab : 'photo')) as VaultItemCategory;
    const currentList = customFolders[cat] || ['General'];
    if (!currentList.includes(name)) {
      const updated = [...currentList, name];
      setCustomFolders((prev) => ({ ...prev, [cat]: updated }));
      localStorage.setItem(`vault_subfolders_${cat}`, JSON.stringify(updated));
      showToast(`Carpeta "${name}" creada con éxito`, 'success');
      setActiveSubfolder(name);
      if (activeCategory === null) {
        openCategory(cat);
      }
    }
  };

  const handleRenameFolder = async (oldName: string, newName: string) => {
    if (activeTab === 'all' || activeTab === 'favorites') return;
    if (oldName === 'General') return;
    const cat = activeTab;

    await batchRenameAlbum(cat, oldName, newName);

    // Update in memory items
    setItems((prev) =>
      prev.map((it) => {
        const itemCat = it.record.category;
        const itemAlbum = it.record.album || 'General';
        if (itemCat === cat && itemAlbum === oldName) {
          return { ...it, record: { ...it.record, album: newName } };
        }
        return it;
      })
    );

    // Update custom folders
    const currentList = customFolders[cat] || ['General'];
    const updated = currentList.map((f) => (f === oldName ? newName : f));
    setCustomFolders((prev) => ({ ...prev, [cat]: updated }));
    localStorage.setItem(`vault_subfolders_${cat}`, JSON.stringify(updated));

    if (activeSubfolder === oldName) {
      setActiveSubfolder(newName);
    }

    setFolderToRename(null);
    showToast(`Carpeta renombrada a "${newName}"`, 'success');
  };

  const handleDeleteFolder = async (folderNameToDelete: string) => {
    if (folderNameToDelete === 'General' || activeTab === 'all' || activeTab === 'favorites') return;
    const cat = activeTab;

    // Move any items in this folder back to 'General' in DB and memory
    const itemsInFolder = items.filter(
      (it) => it.record.category === cat && it.record.album === folderNameToDelete
    );

    if (itemsInFolder.length > 0) {
      const ids = itemsInFolder.map((it) => it.id);
      await batchUpdateAlbum(ids, 'General');
      setItems((prev) =>
        prev.map((it) =>
          ids.includes(it.id)
            ? { ...it, record: { ...it.record, album: 'General' } }
            : it
        )
      );
    }

    const currentList = customFolders[cat] || ['General'];
    const updated = currentList.filter((f) => f !== folderNameToDelete);
    setCustomFolders((prev) => ({ ...prev, [cat]: updated.length > 0 ? updated : ['General'] }));
    localStorage.setItem(`vault_subfolders_${cat}`, JSON.stringify(updated.length > 0 ? updated : ['General']));

    if (activeSubfolder === folderNameToDelete) {
      setActiveSubfolder(null);
    }
    showToast(`Carpeta "${folderNameToDelete}" eliminada (sus archivos se pasaron a General)`, 'info');
  };

  const handleMoveSelected = async (targetFolder: string) => {
    if (selectedIds.size === 0) return;
    const ids = Array.from(selectedIds);
    await batchUpdateAlbum(ids, targetFolder);

    // Update in memory state
    setItems((prev) =>
      prev.map((it) =>
        ids.includes(it.id)
          ? { ...it, record: { ...it.record, album: targetFolder } }
          : it
      )
    );

    showToast(`${ids.length} elemento(s) movidos a "${targetFolder}"`, 'success');
    setSelectedIds(new Set());
    setIsSelectMode(false);
    setIsMoveModalOpen(false);
  };

  // Navigation tab and category state references
  const activeNavTabRef = useRef(activeNavTab);
  activeNavTabRef.current = activeNavTab;
  const activeCategoryRef = useRef(activeCategory);
  activeCategoryRef.current = activeCategory;

  const navigateToTab = (tab: 'vault' | 'import' | 'security' | 'settings') => {
    if (tab !== activeNavTab) {
      window.history.pushState({ vaultNav: tab }, '');
      setActiveNavTab(tab);
    }
  };

  // Listener para redirigir directamente al menú de actualización cuando se toca el banner
  useEffect(() => {
    const handleOpenUpdatesMenu = () => {
      setActiveCategory(null);
      setIsSelectMode(false);
      setSelectedIds(new Set());
      navigateToTab('settings');
      sessionStorage.setItem('open_updates_tab_on_settings', 'true');
      // Enviar evento al sub-menú para abrir la sección de Actualizaciones de inmediato
      setTimeout(() => {
        window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('settings_switch_submenu', { detail: { submenu: 'updates' } }));
      }, 50);
    };

    window.addEventListener('vault_open_updates_menu', handleOpenUpdatesMenu);
    return () => window.removeEventListener('vault_open_updates_menu', handleOpenUpdatesMenu);
  }, []);

  const openCategory = (cat: 'photo' | 'video' | 'audio' | 'app') => {
    window.history.pushState({ vaultCat: cat }, '');
    setActiveCategory(cat);
    setActiveTab(cat);
  };

  // Swipe Gesture Handling - retroceso en pantalla únicamente dentro de vistas hijas
  const touchStartX = useRef<number | null>(null);
  const touchStartY = useRef<number | null>(null);

  const handleTouchStart = (e: React.TouchEvent) => {
    touchStartX.current = e.touches[0].clientX;
    touchStartY.current = e.touches[0].clientY;
  };

  const handleTouchEnd = (e: React.TouchEvent) => {
    if (touchStartX.current === null || touchStartY.current === null) return;
    const deltaX = e.changedTouches[0].clientX - touchStartX.current;
    const deltaY = e.changedTouches[0].clientY - touchStartY.current;
    touchStartX.current = null;
    touchStartY.current = null;

    // Deslizar de izquierda a derecha (swipe horizontal para atrás: deltaX > 65 y dominante)
    if (deltaX > 65 && Math.abs(deltaX) > Math.abs(deltaY) * 1.4) {
      const target = e.target as HTMLElement | null;
      if (target && (target.tagName === 'INPUT' || target.tagName === 'SELECT' || target.tagName === 'TEXTAREA')) {
        return;
      }
      triggerBackAction();
    }
  };

  // Total storage calculated from real user files
  const totalStorageBytes = useMemo(() => {
    return items.reduce((acc, it) => acc + (it.record.size || 0), 0);
  }, [items]);

  const storagePercent = useMemo(() => {
    if (items.length === 0) return 0;
    // Percentage calculated on a standard 500MB secure quota partition
    const baseQuota = 500 * 1024 * 1024;
    return Math.min(100, Math.max(1, Math.round((totalStorageBytes / baseQuota) * 100)));
  }, [items.length, totalStorageBytes]);

  // Recent files list matching real user saved files - exactly last 5 records
  const recentList = useMemo(() => {
    return items.slice(0, 5).map((it) => {
      const d = new Date(it.record.timestamp);
      const timeStr = `${String(d.getHours()).padStart(2, '0')}:${String(d.getMinutes()).padStart(2, '0')}`;
      const nameWithoutExt = it.record.name.replace(/\.[^/.]+$/, '');
